### [1] [An Open-Source Flow for Single-Phase, Edge-Triggered to Two-Phase, Non-Overlapping Clocking Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05374)
*Paolo Pedroso,Lee-Way Wang,Matthew Guthaus*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个集成于OpenROAD流程的全自动两相时钟设计流程，实现了从RTL到GDS的自动化转换，相比触发器基准设计，门控时钟方案平均降低功耗29.2%、锁存器数量减少50%，并通过时间借用成功解决了触发器设计时序收敛失败的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两相时钟在时序裕量和时钟灵活性方面具有显著优势，但严格非重叠和180度相位分离的要求导致RTL实现和时序收敛复杂度高，缺乏现代设计流程的自动化支持，使其在实际应用中罕见。

Method: 提出首个集成于OpenROAD Flow Scripts (ORFS)的全自动两相时钟设计流程，包括：基于Yosys技术映射将触发器RTL自动转换为两相锁存器设计、ABC重定时、双时钟树综合、两相正确性验证、以及从RTL到GDS的完整物理设计；实现了门控时钟和循环多路复用器两种变体。

Result: 门控时钟变体相比循环多路复用器变体平均功耗降低29.2%、锁存器数量减少50%；两种变体与触发器基准对比，在一个触发器设计时序收敛失败的案例中，通过时间借用机制成功实现了时序收敛。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了两相时钟设计的完全自动化，填补了现代设计流程的空白，证明了门控时钟变体的优越性，并展示了时间借用机制在解决时序收敛问题上的有效性，为两相时钟在实际设计中的应用提供了可行的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Two-phase clocking offers significant advantages in timing margin and clock flexibility, yet its adoption remains limited due to the absence of automation in modern design flows. Managing strict non-overlap and 180$^\circ$ phase separation introduces complexity in RTL implementation and timing closure, leaving two-phase clocking rare in practice. This paper presents the first fully automated two-phase clocking flow integrated into OpenROAD Flow Scripts (ORFS). Our methodology automatically transforms flip-flop-based RTL into two-phase latch-based designs using Yosys technology mapping, ABC retiming, dual clock tree synthesis, two-phase correctness validation, and full physical design from RTL-to-GDS. We implement clock-gated and recirculation mux variants, where clock-gated achieves an average 29.2\% power reduction and 50\% latch count reduction over recirculation mux. Both variants are compared against flip-flop baselines, demonstrating timing closure through time borrowing on a design that failed timing with flip-flops.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond Static Policies: Exploring Dynamic Policy Selection for Single-Thread Performance Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05471)
*Yanxin Zhang,Ian McDougall,Junnan Li,Shayne Wadle,Vikas Singh,Karthikeyan Sankaralingam*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过实验分析发现，处理器在不同工作负载或执行阶段采用不同的策略组合能显著提升性能。研究显示，最佳静态策略仅在19.18%的执行阶段达到最优，平均IPC损失为1.54%。而能够动态选择策略的处理器可将平均IPC损失降低13.6倍（至0.11%），并在52.65%的情况下达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统处理器设计采用单一的静态策略选择，但随着工作负载日益复杂多样，研究提出一个根本问题：不同的工作负载或同一工作负载的不同执行阶段是否受益于不同的策略组合？这促使研究者探索动态策略选择是否能够突破传统静态策略处理器的性能瓶颈。

Method: 研究使用基于ChampSim的模拟器，在49个基准测试程序中分段生成490个执行阶段（每个阶段2000万条指令），针对缓存替换和预取机制评估多种策略组合的性能表现。通过对比静态策略处理器与假设能够动态选择策略的处理器，量化性能差异。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 最佳静态策略仅在19.18%的执行阶段达到最优性能，相比理想方案平均IPC损失为1.54%；(2) 85个执行阶段（17.35%）的IPC损失超过2.5%；(3) 能够在两个精心选择的策略间动态切换的处理器可将平均IPC损失从1.54%降至0.11%（降低13.6倍），并在52.65%的情况下匹配理想性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明动态策略选择具有巨大的性能提升潜力，能够显著减少单线程性能损失。这为未来处理器设计提供了新方向，即通过动态策略选择机制来解锁日益难以获得的单线程性能提升，代表了处理器架构设计的一个有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: For over a decade, processor design has focused on implementing sophisticated policies for various components of the out-of-order pipeline, including cache replacement and prefetching. The prevailing design philosophy has been to build processors with a single, static selection of policies across these different mechanisms. This paper investigates a fundamental question: do different workloads, or even different execution phases within the same workload, benefit from different policy combinations? We present a comprehensive analysis exploring whether a hypothetical processor capable of dynamically selecting from multiple policies could significantly outperform traditional static-policy processors. Using ChampSim-based simulation across 49 benchmarks segmented into 490 execution phases of 20M instructions each, we evaluate performance across multiple policy combinations for cache replacement and prefetching. Our findings reveal that significant performance headroom exists: the best static policy achieves optimal performance for only 19.18\% of execution phases and incurs a mean IPC loss of 1.54\% compared to an oracle. Moreover, 85 phases (17.35\%), spanning 14 of the 49 applications, exhibit more than 2.5\% IPC loss relative to the oracle. Furthermore, we demonstrate that a processor capable of dynamically switching between two carefully chosen policies can achieve a 13.6$\times$ reduction in mean IPC loss (from 1.54\% to 0.11\%) and match oracle performance 52.65\% of the time. These results suggest that dynamic policy selection represents a promising avenue for unlocking single-thread performance improvements that have become increasingly difficult to achieve.

</details>


### [3] [DICE: Enabling Efficient General-Purpose SIMT Execution with Statically Scheduled Coarse-Grained Reconfigurable Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05496)
*Jiayi Wang,Ang Da Lu,Zhichen Zeng,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出DICE架构，用粗粒度可重构阵列（CGRA）替代GPU的SIMD后端，通过空间流水线执行大幅降低寄存器访问和功耗，在保持相当性能的同时实现了近2倍的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU虽然在并行计算领域占据主导地位，但其底层的SIMD执行模式存在显著的能耗开销，主要来源于频繁的寄存器堆访问和复杂的控制逻辑，亟需一种更高效的架构来解决这些能效瓶颈。

Method: 提出DICE架构，用静态调度的粗粒度可重构阵列（CGRA）替代传统SIMD后端。核心方法包括：（1）将活跃线程以流水线方式分发到CGRA fabric上，使数据在处理单元间直接流动以减少中间值的RF访问；（2）引入p-graph编译技术，通过将动态依赖边划分到不同CGRA配置来处理运行时动态性；（3）采用双缓冲配置存储隐藏重配置延迟；（4）编译时p-graph展开提升资源利用率；（5）设计时间存储合并单元（TMCU）合并连续流水线线程的存储请求。

Result: 在Rodinia基准测试上，DICE平均减少68%的寄存器堆访问；在相同计算和存储资源下，CGRA处理器相比NVIDIA Turing流多处理器实现1.77-1.90倍动态能效提升（几何平均）和42.0%-45.9%的平均功耗降低；完整DICE系统达到与Turing GPU基准相当的性能。

Conclusion: DICE证明了空间流水线执行可以在不牺牲性能的前提下实现显著的能耗节省，为GPU架构设计提供了新的方向，表明用CGRA替代传统SIMD后端是一种可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: While GPUs dominate massively parallel computing through the single-instruction, multiple-thread (SIMT) programming model, their underlying single-instruction, multiple-data (SIMD) execution incurs substantial energy overhead from frequent register file (RF) accesses and complex control logic. We present DICE, a novel architecture that addresses these inefficiencies by replacing the SIMD backend with minimal-overhead, statically scheduled coarse-grained reconfigurable arrays (CGRAs). Unlike SIMD units that execute warps of threads in lockstep, DICE dispatches active threads in a pipelined manner onto the CGRA fabric, where data flow directly between processing elements (PEs), reducing RF accesses for intermediate values. To handle operations with runtime dynamism, such as variable-latency memory loads and data-dependent control flow, while preserving static scheduling, DICE compiles programs into "p-graphs" by partitioning dynamic dependence edges across separate CGRA configurations. DICE further introduces several key optimizations: double-buffered configuration memory to hide reconfiguration latency, compile-time p-graph unrolling to enhance resource utilization, and a temporal memory coalescing unit (TMCU) to merge memory requests from consecutive, pipelined threads. Evaluations on Rodinia benchmarks in Accel-sim demonstrate that DICE reduces register file accesses by 68% on average. With equivalent computation and memory resources, DICE's CGRA Processors (CPs) achieve a geometric mean of 1.77-1.90x dynamic energy efficiency and 42.0%-45.9% average power reduction compared to the modeled NVIDIA Turing Streaming Multiprocessors (SMs), while the full DICE system achieves performance comparable to the modeled Turing GPU baselines. DICE demonstrates that spatial pipeline execution can deliver substantial energy savings without sacrificing performance.

</details>


### [4] [Accelerating MoE with Dynamic In-Switch Computing on Multi-GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05607)
*Qijun Zhang,Chen Zhang,Zhuoshan Zhou,Haibo Wang,Zhe Zhou,Zhipeng Tu,Guangyu Sun,Zhiyao Xie,Yijia Diao,Zhigang Ji,Jingwen Leng,Guanghui He,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出DySHARP，一种动态交换机内计算方案，通过动态多播寻址和令牌中心内核融合技术加速MoE专家并行，相比现有方案最高实现1.79倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE在大模型中被广泛采用以降低计算需求，但专家并行中的频繁GPU间通信成为性能瓶颈。观察到MoE中存在大量冗余的GPU间数据传输，现有交换机内计算方案NVLS仅支持静态规则集合通信，无法处理MoE中动态不规则通信模式。

Method: 1) 动态多播寻址：通过协同设计指令集、架构和运行时，作为NVLS的动态扩展，减少冗余流量；2) 令牌中心内核融合：深度融合分发-计算-合并流水线，解决流量减少在两个方向上的不对称性，将流量减少转化为实际加速。

Result: 相比最先进方案，DySHARP最高实现1.79倍加速。

Conclusion: DySHARP成功填补了现有交换机内计算方案在动态不规则通信模式方面的功能空白，为加速MoE专家并行提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has been adopted by many leading large models to reduce computational requirements. However, frequent inter-GPU communication in MoE expert parallelism (EP) becomes a performance challenge. We observe substantial redundant inter-GPU data transfers in MoE that can be potentially addressed by in-switch computing. Unfortunately, the existing solution, NVLink SHARP (NVLS), can only support static collectives with regular patterns, incapable of dynamic communication with irregular patterns in MoE. To bridge the functionality gap, we propose DySHARP, an integral dynamic in-switch computing solution to accelerate MoE, encompassing both communication primitives and communication-aware scheduling: 1) Dynamic multimem addressing co-designs ISA, architecture, and runtime, as a dynamic extension to NVLS, reducing redundant traffic. However, the resulting traffic reduction is inherently asymmetric between two directions, preventing it from directly translating into speedup. 2) Token-centric kernel fusion deeply fuses the dispatch-computation-combine pipeline, resolving this asymmetry to translate traffic reduction into actual speedup. Compared with the state-of-the-art solution, DySHARP achieves up to 1.79$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [5] [Towards Compute-Aware In-Switch Computing for LLMs Tensor-Parallelism on Multi-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05628)
*Chen Zhang,Qijun Zhang,Zhuoshan Zhou,Yijia Diao,Haibo Wang,Zhe Zhou,Zhipeng Tu,Zhiyao Li,Guangyu Sun,Zhuoran Song,Zhigang Ji,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出CAIS框架，首个计算感知的交换机内计算框架，通过匹配通信模式与内存语义需求，在LLM训练中实现平均1.38倍（对比NVLS方案）和1.61倍（对比T3方案）的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交换机内计算技术（如NVLink SHARP）以通信为中心的设计理念与LLM计算核的内存语义需求不匹配，导致计算与通信阶段隔离、资源利用率低且重叠有限，无法充分发挥多GPU系统的性能潜力。

Method: 提出CAIS框架，包含三项核心技术：（1）计算感知的指令集与微架构扩展，支持计算感知的交换机内计算；（2）合并感知的线程块协调机制，提升时序对齐以实现高效请求合并；（3）图级数据流优化器，实现紧密的跨核重叠。

Result: 在LLM工作负载上的评估表明，CAIS相比现有NVLS方案实现平均1.38倍的端到端训练加速，相比不使用NVLS的最先进计算-通信重叠方案T3实现1.61倍加速。

Conclusion: CAIS通过将通信模式与计算内存语义需求对齐，有效解决了交换机内计算在张量并行场景下的局限性，显著提升了多GPU系统上LLM训练的性能。

Abstract: Tensor parallelism (TP) in large-scale LLM inference and training introduces frequent collective operations that dominate inter-GPU communication. While in-switch computing, exemplified by NVLink SHARP (NVLS), accelerates collective operations by reducing redundant data transfer, its communication-centric design philosophy introduces the mismatch between its communication mode and the memory semantic requirement of LLM's computation kernel. Such a mismatch isolates the compute and communication phases, resulting in underutilized resources and limited overlap in multi-GPU systems. To address the limitation, we propose CAIS, the first Compute-Aware In-Switch computing framework that aligns communication modes with computation's memory semantics requirement. CAIS consists of three integral techniques: (1) compute-aware ISA and microarchitecture extension to enable compute-aware in-switch computing. (2) merging-aware TB (Thread Block) coordination to improve the temporal alignment for efficient request merging. (3) graph-level dataflow optimizer to achieve a tight cross-kernel overlap. Evaluations on LLM workloads show that CAIS achieves 1.38$\times$ average end-to-end training speedup over the SOTA NVLS-enabled solution, and 1.61$\times$ over T3, the SOTA compute-communicate overlap solutions but do not leverage NVLS, demonstrating its effectiveness in accelerating TP on multi-GPU systems.

</details>


### [6] [TokenStack: A Heterogeneous HBM-PIM Architecture and Runtime for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05639)
*Zhuoran Li,Zhuohang Bian,Zihao Huang,Guangyu Sun,Yun Liang,Youwei Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出TokenStack，一种垂直异构HBM-PIM架构，通过分离容量层和计算层并利用HBM4逻辑基板进行智能KV缓存管理，显著提升LLM服务吞吐量和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HBM-PIM架构存在资源浪费问题：统一HBM-PIM堆栈使所有层承担PIM逻辑开销，而专用PIM设计（如AttAcc）虽恢复容量但缩减了GPU可用带宽。实际上，仅热KV块受益于近存计算，权重、激活和冷KV主要需要密集存储和GPU可见带宽。

Method: 利用HBM4逻辑裸片基底构建垂直异构HBM-PIM架构，将堆栈分离为密集容量层和PIM计算层；基板控制器处理跨层DMA、分层地址转换、注意力侧聚集/广播协调及迁移时内联量化；实现拓扑感知KV放置、负载感知驱逐和有界复制策略，保持热KV靠近PIM计算。

Result: 基于四种模型的生产追踪测试表明，相比AttAcc，TokenStack将几何平均token吞吐量提升1.62倍，SLO合规服务容量提升1.70倍，每个token能耗降低30-47%。

Conclusion: TokenStack通过垂直异构设计和智能KV管理策略，有效解决了LLM服务中KV缓存的带宽和容量瓶颈问题，在吞吐量、服务容量和能效方面均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving is now limited by the key-value (KV) cache. During decode, each new token rereads prior KV state, so attention becomes a bandwidth- and capacity-heavy memory task. HBM-PIM helps by moving attention closer to memory, but current stack organizations still waste resources. In practice, only hot KV blocks benefit from near-memory compute. Weights, activations, and cold KV mainly need dense storage and GPU-visible bandwidth. A uniform HBM-PIM stack makes all layers pay for PIM logic, while a dedicated-PIM design such as AttAcc recovers capacity but shrinks the HBM bandwidth left for GPU-side work. We propose TokenStack, a vertically heterogeneous HBM-PIM architecture for KV-centric LLM serving that leverages HBM4's logic-die substrate. TokenStack separates each stack into dense capacity layers and PIM-enabled compute layers, then uses the logic base die as a stack-local control point that manages cross-layer movement without host-side overhead. The base-die controller handles cross-layer DMA, layered address translation, attention-side gather/broadcast coordination, and inline quantization during migration. On top of this hardware, TokenStack uses topology-aware KV placement, workload-aware eviction, and bounded replication to keep hot KV near PIM compute while moving colder state to dense layers. Using production-derived traces across four models, completed multi-QPS runs show that TokenStack increases geometric-mean token throughput by 1.62x and SLO-compliant serving capacity by 1.70x over AttAcc, and reduces per-token energy by 30-47%.

</details>


### [7] [MoE-Hub: Taming Software Complexity for Seamless MoE Overlap with Hardware-Accelerated Communication on Multi-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05888)
*Zhuoshan Zhou,Chen Zhang,Shuyi Zhang,Qijun Zhang,Haibo Wang,Zhe Zhou,Zhipeng Tu,Guangyu Sun,Yijia Diao,Zhigang Ji,Jingwen Leng,Guanghui He,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出MoE-Hub软硬件协同设计，通过解耦数据传输与地址管理，解决MoE架构中动态token映射与GPU静态通信模型的不匹配问题，实现高效通信与计算重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)架构在扩展大型语言模型时面临多GPU系统中严重的跨GPU通信瓶颈。尽管通信与计算重叠被广泛认为是一种优化手段，但由于MoE动态、不规则的token-to-expert映射与现代GPU静态、以地址为中心的通信模型存在根本性的抽象不匹配，导致需要复杂的软件中介阶段来解析地址，限制了性能和软件灵活性。

Method: 提出MoE-Hub软硬件协同设计方案，引入一种与目的地无关（destination-agnostic）的通信范式。该方法将数据传输与地址管理解耦，允许生产者仅使用逻辑目的地址在路由后立即发送数据，而地址分配和数据流编排则由GPU hub中的轻量级硬件透明处理。通过硬件加速整个通信控制平面，实现无缝透明的通信计算重叠。

Result: 实验评估表明，与最先进的系统相比，MoE-Hub实现了1.40x-3.08x的单层加速比和1.21x-1.98x的端到端加速比。

Conclusion: MoE-Hub通过软硬件协同设计，成功解决了MoE模型中通信抽象不匹配的核心问题，实现了高效的通信与计算重叠，显著提升了大规模语言模型的扩展性能。

Abstract: The Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is crucial for scaling large language models, but its scalability is severely limited by inter-GPU communication bottlenecks in multi-GPU systems. Although overlapping communication with computation is a widely recognized optimization, its effective deployment still remains challenging, both in terms of performance and programmability. In this work, we identify the root cause as a fundamental abstraction mismatch between MoE's dynamic, irregular token-to-expert mapping and the static, address-centric communication model of modern GPUs, which necessitates a complex software mediation phase to resolve addresses before data transfers, limiting performance and software flexibility. To resolve this, we propose MoE-Hub, a hardware-software co-design that introduces a destination-agnostic communication paradigm. MoE-Hub decouples data transmission from address management, allowing producers to send data immediately after routing using only a logical destination, while address allocation and data-flow orchestration are handled transparently by lightweight hardware in the GPU hub. By hardware-accelerating the entire communication control plane, MoE-Hub enables seamless and transparent overlap. Our evaluation shows that MoE-Hub achieves 1.40x-3.08x per-layer and 1.21x-1.98x end-to-end speedup over state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [8] [LLM-Driven Design Space Exploration of FPGA-based Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05920)
*Vinamra Sharma,Xingjian Fu,Jude Haris,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了SECDA-DSE框架，将大语言模型（LLM）集成到SECDA生态系统中，用于自动化FPGA加速器的设计空间探索（DSE）。通过结合检索增强生成和思维链推理，该框架能够自动生成满足时序和资源约束的加速器配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为现代AI工作负载设计基于FPGA的加速器需要探索庞大且复杂的硬件设计空间（包括架构参数、数据流策略和存储层次结构），该过程耗时且资源密集。虽然SECDA方法论支持快速软硬件协同设计，但确定最优加速器配置仍需大量人工干预和领域专业知识。

Method: 构建了SECDA-DSE框架，包含一个用于生成加速器配置的结构化DSE探索器，以及一个利用检索增强生成（RAG）和思维链（CoT）提示进行推理引导探索的LLM技术栈；同时引入反馈循环机制以实现强化微调和持续改进。

Result: 通过基于高层次综合（HLS）的初步评估，验证了SECDA-DSE的可行性。生成的加速器设计在Zynq-7000 FPGA上成功满足综合时序和资源约束。

Conclusion: SECDA-DSE框架成功实现了FPGA加速器设计空间探索的自动化，减少了对手动调优和领域专业知识的依赖，为加速器设计提供了高效的新途径。

Abstract: Designing field-programmable gate array (FPGA)-based accelerators for modern artificial intelligence workloads requires navigating a large and complex hardware design space encompassing architectural parameters, dataflow strategies, and memory hierarchies, making the process time-consuming and resource-intensive. While the SECDA methodology enables rapid hardware-software co-design of accelerators through SystemC simulation and FPGA execution, identifying optimal accelerator configurations still requires substantial manual effort and domain expertise. This work presents SECDA-DSE, a framework that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) into the SECDA ecosystem, comprising tools built around SECDA to automate the design space exploration (DSE) of FPGA-based accelerators. SECDA-DSE combines a structured DSE Explorer for generating accelerator configurations with an LLM Stack that performs reasoning-guided exploration using retrieval-augmented generation and chain-of-thought prompting, alongside a feedback loop that enables reinforced fine-tuning for continuous improvement. We demonstrate the feasibility of SECDA-DSE through an initial high-level synthesis based evaluation of a generated accelerator design that meets synthesis timing and resource constraints on an Zynq-7000 FPGA.

</details>


### [9] [A virtually connected probabilistic computer as a solver for higher-order, densely connected, or reconfigurable combinatorial optimisation problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06037)
*Amy J. Searle,Harry Youel,Fredrik Hasselgren,Annika Möslein,Ramy Aboushelbaya,Marko von der Leyen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于光子量子随机数发生器的虚拟连接概率计算架构，避免了传统硬件所需的嵌入、稀疏化和二次化等拓扑变换，在解决特定NP-hard问题上比数字退火单元快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非常规计算方法在解决NP-hard问题时，由于硬件拓扑限制，需要通过嵌入、稀疏化和二次化等技术对问题几何结构进行变换，导致解的质量下降。为了克服这一限制，研究者希望找到一种能够绕过这些变换过程的计算架构。

Method: 采用基于高速光子量子随机数发生器的概率计算架构，利用虚拟硬件连接来避免拓扑硬件限制。同时使用贪婪图着色算法实现硬件并行化，以获得良好的解质量扩展性。通过模拟仿真来模拟硬件性能。

Result: 模拟结果表明，在Erdos-Renyi图自旋玻璃基态近似问题上，光子概率计算机比近期报道的数字退火单元在求解时间上快了数个数量级。同时证明了使用二次化或稀疏化会导致问题规模显著增加，凸显了虚拟连接硬件的优势。

Conclusion: 虚拟连接硬件架构能够有效避免物理连接硬件的拓扑限制，无需进行问题几何变换，在解决特定几何结构的NP-hard问题上具有显著的性能优势，为非常规计算提供了一条新的有效途径。

Abstract: Recently, there has been growing interest in unconventional computing as an approach for solving NP-hard problems, by developing dedicated hardware to find solutions more efficiently than conventional CPUs. In many of these approaches, however, certain problem geometries must be transformed into forms that are more amenable to the available hardware topology through techniques such as embedding, sparsification, and quadratisation, leading to a deterioration in solution quality. A probabilistic computing architecture based on high speed photonic quantum random number generators was recently proposed which utilises virtual hardware connections (Aboushelbaya et al., 2025), circumventing the necessity for such procedures. Here, we discuss the applicability of virtually connected hardware for running heuristic solving methods to solve a selection of problems, which due to their geometry, would suffer from topological hardware restrictions. We also employ greedy graph colouring algorithms for hardware parallelisation, allowing favourable scaling for desirable solution qualities. To emphasise the difficulty in solving these problems on physically connected hardware, we demonstrate the increase in problem size that would occur with quadratisation or sparsification. Using simulations to emulate hardware, we predict that a photonic probabilistic computer would outperform the time to solution recently reported for digital annealing units, on the ground state approximation of Erdos-Renyi graph spin-glasses, by orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [10] [XtraMAC: An Efficient MAC Architecture for Mixed-Precision LLM Inference on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06052)
*Feng Yu,Hongshi Tan,Yao Chen,Weng-Fai Wong,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了XtraMAC，一种面向大语言模型混合精度量化需求的新型FPGA MAC架构，通过统一整数、浮点和混合精度运算，在单一微架构中实现了显著的资源效率、计算密度和能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型广泛采用混合精度量化，需要硬件高效支持跨混合数据类型的乘累加（MAC）运算以及运行时数据类型切换。现有基于FPGA的MAC解决方案存在固定数据类型设计、低效的空间或时间资源共享以及对混合精度执行支持差等局限性，导致DSP资源利用率不足，限制了并行度和吞吐量。

Method: 提出XtraMAC架构，将所有支持的MAC格式分解为共享的整数尾数乘积，配合轻量级的符号和指数处理。该设计实现了动态操作数打包、高效的DSP资源共享，并在所有数据类型上保持恒定延迟和一个周期的发起间隔（II=1）。

Result: 在AMD Xilinx U55c FPGA上评估，XtraMAC实现了：计算密度提升1.4-2.0倍；每操作LUT、FF和DSP消耗降低27-51%；能效提升高达1.9倍；在代表性混合精度LLM工作负载上加速1.2倍。

Conclusion: XtraMAC成功解决了现有FPGA MAC方案在混合精度支持方面的不足，通过统一的数据类型自适应微架构显著提升了资源利用率和能效，为大语言模型的高效部署提供了有效解决方案，并已开源。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of mixed-precision quantization in large language models (LLMs) has created demand for hardware that can efficiently perform multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations across mixed datatypes and switch datatypes at runtime. Existing FPGA-based MAC solutions fall short due to limitations in fixed-datatype design, inefficient spatial or temporal resource sharing, and poor support for mixed-precision execution. These limitations collectively lead to under-utilization of DSP resources, limiting achievable parallelism and throughput. In this work, we present XtraMAC, a novel MAC architecture that unifies integer, floating-point, and mixed-precision operations within a single, datatype-adaptive microarchitecture. XtraMAC decomposes all supported MAC formats into a shared integer mantissa product with lightweight sign and exponent handling, enabling dynamic operand packing and efficient DSP resource sharing with constant latency and initiation interval of one across all datatypes. Evaluated on an AMD Xilinx U55c FPGA, XtraMAC achieves 1.4-2.0x higher compute density, reduces per-operation LUT, FF, and DSP consumption by 27-51%, and delivers up to 1.9x greater energy efficiency and 1.2x speedup on representative mixed-precision LLM workloads. The implementation of XtraMAC is open-sourced at https://github.com/Xtra-Computing/XtraMAC.

</details>


### [11] [PoTAcc: A Pipeline for End-to-End Acceleration of Power-of-Two Quantized DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06082)
*Rappy Saha,Jude Haris,Nicolas Bohm Agostini,David Kaeli,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了PoTAcc，一个用于在资源受限边缘设备上加速和评估PoT量化DNN的开源端到端流水线，在FPGA平台上实现了高达3.6倍加速和78%的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PoT量化能显著减少DNN模型大小并用位移操作替代乘法，但现有研究对其在资源受限边缘设备上的性能理解不足。通用边缘CPU/GPU缺乏对位移操作的优化后端支持，虽然定制硬件加速器能更好利用PoT量化，但现有推理框架支持有限，且不同PoT量化策略对硬件设计、性能和能效的影响缺乏系统探索。

Method: 提出PoTAcc开源端到端流水线，通过TensorFlow Lite实现PoT量化模型在异构平台（仅CPU系统和CPU-FPGA混合系统）上的无缝准备和部署。针对三种PoT量化方法设计了基于位移的处理单元(Shift-PE)加速器，并在两个FPGA平台上实现。评估了包括CNN和Transformer架构在内的多种模型的准确性、性能、能效和资源利用率。

Result: 在PYNQ-Z2和Kria开发板上，CPU-加速器设计相比仅CPU执行实现了最高3.6倍加速和78%的能耗降低。代码已在GitHub开源发布。

Conclusion: PoTAcc成功填补了PoT量化DNN在边缘设备部署方面的空白，证明了定制硬件加速器能显著提升PoT量化模型的性能和能效，为PoT量化在资源受限环境中的应用提供了完整的解决方案和系统评估框架。

Abstract: Power-of-two (PoT) quantization significantly reduces the size of deep neural networks (DNNs) and replaces multiplications with bit-shift operations for inference. Prior work has shown that PoT-quantized DNNs can preserve accuracy for tasks such as image classification; however, their performance on resource-constrained edge devices remains insufficiently understood. While general-purpose edge CPUs and GPUs do not provide optimized backends for bit-shift operations, custom hardware accelerators can better exploit PoT quantization by implementing dedicated shift-based processing elements. However, deploying PoT-quantized models on such accelerators is challenging due to limited support in existing inference frameworks. In addition, the impact of different PoT quantization strategies on hardware design, performance, and energy efficiency during full inference has not been systematically explored.
  To address these challenges, we propose PoTAcc, an open-source end-to-end pipeline for accelerating and evaluating PoT-quantized DNNs on resource-constrained edge devices. PoTAcc enables seamless preparation and deployment of PoT-quantized models via TensorFlow Lite (TFLite) across heterogeneous platforms, including CPU-only systems and hybrid CPU-FPGA systems with custom accelerators. We design shift-based processing element (shift-PE) accelerators for three PoT quantization methods and implement them on two FPGA platforms. We evaluate accuracy, performance, energy efficiency, and resource utilization across a range of models, including CNNs and Transformer-based architectures. Results show that our CPU-accelerator design achieves up to 3.6x speedup and 78% energy reduction compared to CPU-only execution for PoT-quantized DNNs on PYNQ-Z2 and Kria boards. The code will be publicly released at https://github.com/gicLAB/PoTAcc

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [12] [Are Flat Minima an Illusion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05209)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“平坦极小值带来更好泛化”的经典观点，指出锐度可通过重参数化人为制造，因此不能作为泛化的原因。作者提出“弱度”（weakness）这一新指标，证明其具有重参数化不变性且能更好地预测泛化性能。实验表明，在大规模数据下大批量训练的泛化优势消失，而弱度在预测泛化上显著优于锐度和简单性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论认为损失景观中的平坦区域有助于泛化，锐度感知最小化（SAM）等方法基于此。然而，作者发现通过函数保持的重参数化可以将任意极小值的Hessian特征值放大两个数量级而不改变任何预测。如果权重空间的几何性质可以人为制造，它就不可能是泛化的根本原因。这促使作者寻找真正的泛化驱动因素。

Method: 作者提出“弱度”概念，定义为在学习者具身语言中与学习函数兼容的补全体积，基于网络的功能而非参数化方式定义，因此具有重参数化不变性。作者证明了弱度在可交换需求下的minimax最优性，并证明PAC-Bayes界之所以有效是因为其与弱度相关。作者在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上进行了大规模实验对比。

Result: 在MNIST上，大批量训练的泛化优势随训练数据增加而消失（从n=2000时的+1.6%降至n=60000时的+0.02%），表明锐度是混淆因素而非原因。在100个相同架构和训练的网络对比中：MNIST上弱度预测泛化（ρ=+0.374, p=0.00012），锐度反相关（ρ=-0.226），简单性无预测力（p=0.848）；Fashion-MNIST上弱度同样有效（ρ=+0.384, p=8.15×10^-5）。简单性依赖数据集，而弱度具有不变性。

Conclusion: 平坦极小值从来不是答案。真正的泛化驱动因素是“弱度”——与学习函数兼容的补全体积，它在重参数化下保持不变且能一致地预测泛化性能。锐度和简单性要么可人为制造、要么依赖数据集，不具备作为泛化原因的必要性质。

Abstract: Neural networks that land in flat regions of the loss landscape tend to generalise better than those in sharp regions. Sharpness-Aware Minimisation exploits this to improve generalisation. But function-preserving reparameterisation can inflate the Hessian of any minimum by two orders of magnitude without changing a single prediction. If the geometry of weight space can be manufactured from nothing, it cannot be the cause of anything. In other words, flat is simple and simplicity depends on encoding. Here I show that the actual driver is weakness, the volume of completions compatible with the learned function in the learner's embodied language. Weakness is reparameterisation-invariant because it is defined over what the network \emph{does}, not how it is parameterised. I prove weakness is minimax-optimal under exchangeable demands, and that PAC-Bayes bounds work because they correlate with it. On MNIST, the large-batch generalisation advantage \emph{vanishes} as training data grows, from $+1.6\%$ at $n = 2{,}000$ to $+0.02\%$ at $n = 60{,}000$. A quantity whose predictive power depends on how much data you have is not a cause but a confounder. I run head-to-heads on 100 networks with identical architecture and training. For MNIST weakness predicts generalisation ($ρ= +0.374$, $p = 0.00012$), sharpness anticorrelates ($ρ= -0.226$) and simplicity predicts nothing ($p = 0.848$). For Fashion-MNIST ($ρ= +0.384$, $p = 8.15 \times 10^{-5}$), though simplicity is at least somewhat predictive there. Simplicity is dataset dependent, whereas weakness is invariant. Flat minima were never the answer.

</details>


### [13] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用全美纵向EHR数据，通过混合特征选择和人口分层建模，实现了慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）的早期预测，AUC达到0.8461，有助于初级保健中的早期分诊和转诊优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）是一种常见的异质性炎症性疾病，症状与过敏性鼻炎等常见疾病重叠，且存在异质性表型，导致早期识别困难。既往研究多依赖单机构队列，限制了人群水平的普适性。

Method: 利用\textit{All of Us}研究计划的全美纵向EHR数据，使用诊断前两年的病史进行预测。采用混合特征选择流程（结合基于患病率的统计筛选和基于模型的重要性排序），将约11万个候选代码压缩为100个可解释特征。针对六个人口统计学亚组（性别和生命阶段）训练分层模型，并进行亚组特定的超参数调优。

Result: 模型总体AUC达到0.8461，相比最佳基线模型提高了0.0168的判别能力。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据可支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，并能指导初级保健中的早期分诊和转诊优先级决策。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [14] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了顺序代理调优(SAT)方法，通过无协调器的训练范式，使多个小型LLM团队的性能能够匹敌或超越单一大型模型，并支持即插即用的代理升级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然性能强大但部署成本过高，而使用多个小型LLM协作的方案在训练时面临复合分布偏移问题，导致协调困难且训练不稳定。

Method: 提出无协调器的顺序代理调优(SAT)范式：将团队表示为因子化策略，采用代理间的块坐标更新；开发了序列感知的在线策略优势估计器和针对每个代理的KL信任区域。

Result: 三个4B代理(共12B)组成的团队在AIME24/25基准上平均超越Qwen3-32B模型3.9%；替换两个8B代理后，综合得分提升10.4%，验证了即插即用理论。

Conclusion: SAT框架在理论上保证单调改进和即插即用不变性，实验证明多个小型LLM可以通过协作达到甚至超越大型模型的性能，同时具有灵活的升级能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [15] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Learnable Loss Balancing and Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05217)
*Reza Pirayeshshirazinezhad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应平衡物理约束与数据驱动的自监督PINN框架，结合迁移学习，在极少数据（87个样本）下实现了液态金属微型散热器传热预测误差<8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对科学机器学习中数据稀缺的挑战，传统PINN方法依赖固定或启发式权重来平衡物理残差与数据损失，导致需要繁琐的手动调参且难以实现最优平衡，因此亟需一种能够自适应调整并保持稳定训练的框架。

Method: 提出了包含可学习混合神经元的自监督PINN框架，该神经元能根据不确定性动态调整物理损失项与数据损失项的相对权重；同时结合迁移学习策略，将相关领域的表征复用并适应到新物理系统中。

Result: 在液态金属微型散热器传热预测任务中，仅使用87个CFD数据点，自适应PINN的预测误差低于8%，性能优于浅层神经网络、核方法及纯物理基准模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为在神经网络中自适应嵌入物理提供了通用范式，为流体动力学、材料建模等科学领域的数据稀缺问题提供了稳健且可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a self-supervised physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework that adaptively balances physics-based and data-driven supervision for scientific machine learning under data scarcity. Unlike prior PINNs that rely on fixed or heuristic weighting of physics residuals and data loss, our approach introduces a learnable blending neuron that dynamically adjusts the relative contribution of each term based on their uncertainties. This mechanism enables stable training and improved generalization without manual tuning. To further enhance efficiency, we integrate a transfer learning strategy that reuses representations from related domains and adapts them to new physical systems with limited data. We validate the framework for the prediction of heat transfer in liquid-metal miniature heat sinks using only 87 CFD datapoints, where the adaptive PINN achieves an error <8%, outperforming shallow neural networks, kernel methods, and physics-only baselines. Our framework provides a general recipe for embedding physics adaptively into neural networks, offering a robust and reproducible approach for data-scarce problems across various scientific domains, including fluid dynamics and material modeling.

</details>


### [16] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的联系，提出了视界约束Rashomon集框架，证明了有效Rashomon集随预测时长呈指数收缩，并开发了基于决策对齐的模型选择算法，在多个任务上提升了18-34%的决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中的预测多重性与混沌动力学是两个独立发展的基础性挑战，尽管它们在概念上存在联系。在混沌系统中，模型多重性随预测时间演变，这与Rashomon集保持不变的静态预测任务不同，因此需要一个新的理论框架来描述这种演变。

Method: 提出了视界约束Rashomon集理论框架，证明其随预测时长以最大Lyapunov指数决定的速率呈指数收缩；引入Lyapunov加权度量提供更紧的预测分歧界限；基于此开发了决策对齐选择算法，根据下游效用而非仅凭预测精度在近优模型中进行选择。

Result: 在合成混沌系统（Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky）和实际应用（风电、交通、天气）的广泛实验中，该框架在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，将决策质量提升了18-34%。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间首个严格的理论联系，为在安全关键的混沌领域部署机器学习提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [17] [Sparse Prefix Caching for Hybrid and Recurrent LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05219)
*Mikhail Shirokikh,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对状态空间模型（SSM）提出了一种稀疏前缀缓存方法，通过在稀疏位置存储检查点来平衡无重用和密集缓存，在保持精确输出的同时显著提升了低预算下的服务延迟优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前缀缓存系统主要针对Transformer架构的密集token级键值重用设计，而状态空间模型（SSM）具有循环结构，可从单个存储状态恢复，无需完整的token历史。这种不对称性揭示了在无重用和密集缓存之间存在一个新的设计空间，亟需针对SSM的特性开发高效的缓存策略。

Method: 形式化稀疏前缀缓存问题为在重叠深度分布下的检查点放置问题，提出了一种精确的O(NM)动态规划算法进行求解。该方法在稀疏的检查点位置存储精确的循环状态，在缓存命中时从最深的存储检查点恢复并精确重计算剩余后缀，适用于可提取和恢复隐状态的循环/SSM层，且可与混合模型中的现有KV-cache压缩技术结合。

Result: 在QuALITY和System Prompts数据集上的实验表明，分布感知的放置策略在测量的层组帕累托前沿上主导了每个固定预算基线，匹配或超越了最强的启发式方法（块缓存），同时通常使用显著更少的检查点。在检查点预算较低且重叠分布最不均匀的情况下，取得了最大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法在许多请求共享大量但不完全相同前缀的场景下最为有效，能够保持精确输出，不改变循环计算本身，也不需要新的循环更新内核，为SSM架构的LLM服务提供了一种高效实用的延迟优化方案。

Abstract: Prefix caching is a key latency optimization for autoregressive LLM serving, yet existing systems assume dense per-token key/value reuse. State-space models change the structure of the problem: a recurrent layer can resume from a single stored state rather than requiring the entire token history. This asymmetry opens a new design point between no reuse and dense caching: store exact recurrent states at a sparse set of checkpoint positions and, on a cache hit, resume from the deepest stored checkpoint and recompute the remaining suffix exactly.
  We formalize sparse prefix caching as checkpoint placement under a distribution over overlap depths, yielding an exact O(NM) dynamic program. For use cases where requests share a non-trivial prefix (e.g. asking different questions about a single long document), we show that our method consistently improves the Pareto frontier traced by standard heuristics on real-world data. Across QuALITY and System Prompts, distribution-aware placement dominates every fixed-budget baseline on the measured layer-group Pareto frontier and matches or outperforms the strongest heuristic (block caching) while typically using substantially fewer checkpoints, with the largest gains at low checkpoint budgets where the overlap distribution is most non-uniform. The method is most relevant when many requests share a substantial but not identical prefix within a retained cache entry. It preserves exact outputs, does not change the recurrent computation itself or require new recurrent update kernels, applies to recurrent/SSM layers whose hidden state can be extracted and restored exactly, and for hybrid models can be combined with existing KV-cache compression techniques.

</details>


### [18] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了概念引导的理论框架，证明了标准方法与LEACE的联系，并提出了两种方法：LEACE-Switch（概念切换）和MidSteer（最小扰动概念引导），后者在各种任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管引导中间表示在控制生成模型方面取得了实证成功，特别是在部署后对齐和安全设置中，但目前缺乏一个全面的理论框架。本文旨在弥合这一差距，将概念引导理论形式化。

Method: 1. 建立引导与仿射概念擦除之间的联系，证明消除不需要行为的标准方法是LEACE（一种仿射擦除的闭式方法）的特例；2. 制定概念切换的原则性理论框架LEACE-Switch，并刻画其提供最优仿射解的假设条件；3. 引入MidSteer（最小扰动概念引导），这是一个更通用的仿射框架，放松了假设条件并实现定向的、最小扰动的变换。

Result: MidSteer在一系列任务、模态和架构中表现良好，包括视觉扩散模型和大型语言模型。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了概念引导的理论框架，将现有方法统一在理论基础上，并提出的新方法MidSteer在保持最小扰动的同时实现了有效的概念操纵，为生成模型的控制提供了理论指导和实践工具。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [19] [Data-Driven Variational Basis Learning Beyond Neural Networks: A Non-Neural Framework for Adaptive Basis Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05221)
*Andrew Kiruluta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了数据驱动变分基学习（DVBL）框架，一种非神经网络的替代方法，通过变分优化直接从数据中学习基函数，在保持显式、可解释和数学透明性的同时实现数据自适应的基展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典表示系统（如傅里叶级数、小波和固定字典）提供了分析上易于处理的基展开，但本质上不适应现代高维数据的经验结构；神经网络虽然能从数据中学习特征，但通过分层非线性参数化牺牲了可解释性、对基结构的显式控制和数学透明性。需要一种既能从数据中学习特征又能保持可解释性和数学严谨性的方法。

Method: 提出数据驱动变分基学习（DVBL）框架，将基原子作为主要优化变量，与样本特定系数以及潜在线性演化算子联合学习。采用交替最小化算法，建立块下降性质，并结合流形和动力学正则化方法，不依赖神经架构。

Result: 建立了最小化子的存在性，证明了交替最小化算法的块下降性质，给出了系数恢复和基可识别性的条件，展示了如何在不引入神经架构的情况下集成流形和动力学正则化。

Conclusion: DVBL框架成功地在保持显式、可解释和可进行严格分析的基础上，实现了数据自适应的基展开，为经典字典学习、谱方法、Koopman算子方法和深度表示学习提供了一种概念上新颖的替代方案。

Abstract: Classical representation systems such as Fourier series, wavelets, and fixed dictionaries provide analytically tractable basis expansions, but they are not intrinsically adapted to the empirical structure of modern high-dimensional data. Neural networks overcome this limitation by learning features from data, yet they do so through layered nonlinear parameterizations that often sacrifice interpretability, explicit control over basis structure, and mathematical transparency. In this manuscript we develop a non-neural alternative that learns basis functions directly from data through variational optimization. The proposed framework, termed Data Driven Variational Basis Learning (DVBL), treats basis atoms as primary optimization variables and learns them jointly with sample-specific coefficients and, when appropriate, a latent linear evolution operator. This yields a data-adaptive basis expansion that remains explicit, interpretable, and amenable to rigorous analysis. We formulate the model, establish existence of minimizers, prove blockwise descent properties for an alternating minimization algorithm, give conditions for coefficient recovery and basis identifiability, and show how manifold and dynamical regularization can be integrated without invoking neural architectures. We also discuss the conceptual novelty of the framework relative to classical dictionary learning, spectral methods, Koopman operator methods, and deep representation learning.

</details>


### [20] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，一种基于学习到的逐token门控机制，能够根据上下文难度选择性跳过Transformer层，在字符级语言建模任务上节省14-23%的计算量，质量损失小于0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer架构对所有token应用相同数量的层，而忽略了不同token的上下文难度差异。这种"一刀切"的方式存在计算冗余，因为并非所有token都需要相同深度的处理。

Method: TSA在连续的Transformer块之间的残差连接上引入了学习的逐token门控机制。每个门控由一个轻量级两层MLP组成，产生连续的停止概率，使机制端到端可微，仅增加1.7%的参数开销，无需改变基础架构。即使没有显式的深度正则化(λ=0)，仅任务损失梯度就能驱动路由器跳过20%的token-layer操作。

Result: 在字符级语言建模任务上(Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8)，TSA节省了14-23%的token-layer操作(TLOps)，质量损失小于0.5%。在相同效率下，TSA的验证损失比早期退出(early exit)方法低0.7%，学习到的路由可直接应用于推理时的稀疏执行，实现真实的wall-clock加速。

Conclusion: TSA成功实现了与难度成正比的路由机制，无需显式的深度压力即可自动学习跳过不必要的计算，在保持性能的同时显著提升了计算效率，并可直接迁移到推理阶段获得实际加速。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [21] [A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18555)
*Ran Ben-Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Gal Mendelson,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文澄清TurboQuant是EDEN的特例且性能全面劣于EDEN；实验表明EDEN在所有场景下均更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清近期TurboQuant工作与早期DRIVE/EDEN方案之间的关系，纠正TurboQuant在量化设计中存在的冗余与次优性。

Method: 从理论与实验两方面对比：分析TurboQuant_{mse}（固定S=1的EDEN特例）与TurboQuant_{prod}（级联(b-1)位EDEN与1位QJL残差）的构造缺陷；并复现与扩展相关实验。

Result: TurboQuant_{mse}因固定S=1而在中小维度下次优；TurboQuant_{prod}在三方面次优（子优S、残差MSE更差、级联不如直接b位EDEN）；EDEN在所有精度与比特设置上均显著优于TurboQuant。

Conclusion: TurboQuant未超越EDEN，其核心组件本质是EDEN的受限形式；应优先采用EDEN进行量化。

Abstract: This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN.
  First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$.
  Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN.
  Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations.
  Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.

</details>


### [22] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏自编码器（SAE）中的组合式激活控制问题，提出了几何分析框架，揭示了特征联合时的组合坍缩阈值及ReLU偏置下的棘轮效应，证明了非线性干扰会限制线性叠加控制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管稀疏自编码器已成为解耦Transformer特征叠加的强大范式，但组合式控制（即同时激活多个不同语义潜变量）的理论基础尚未被充分探索。现有的线性表示假设常忽略过完备字典中产生的非线性干扰效应，而这些效应对多特征联合控制的稳定性至关重要。

Method: 作者提出了一个几何框架来分析特征联合的不稳定性：将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，在球面字典模型下推导了渐近组合坍缩阈值（由信号锥的高斯平均宽度/统计维度表征）；进一步证明在高偏置状态下，ReLU整流会将微观相关性引起的方差波动转化为系统性漂移，并在组合过程中累积。实验部分在CLEVR数据集上提取结构化语义特征进行验证。

Result: 理论推导得出了组合坍缩的临界阈值，并揭示了ReLU偏置下的棘轮效应——微观相关性引起的方差波动在组合时产生系统性漂移干扰。实验验证了所预测的缩放趋势，发现在CLEVR的结构化语义特征上，层次化相关性会加速从随机基线的过渡转变。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了基于联合的控制存在几何约束，证明了简单线性叠加方法存在可扩展性瓶颈，强调了需要设计显式管理干扰的组合机制来替代朴素的线性叠加方法。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [23] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习样本（UEs）在预训练-微调范式下的表现，发现预训练权重会削弱现有UEs的有效性，并提出浅层语义伪装（SSC）方法以解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习样本研究主要评估从零开始训练的场景，但其在广泛采用的预训练-微调范式下的行为尚未被探索，而该范式下UEs的有效性会显著降低。

Method: 通过语义过滤机制解释UEs失效原因，并提出浅层语义伪装（SSC）策略，将生成过程约束在语义有效子空间内，以绕过预训练权重引入的语义抑制。

Result: SSC方法在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练等挑战性训练范式下均能保持数据的不可学习性，填补了基于预训练的不可学习学习的空白。

Conclusion: 预训练-微调范式会削弱传统UEs的保护效果，而通过层次化欺骗策略将噪声生成限制在语义有效子空间，可以有效解决这一问题。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [24] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了两次随机Hadamard变换(RHT)组合在统计特性上足以替代均匀随机旋转(URR)用于单坐标压缩方案，而三次RHT组合则可满足矢量量化(VQ)对跨坐标弱相关性的需求，并提出了一种线性时间的动态自适应方法以提升实际性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机旋转(URR)是现代量化方法中常见的预处理步骤，但计算开销大。实践中常用随机Hadamard变换(RHT)替代，但其在最坏情况输入下的性能缺乏理论保证。研究旨在确定需要多少次RHT组合才能在统计特性上逼近URR，从而在保持计算效率的同时确保理论性能。

Method: 1) 证明了两次RHT组合后，归一化旋转向量的每个固定坐标的边际分布在Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离下都与标准高斯分布相差O(d^{-1/2})；2) 将这些界限应用于DRIVE和QUIC-FL压缩方案的分析；3) 证明三次RHT组合能实现衰减的坐标协方差，满足矢量量化需求；4) 提出线性时间O(d)的输入矩检验方法，动态调整运行时使用的RHT次数。

Result: 两次RHT组合在DRIVE和QUIC-FL等现代压缩方案中实现了与URR渐近匹配的性能；三次RHT组合确保了任何针对URR优化的有界多维VQ码本在使用时具有相同的期望误差（误差项随维度衰减）；提出的动态自适应方法能根据输入特性优化RHT使用次数。

Conclusion: 本文为RHT替代URR提供了坚实的理论基础：两次RHT足以满足单坐标边际分布收敛需求，三次RHT可满足矢量量化的跨坐标弱相关性需求。这为现代量化方法在保持计算效率的同时确保理论性能提供了指导，而动态自适应策略进一步优化了实际应用中的性能表现。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


### [25] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了递归智能体优化（RAO）方法，通过强化学习训练能够递归生成子任务并委托给自身新实例的智能体，实现了在推理时通过分治策略扩展上下文、泛化到更难任务并提高训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单智能体系统在处理超长上下文和复杂任务时存在上下文窗口限制和泛化能力不足的问题，亟需一种能够让智能体自主分解任务、扩展推理能力的方法。

Method: 提出递归智能体优化（RAO）强化学习方法，训练递归智能体：智能体能够递归地生成子任务并委托给自身的新实例，通过分治策略实现推理时的规模扩展，学习何时、如何委托与通信。

Result: 递归智能体相比单智能体系统享有更好的训练效率，能够处理超出模型上下文窗口的任务，泛化到比训练时难得多的问题，并减少实际运行时间。

Conclusion: RAO方法有效提升了智能体处理复杂任务的能力，证明了递归智能体在训练效率、上下文扩展、任务泛化和实际运行时间方面的优势。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [26] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对混合专家多模态大模型在专家并行推理中的效率瓶颈，提出MACS框架，通过量化视觉token语义价值和动态调整专家资源分配，解决了现有方法在信息异质性和模态动态性上的不足，显著提升了推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理过程中存在严重的“掉队效应”效率瓶颈。现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法应对多模态场景下的两大独特挑战：（1）信息异质性，即大量冗余视觉token与语义关键的token被同等对待；（2）模态动态性，即不同任务中视觉与文本比例的变化导致资源分配不当。

Method: 提出MACS，一种无需训练的推理框架。首先，引入“熵加权负载”机制，通过量化视觉token的语义价值来解决信息异质性问题；其次，设计“动态模态自适应容量”机制，根据输入的实时模态组成动态分配专家资源。

Result: 在多种多模态基准测试上的广泛实验表明，MACS显著优于现有的负载均衡方法，有效解决了MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中的效率问题。

Conclusion: MACS为混合专家多模态大语言模型的高效部署提供了一个新颖、鲁棒且无需训练的解决方案，能够有效缓解专家并行推理中的负载不均衡问题。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [27] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种将结果监督内化为过程监督的方法，通过识别、纠正和重用失败的推理轨迹自动提取过程级学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习推理面临的根本挑战在于如何将序列末尾的稀疏反馈转化为能指导中间推理步骤的细粒度学习信号；现有方法在信用分配精确性和规模化可持续性上存在不足。

Method: 提出监督内化方法，使模型通过识别、纠正和重用失败的推理轨迹，在仅有结果监督的情况下自动提取过程级学习信号，实现更精细的策略优化。

Result: 成功实现了在仅有结果监督条件下自动提取过程级学习信号，为强化学习推理中的细粒度信用分配提供了新路径。

Conclusion: 抽象出一种新的训练范式，模型在强化学习过程中持续生成和完善自身的内部过程监督，区别于外部提供的过程监督。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [28] [CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06571)
*Iason Ofeidis,Nikos Papadis,Randeep Bhatia,Leandros Tassiulas,TV Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CLAD框架，通过结合聚类联邦学习（CFL）和双模式微架构（DM²A），解决了物联网安全中的设备异构性和标签稀缺两大瓶颈，在80%客户端无标签场景下检测性能相对提升30%，通信成本减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和工业物联网的快速扩张带来了大规模、异构的攻击面，传统网络安全机制面临挑战。现有联邦学习入侵检测系统在跨多样化设备行为泛化方面存在困难，且无法有效利用边缘环境中大量未标记数据。

Method: 提出CLAD框架，整合聚类联邦学习（CFL）与新型双模式微架构（DM²A）。DM²A包含共享编码器和两个分支，实现联合无监督异常检测和有监督攻击分类，同时利用标记和未标记客户端数据。聚类组件动态将具有相似流量模式的设备分组，防止全局模型发散。

Result: 在80%客户端无标签场景下，检测性能相对基线提升30%，同时通信成本减半。该框架确保不丢弃任何数据并保留不同操作模式。

Conclusion: CLAD通过统一处理设备异构性和标签稀缺问题，为物联网安全提供了一种有效的隐私保护入侵检测解决方案，在真实边缘环境中展现出显著优越性。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.

</details>


### [29] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT框架，将数据管理重新定义为在线重加权问题，通过动态调整样本重要性实现隐式课程学习，在指令微调和大尺度预训练中均优于现有离线和在线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据管理方法（如数据选择和混合）采用离线范式，与训练过程分离，导致工程开销大、对模型/任务变化脆弱，且硬过滤或重采样会改变数据规模并损害数据多样性。

Method: 提出ADAPT（自适应数据重加权）在线框架，在训练过程中通过基于相似性的质量信号引导的自适应逐样本学习率来动态调整样本权重，而不改变训练样本数量，充当隐式课程学习器。

Result: 在指令微调和大尺度预训练实验中，ADAPT持续优于离线选择/混合方法和先前的在线方法，在相同FLOPs下实现了更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将数据管理重新定义为在线重加权问题是有效的，ADAPT框架能够根据模型演化动态调整样本重要性，在不牺牲数据多样性的前提下提升模型性能，为LLM训练中的数据管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [30] [OpenG2G: A Simulation Platform for AI Datacenter-Grid Runtime Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05519)
*Jae-Won Chung,Zhirui Liang,Yanyong Mao,Jiasi Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Vladimir Dvorkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenG2G，一个用于AI数据中心与电网运行时协调的仿真平台，通过模块化架构支持多种控制范式的比较和评估，以解决AI计算需求增长对电网容量的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算需求的快速增长和数据中心的大规模建设对电网容量和可靠性构成重大挑战，导致新数据中心面临数年的并网延迟，成为AI发展的瓶颈。为缓解这一压力，数据中心开始提供快速功率灵活性以响应电网信号，但缺乏有效的工具来理解大型数据中心对电网的影响并设计有效的协调策略。

Method: 构建OpenG2G仿真平台，采用模块化可扩展架构，包括：基于生产级AI服务真实测量数据的数据中心后端、基于高保真电网模拟器的电网后端，以及连接两者的通用控制器接口。平台支持实现和比较多种控制范式（包括经典控制、优化方法和基于学习的控制器），并量化AI模型和部署选择对数据中心灵活性和协调效果的影响。

Result: OpenG2G能够回答广泛的协调问题，允许用户比较不同控制范式的性能，并量化AI模型和部署决策对协调结果的影响。通过真实的电网场景和AI工作负载验证了平台的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: OpenG2G为AI数据中心与电网的协调研究提供了通用的仿真工具，其模块化架构使研究人员能够快速实现和评估各种控制策略，为解决AI基础设施与电网容量之间的紧张关系提供了技术支撑。

Abstract: AI's growing compute demand and new datacenter buildouts present major capacity and reliability challenges for the electricity grid, leading to multi-year interconnection delays for new datacenters and bottlenecking AI growth. To ease this strain, datacenters increasingly offer rapid power flexibility in response to grid signals, where the datacenter can increase or decrease its power consumption by adapting its workload in real time.
  In order to understand the impact of large datacenters on the grid and to facilitate the design of effective coordination strategies, we build OpenG2G, a simulation platform for AI datacenter-grid runtime coordination. We show that OpenG2G is capable of answering a wide range of coordination questions by allowing users to implement and compare various control paradigms (including classic, optimization, and learning-based controllers), and quantify how AI model and deployment choices affect datacenter flexibility and coordination outcomes. This versatility is enabled by OpenG2G's modular and extensible architecture: a datacenter backend driven by real measurements of production-grade AI services, a grid backend built on high-fidelity grid simulators, and a generic controller interface that closes the loop between them. We describe the design of OpenG2G and demonstrate its usefulness through realistic grid scenarios and AI workloads.

</details>


### [31] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于进化策略的深度学习模型量化优化方法，通过迭代调整部分权重的量化状态来提升模型精度，在VGG、ResNet等架构上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在IoT、移动设备和实时系统中部署面临复杂度和存储大小的限制。虽然量化是主流的模型压缩技术，但现有基于最近邻舍入的量化方法无法保证获得最优精度，因此需要改进量化效率。

Method: 采用进化策略作为优化方法，在每次迭代中改变小部分权重的值，将其转移到不同的量化状态，而非使用传统的最近邻舍入技术。通过适当的算子和参数集来优化已量化的预训练模型。

Result: 该方法能够快速提升量化模型的精度。在VGG和ResNet等流行架构上的图像分类和检测任务中展示了有效性，同时还在自编码器架构上进行了仿真验证。

Conclusion: 基于进化策略的量化优化方法可以有效改进量化模型的最终精度，证明了传统的最近邻舍入量化并不能保证最优精度，为深度学习模型在资源受限设备上的部署提供了新的优化思路。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [32] [Expert Routing for Communication-Efficient MoE via Finite Expert Banks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05278)
*Mohammad Reza Deylam Salehi,Ali Khalesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的MoE路由分析框架，将门控视为随机信道，通过经验估计量证明互信息$I(S;W)$单调追踪泛化差距，为资源高效的专家路由设计提供了实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家（MoE）架构在资源高效机器学习中应用广泛，其门控既是学习组件又是路由接口。现有研究缺乏对MoE门控路由信息量的定量分析，且理论界限通常过于宽松。受MoE门控有限速率解释的启发，研究者希望量化路由信息并为资源感知的推理系统设计提供理论指导。

Method: 1. 将门控视为随机信道，利用$I(X;T)$量化选中专家可用的路由信息；
2. 构建有限专家库MNIST实验，使用预训练CNN专家和离散的、数据相关的选择规则；
3. 基于经验后验$q(W|S)$推导算法互信息$I(S;W)$的闭式离散熵估计器；
4. 扫描数据依赖参数$α$，比较$I(S;W)$与泛化差距的关系及Xu-Raginsky界限的紧致性；
5. 提出均匀联合界限基准及$I(X;T)$的经验估计器；
6. 利用Blahut-Arimoto算法追踪专家库上的精度-速率曲线。

Result: 1. 经验互信息估计$\widehat I(S;W)$单调追踪泛化差距，验证了信息论量与泛化性能的关联；
2. Xu-Raginsky界限如预期般宽松，表明需要更紧的理论界限；
3. 提出的$I(X;T)$经验估计器和Blahut-Arimoto程序能够有效追踪精度-速率曲线；
4. 框架成功应用于资源感知MoE推理系统的分析，证明$I(X;T)$和$D(R_g)$可作为高效专家路由的设计代理。

Conclusion: 本文建立了信息论与MoE路由的理论桥梁，证明了互信息量可作为泛化性能的有效指示器。所提框架为资源高效的专家路由设计提供了实用分析工具，推动了MoE系统的可解释性和优化设计。未来可扩展至更大规模和更复杂的MoE架构。

Abstract: Resource-efficient machine learning increasingly uses sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures, where the gate acts as both a learning component and a routing interface controlling computation, communication, and accuracy. Motivated by finite-rate interpretations of MoE gating, we treat the gate as a stochastic channel and use $I(X;T)$ to quantify the routing information available to the selected expert. To make the associated information quantities tractable beyond synthetic examples, we develop a finite-bank MNIST construction using pretrained CNN experts and a discrete, data-dependent selection rule. Since the selected model belongs to a finite candidate set, the algorithmic mutual information $I(S;W)$ admits a closed-form discrete-entropy estimator from the empirical posterior $q(W|S)$. Sweeping a data-dependence parameter $α$, we observe that $\widehat I(S;W)$ monotonically tracks the generalization gap, while the Xu-Raginsky bound exhibits the expected looseness. We also compare with a uniform union-bound baseline and introduce an empirical estimator of $I(X;T)$ together with a Blahut-Arimoto procedure for tracing an accuracy-rate curve over the expert bank. The proposed framework provides a practical tool for analyzing resource-aware MoE inference systems and for interpreting $I(X;T)$ and $D(R_g)$ as design proxies for efficient expert routing.

</details>
