Result: 在400层单流DiT上，MV-Split成功阻止了导致未稳定基线崩溃的散度崩溃，其轨迹接近基线崩溃前的表现，且在整个训练过程中显著优于LayerScale等token各向同性门控方法。最终成功训练了1000层DiT，验证了架构在极端深度下的可训练性。

Conclusion: MV-Split残差连接有效解决了深度DiT的均值主导崩溃问题，使架构在极端深度（1000层）下仍能保持稳定训练，为深度Transformer的扩展提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Scaling Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) to hundreds of layers introduces a structural vulnerability: networks can enter a silent, mean-dominated collapse state that homogenizes token representations and suppresses centered variation. Through mechanistic auditing, we isolate the trigger event of this collapse as Mean Mode Screaming (MMS). MMS can occur even when training appears stable, with a mean-coherent backward shock on residual writers that opens deep residual branches and drives the network into a mean-dominated state. We show this behavior is driven by an exact decomposition of these gradients into mean-coherent and centered components, compounded by the structural suppression of attention-logit gradients through the null space of the Softmax Jacobian once values homogenize.
  To address this, we propose Mean-Variance Split (MV-Split) Residuals, which combine a separately gained centered residual update with a leaky trunk-mean replacement. On a 400-layer single-stream DiT, MV-Split prevents the divergent collapse that crashes the un-stabilized baseline; it tracks close to the baseline's pre-crash trajectory while remaining substantially better than token-isotropic gating methods such as LayerScale across the full schedule. Finally, we present a 1000-layer DiT as a scale-validation run at boundary scales, establishing that the architecture remains stably trainable at extreme depth.

</details>


### [142] [In-Context Black-Box Optimization with Unreliable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06187)
*Nicolas Samuel Blumer,Julien Martinelli,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FICBO方法，通过结合优化历史和辅助反馈信息，使用结构化反馈先验预训练Transformer模型，在黑盒优化中实现了对多源反馈的自适应利用，在保持对弱或误导性反馈鲁棒性的同时有效利用了有效信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和工程中的黑盒优化通常带有专家、模拟器或启发式等辅助反馈信息。现有方法存在局限：反馈感知的贝叶斯优化方法通常一次只处理一个任务，泛化能力有限；上下文优化器虽然能跨任务适应，但通常假设优化历史是测试时唯一可用的信号。因此需要一种能同时利用优化历史和多源辅助反馈的方法。

Method: 提出反馈信息驱动的上下文黑盒优化（FICBO）框架。该方法的核心是：1）引入结构化反馈先验，建模反馈源在相对于真实目标的可访问性、相关性和失真度方面的变化；2）基于此先验预训练一个反馈感知的Transformer模型；3）测试时，模型通过比较观测到的目标值与辅助信号，在上下文中估计源的可靠性，从而改进查询选择。

Result: 在合成任务和真实世界任务上的实验表明，FICBO能够有效利用信息丰富的反馈源，同时对弱或误导性的反馈源保持鲁棒性，性能优于其他基线方法。实证研究还揭示了模型如何感知测试时的反馈源，为其可解释性和决策过程提供了见解。

Conclusion: FICBO成功地将上下文优化扩展到了多源反馈设置，通过结构化反馈先验和上下文可靠性估计，实现了对异质、潜在不可靠反馈源的自适应利用，为科学和工程领域的黑盒优化提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Black-box optimization in science and engineering often comes with side information: experts, simulators, pretrained predictors, or heuristics can suggest which candidates look promising. This information can accelerate search, but it can also be biased, input-dependent, or misleading. Feedback-aware BO methods typically handle one task at a time, limiting their ability to generalize over multiple sources of feedback. In-context optimizers address cross-task adaptation, but usually assume that optimization history is the only available signal at test time. We study feedback-informed in-context black-box optimization (FICBO), where a pretrained optimizer conditions on both the observed history and cheap auxiliary feedback for the current candidate set. We introduce a structured feedback prior that models how feedback sources vary in their access, relevance, and distortion relative to the true objective, and use it to pretrain a feedback-aware transformer. At test time, the model estimates source reliability in context by comparing observed objective values with auxiliary signals, improving query selection. On synthetic and real-world tasks, FICBO effectively exploits informative feedback while remaining robust to weak or misleading sources, improving over other baselines. Empirical investigations further illustrate how the model perceives test-time sources, offering insights into its interpretability and decision-making process.

</details>


### [143] [Constrained Contextual Bandits with Adversarial Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06190)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个简洁的模块化框架，通过在线回归预言机将带预算约束的上下文赌博问题转化为无约束问题，首次在对抗性上下文设定下同时实现了对遗憾和预算约束违反量的有效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注随机上下文设定，而现实场景中上下文往往具有对抗性。作者旨在解决对抗性上下文下的预算约束上下文赌博问题，在持续设定（预算耗尽后算法仍继续运行）中同时控制遗憾与预算违反量。

Method: 基于Foster等人提出的SquareCB框架，构建了一个模块化框架。该框架利用在线回归预言机，通过自适应定义的代理奖励函数，将有预算约束的问题归约为标准的无约束上下文赌博问题。

Result: 该框架在对抗性上下文设定下取得了改进的理论保证，并提供了高效算法。与以往仅适用于随机上下文的工作相比，本方法适用于更一般的对抗性上下文场景，且分析过程更加简洁透明。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架成功将预算约束上下文赌博问题归约为无约束问题，在对抗性上下文设定下实现了理论保证的突破，证明了在线回归方法在约束优化问题中的有效性。

Abstract: We study budget-constrained contextual bandits with adversarial contexts, where each action yields a random reward and incurs a random cost. We adopt the standard realizability assumption: conditioned on the observed context, rewards and costs are drawn independently from fixed distributions whose expectations belong to known function classes. We focus on the continuing setting, in which the algorithm operates over the entire horizon even after the budget for cumulative cost is exhausted. In this setting, the objective is to simultaneously control regret and the violation of the budget constraint. Building on the seminal $\mathsf{SquareCB}$ framework of Foster et al. [2018], we propose a simple and modular framework that leverages online regression oracles to reduce the constrained problem to a standard unconstrained contextual bandit problem with adaptively defined surrogate reward functions. In contrast to prior works, which focus on stochastic contexts, our reduction yields improved guarantees for more general adversarial contexts, together with an efficient algorithm with a compact and transparent analysis.

</details>


### [144] [Bandit Learning in General Open Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06202)
*Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对开放系统中的强盗学习问题提出了统一框架，引入预训练度、稳定性和全局动态遗憾等新概念，并开发了具有理论保证的certified global-UCB学习方法，证明了遗憾界限的紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于开放系统（智能体可随时到达和离开）的强盗学习研究施加了在实际中常被违反的结构性假设。一般开放系统的学习范式面临新挑战：新到达的智能体导致内生非平稳性；智能体模式决定信息积累速度；新智能体使遗憾随时间范围进一步增长。

Method: 提出统一开放系统强盗问题框架，涵盖异质奖励和一般智能体模式；引入预训练度（量化智能体入场时携带的信息量）、稳定性（衡量新智能体对系统的影响）和全局动态遗憾等新概念；开发具有可证明保证的certified global-UCB学习方法。

Result: 遗憾界限表明，进入不确定性通过预训练度线性进入；在稳定机制中，遗憾由识别持久最优臂所需的时间及智能体模式决定；通过困难实例的下界证明了这些依赖关系的紧性。

Conclusion: 该研究为一般开放系统中的强盗学习提供了理论基础和实用方法，揭示了预训练度和稳定性在控制遗憾中的关键作用，为实际平台中的动态学习问题提供了新的理论指导和算法工具。

Abstract: Recent developments in digital platforms have highlighted the prevalence of open systems, where agents can arrive and depart over time. While bandit learning in open systems has recently received initial attention, existing work imposes structural assumptions that are frequently violated in practice. A learning paradigm for general open systems creates fresh challenges: newly arriving agents induce endogenous non-stationarity; agent patterns determine how quickly information accumulates; and new agents make regret scale further with the time horizon. To this end, we formulate a unified open-system bandit problem with general dynamics, including heterogeneous rewards and general agent patterns. We introduce new concepts to capture the inherent complexities: the \emph{pre-training degree} of new agents quantifies how much information an agent carries upon entry, \emph{stability} measures the impact of new agents on the system, and \emph{global dynamic regret} compares the cumulative expected reward of all active agents with that of the varying optimal arms. We develop certified global-UCB learning methodologies with provable guarantees. Our regret bounds reveal that entry uncertainty enters linearly via the pre-training degree, while in stable regimes, regret is governed by the time needed to identify a persistent optimal arm, as well as by the agent patterns. We further show that these dependencies are tight via lower bounds in hard instances.

</details>


### [145] [Federation of Experts: Communication Efficient Distributed Inference for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06206)
*Muhammad Shahir Abdurrahman,Chun Deng,Azalia Mirhoseini,Philip Levis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出专家联盟（FoE）架构，通过重组MoE块为多个集群来消除或减少分布式推理中的all-to-all通信，显著提升LLM推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家（MoE）虽能提升大语言模型计算效率，但在分布式设置中，专家间的token嵌入通信成为显著瓶颈，亟需优化通信开销。

Method: 将Transformer层的MoE块重构为多个MoE集群，每个集群负责一个KV头，集群内专家并行；集群间通过求和同步后注意力残差以驱动下一MoE块的路由与分发。单节点时完全消除all-to-all通信，多节点时将其限制在节点内。

Result: 在LongBench上，FoE在单节点和多节点设置下均显著提升推理吞吐量和延迟：端到端前向传播延迟降低最高5.2倍，TTFT降低3.62倍，TBT降低1.95倍，同时保持与同规模MoE模型相当的生成质量。

Conclusion: FoE架构有效解决了分布式MoE推理中的通信瓶颈，大幅提升推理效率且不牺牲模型质量，为大规模LLM部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture of experts has emerged as the primary mechanism for making Large Language Models (LLMs) computationally efficient. However, in distributed settings, communicating token embeddings between experts is a significant bottleneck.
  We present the novel Federation of Experts (FoE) architecture. FoE restructures the MoE block of a transformer layer into multiple MoE clusters. Each cluster is responsible for only one of the KV heads and expert parallelism is applied between those experts. Between clusters, a sum synchronizes the post-attention residuals, which then drives routing and dispatch for the next MoE block. In a single-node setting, FoE completely eliminates all-to-all communication as all experts within a group are contained on the same GPU. In multi-node settings, FoE confines all-to-all communication to the intra-node fabric, thus significantly reducing communication overhead.
  An implementation of FoE finds that on LongBench, FoE significantly improves inference throughput and latency in both single-node and multi-node settings, reducing the end-to-end forward-pass latency by up to 5.2x, TTFT by 3.62x, and TBT by 1.95x. It does so while achieving comparable generation quality to a mixture of experts model of the same size and training configuration.

</details>


### [146] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究对比式极限识别与生成问题，在无噪声场景下给出了对比可识别类的精确刻画、对比闭包维度及对比生成的样本复杂度，并证明了对比生成与文本识别互不可比；有趣的是，在有限对抗性噪声下，对比学习相比正例学习具有更优的鲁棒性——存在某些类在任意有限噪声预算下都可从对比对中识别，但即便只有一个被腐蚀的正例也无法识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极限识别与生成框架关注正例或全标注数据，然而许多自然监督信号本质上是关系性的（对比式的），编码样本间的关系而非个体标签。为弥合这一鸿沟，作者提出研究对比式极限识别与生成：学习器观测无序对{x,y}，满足目标二元假设h下h(x)≠h(y），但哪个元素为正例对学习器隐藏。

Method: 提出统一技术对象——公共交叉图（common crossing graph），以覆盖-关联语言统一编码成对歧义、族级生成障碍和腐蚀缺陷。在无噪声设置下：基于Angluin的telltale条件给出对比可识别类的几何精细化刻画；引入对比闭包维度作为Raman等闭包维度的对比类比；精确刻画均匀对比生成的紧样本复杂度；证明对比生成与文本识别的严格层级互不可比。在有噪声设置下研究有限对抗腐蚀的影响。

Result: 无噪声下：给出了对比可识别类的精确刻画（一行几何细化）；定义对比闭包维度并精确刻画均匀对比生成的紧样本复杂度；证明对比生成与文本识别相互不可比的严格层级。有限对抗腐蚀下发现尖锐反转：存在某些类，存在单个预算无关算法可在任意有限噪声预算下从对比对识别，但即便只有一个被腐蚀正例也无法从正例识别。

Conclusion: 对比式极限识别与生成构成了一个丰富的新研究方向，公共交叉图作为统一技术对象有效捕捉了关键结构。令人惊讶的是，对比学习在有限对抗噪声下比正例学习更鲁棒，这为关系性监督信号的理论理解提供了新视角。

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [147] [Playing the network backward: A Game Theoretic Attribution Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06212)
*Jakob Paul Zimmermann,Jim Berend,Georg Loho,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个博弈论框架，将反向归因方法（如梯度、LRP）统一为扩展网络图上的双人博弈，并基于此推导出性能优于现有Transformer专用方法的新归因规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反向归因方法（包括梯度、LRP和Transformer专用规则）缺乏一个统一的框架来比较和分析其底层的反向计算过程，这限制了对这些方法的深入理解和改进。

Method: 作者将反向归因重构为扩展网络图上的双人博弈（基于Gaubert和Vlassopoulos的ReLU Net Game），在此框架下，梯度和alpha-beta-LRP家族表现为特定均衡下博弈轨迹的积分，归因图成为轨迹分布的投影。解释的属性（如定位焦点、鲁棒性等）可被指定为博弈论概念（如策略正则化、风险规避等），进而转化为对现有反向规则的新颖改进。

Result: 在ViT-B/16模型上的实验表明，通过该框架选定的alpha-beta-LRP改进版本，在所有考虑的定位指标上均优于先前的Transformer专用反向归因方法。

Conclusion: 该博弈论框架不仅统一了反向归因方法的理论基础，还通过博弈论概念指导了新归因规则的设计，证明了其在提升模型解释性和定位性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: Attribution methods explain which input features drive a model's prediction, making them central to model debugging and mechanistic interpretability. Yet backward attribution methods, including gradients, LRP, and transformer-specific rules, lack a shared framework in which to compare the underlying backward calculations. We introduce such a framework by recasting backward attribution as a two-player game on an extended network graph, building on Gaubert and Vlassopoulos' ReLU Net Game. Gradients and the full alpha-beta-LRP family arise as integrals over game trajectories under specific equilibria, so attribution maps become projections of trajectory distributions rather than the primary object. Desired explanation properties, such as localisation focus, robustness to input noise, or stable attention routing, can be specified as game-theoretic concepts, including policy regularization, risk aversion, and extended action sets, and translate directly into novel adaptations of the well-known backward rules. On ViT-B/16, one such selected adaptation of alpha-beta-LRP outperforms prior transformer-specific backward methods across all considered localisation metrics.

</details>


### [148] [AffineLens: Capturing the Continuous Piecewise Affine Functions of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06218)
*Yi Wei,Xuan Qi,Furao shen,Jian Zhao,Vittorio Murino,Cigdem Beyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AffineLens框架，用于精确计算和可视化分段仿射神经网络（PANNs）的仿射区域划分，支持多种现代网络组件，并基于此进行了架构表达性的系统实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络可解释性和表达性分析通常依赖间接代理指标（如激活统计或理论上界），缺乏在实际架构和有界输入域下枚举和可视化区域划分的实用且准确的工具。

Method: 提出AffineLens框架：给定校准的输入多胞体，识别与域相交的神经元诱导超平面子集，逐层枚举仿射子区域，返回可证明非空的最大连续分段仿射区域及其内部代表点；利用CPA网络在固定激活模式下的仿射限制特性，支持批归一化、池化、残差连接、MLP和卷积层等组件。

Result: AffineLens提供了区域划分和决策边界的可视化能力，实现了定性检查与定量区域计数；通过区域复杂度指标比较网络，揭示了设计选择如何影响学习函数的几何结构。

Conclusion: AffineLens为PANNs提供了统一的计算和可视化工具，支持广泛的现代网络组件，使得通过区域复杂度指标系统研究架构表达性成为可能，有助于理解网络设计对学习函数几何特性的影响。

Abstract: Piecewise affine neural networks (PANNs) provide a principled geometric perspective on neural network expressivity by characterizing the input--output map as a continuous piecewise affine (CPA) function whose complexity is governed by the number, arrangement, and shapes of its affine regions. However, existing interpretability and expressivity analyses often rely on indirect proxies (e.g., activation statistics or theoretical upper bounds) and rarely offer practical, accurate tools for enumerating and visualizing the induced region partition under realistic architectures and bounded input domains. In this work, we present AffineLens, a unified framework for computing the hyperplane arrangements and polyhedral structures underlying PANNs. Given a calibrated (bounded) input polytope, AffineLens identifies the subset of neuron-induced hyperplanes that intersect the domain, enumerates the resulting affine sub-regions in a layer-wise manner, and returns provably non-empty maximal CPA regions together with interior representatives. The framework further provides visualizations of region partitioning and decision boundaries, enabling qualitative inspection alongside quantitative region counts. By exploiting the affine restriction property of CPA networks under fixed activation patterns, AffineLens supports a broad class of modern components, including batch normalization, pooling, residual connections, multilayer perceptrons, and convolutional layers. Finally, we use AffineLens to perform a systematic empirical study of architectural expressivity, comparing networks through region complexity metrics and revealing how design choices influence the geometry of learned functions.

</details>


### [149] [Memory Inception: Latent-Space KV Cache Manipulation for Steering LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06225)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Michael Zhang,Ilana Greenberg,Adam Alnasser,Lucas Baker,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了记忆植入（Memory Inception, MI），一种无需训练的大语言模型引导方法。该方法通过在选定层插入文本衍生的键值（KV）库，在潜在注意力空间中进行引导，在保持与提示方法相当的控制力的同时，显著降低了KV存储开销（最高118倍），并在人格引导、可更新引导和结构化推理任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM引导方法存在权衡：指令提示（Prompting）控制力强但会在每层缓存引导词元并使长交互变得杂乱；激活引导（Activation Steering）紧凑但控制力较弱且不支持大型结构化提醒。作者旨在开发一种既能保持强控制力又能实现紧凑存储的新型引导方法。

Method: 提出记忆植入（MI）方法，这是一种免训练的引导技术。核心思想是将引导视为选择性KV分配：从文本推导出键值（KV）库，仅在模型路由到它们的选定层插入潜在槽位，而非在整个提示缓存中实例化提醒内容。这种方法避免了在可见文本中显式添加引导内容。

Result: 在人格引导任务上，MI实现了最佳的控制-漂移权衡，性能与提示方法相当且持续优于CAA；在可更新引导任务上，MI支持对话中行为切换而无需重写可见文本，在Qwen3上实现了最高的切换后对齐度；在结构化推理任务（HARDMath和PHYSICS）上，MI在10/12个实验单元格中优于可见提示，同时将内容匹配的KV存储减少高达118倍。

Conclusion: MI作为一种强大的引导方法，特别适用于引导内容持久、结构化或在可见文本中维护成本高昂的场景，成功解决了现有方法在控制力与存储效率之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Steering large language models (LLMs) is usually done by either instruction prompting or activation steering. Prompting often gives strong control, but caches guidance tokens at every layer and can clutter long interactions; activation steering is compact but typically weaker and does not support large structured reminders. We introduce memory inception (MI), a training-free method that steers in latent attention space by inserting text-derived key-value (KV) banks only at selected layers. Rather than materializing reminder content throughout the prompt cache, MI treats steering as selective KV allocation, injecting latent slots only where the model routes to them. On matched personality-steering tasks, MI gives the best overall control--drift trade-off, remaining competitive with prompting while consistently outperforming CAA. On updateable guidance, MI supports mid-conversation behavior shifts without rewriting the visible transcript, achieving the highest post-shift alignment on Qwen3. On structured reasoning, MI outperforms visible prompting on HARDMath and PHYSICS (10/12 subject$\times$mode cells), serving as proxies for structured reasoning in verifiable domains, while cutting content-matched KV storage by up to 118$\times$. These results position MI as a powerful steering method when guidance is persistent, structured, or expensive to keep in the visible transcript.

</details>


### [150] [Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228)
*Hyunjun Na,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Soft-DPG方法，通过基于高斯平滑的平滑贝尔曼方程，解决了标准DPG在稀疏或离散奖励下因依赖评论家动作梯度而导致策略梯度未定义的问题，并实现了Soft DDPG算法，在离散奖励环境中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的确定性策略梯度（DPG）在策略更新时依赖于评论家函数对动作的可微性。然而在实际控制问题中，当奖励稀疏或离散时，该假设被违反，导致策略梯度定义不清和学习不稳定。

Method: 提出一种基于高斯平滑的平滑贝尔曼方程，定义了新的动作价值函数，并以此推导出软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG）。该公式消除了对评论家动作梯度的显式依赖，确保即使Q函数非平滑时梯度依然定义良好。基于此框架实现了深度强化学习算法Soft DDPG。

Result: 在标准连续控制基准测试及其离散化奖励变体上的实验表明，Soft DDPG在密集奖励环境中保持竞争力，在大多数离散化奖励环境中取得明显优势，而标准DDPG对不规则的评论家地形更加敏感。

Conclusion: Soft-DPG为处理非平滑Q函数提供了一种有原则的替代方案，有效解决了DPG在稀疏或离散奖励下的局限性，提升了算法的稳定性和适用范围。

Abstract: Deterministic policy gradient (DPG) is widely utilized for continuous control; however, it inherently relies on the differentiability of the critic with respect to the action during policy updates. This assumption is violated in practical control problems involving sparse or discrete rewards, leading to ill-defined policy gradients and unstable learning. To address these challenges, we propose a principled alternative based on a smoothed Bellman equation formulated via Gaussian smoothing. Specifically, we define a novel action-value function based on a smoothed Bellman equation and derive the soft deterministic policy gradient (Soft-DPG). Our formulation eliminates explicit dependence on critic action-gradients and ensures that the gradient remains well-defined even for non-smooth Q-functions. We instantiate this framework into a deep reinforcement learning algorithm, which we call soft deep deterministic policy gradient (Soft DDPG). Empirical evaluations on standard continuous control benchmarks and their discretized-reward variants show that Soft DDPG remains competitive in dense-reward settings and provides clear gains in most discretized-reward environments, where standard DDPG is more sensitive to irregular critic landscapes.

</details>


### [151] [Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态推荐系统中的规避式推广攻击，提出了UAT-MC方法，通过引入梯度对齐机制解决跨模态梯度不匹配问题，显著提升了系统的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统利用视觉和文本信号缓解数据稀疏性，但也使其更容易受到规避式推广攻击。现有防御方法主要局限于单模态设置且关注投毒式攻击，规避式威胁研究不足。本文发现在多用户推广设置下存在跨模态梯度不匹配现象，即视觉和文本扰动因不同用户群体的主导作用而在不一致方向上优化，这削弱了攻击效果并导致鲁棒训练低估最坏情况风险。

Method: 提出无目标对抗训练与多模态协调方法（UAT-MC）。该方法针对规避式攻击中目标项目未知（区别于投毒式攻击）的挑战，将所有项目视为潜在目标；引入梯度对齐机制显式纠正跨模态梯度不匹配，确保各模态间的扰动同步，从而最大化对抗训练中的对抗强度。

Result: 大量实验证明，UAT-MC在防御-准确性权衡下，能够显著提升对推广攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持可接受的推荐性能。

Conclusion: UAT-MC有效解决了多模态推荐系统中规避式推广攻击的防御难题，通过梯度对齐机制实现了跨模态扰动的同步优化，为多模态推荐系统的安全提供了新的防御思路。

Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

</details>


### [152] [When Graph Language Models Go Beyond Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06239)
*Masatsugu Yamada,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种校准诊断框架，用于区分图语言模型的结构规律学习与训练集记忆，发现大规模模型（375万图）确实获得了超越记忆的结构对齐能力，但主要局限于高频模式，罕见模式表现依然较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前尚不清楚图语言模型是否真正学习了结构规律，还是仅记忆了训练图；现有的聚合保真度指标无法解决这一问题，因此需要开发新的诊断协议来区分记忆与结构对齐。

Method: 开发了一个校准诊断框架，结合三个核心组件：(1)频繁子图挖掘，(2)图级引导基线，(3)三级频率分层。使用该框架在五种TU基准上评估LLaMA风格的图语言模型，并在不同规模（小规模到375万图）和两种序列化方式（规范DFS码和动作序列）下进行验证。

Result: 小规模模型：保真度与逐字记忆难以区分；大规模模型（375万图）：逐字记忆急剧下降，但排序相关性仍接近上限；高频模式在各规模下都能很好复现，罕见模式表现较差且容量增加仅边际改善；单独的固定子样本分析证实对齐并非仅由逐字回忆驱动。

Conclusion: 图语言模型在大规模下能够获得超越记忆的结构规律，但这种能力主要存在于高频模式中，形成规模依赖的交叉现象；罕见模式的学习仍是挑战，该发现跨不同序列化方法具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: It remains unclear whether graph language models learn structural regularities or merely memorize training graphs; this cannot be resolved by current aggregate fidelity metrics alone. We develop a calibrated diagnostic protocol that combines frequent subgraph mining, a graph-level bootstrap baseline, and three-level frequency stratification to disentangle memorization from structural alignment. Using this framework, we show that graph language models can acquire structural regularities beyond memorization at scale, primarily in the high-frequency regime. This is supported by the following empirical evidence: On five TU benchmarks, LLaMA-style graph language models reach high subgraph-rank correlation, yet their alignment is matched or exceeded by the memorization bootstrap in most cases. At small scale, under our bootstrap diagnostic, fidelity is largely indistinguishable from verbatim recall. In contrast, at large scale with 3.75M graphs, verbatim memorization drops sharply while rank correlation remains near ceiling. Crucially, in a separate fixed-subsample analysis, frequent subgraph mining restricted to the novel-only subset closely tracks the corresponding all-generation Spearman correlation, providing evidence that the alignment is not driven solely by verbatim recall. Across all scales, high-frequency patterns are well reproduced, while rare patterns remain poorly covered, and this deficit narrows only marginally as capacity increases. We observe the same scale-dependent crossover under two distinct graph serializations (canonical DFS code and action sequences), providing evidence of robustness in our analysis.

</details>


### [153] [Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Forward-Forward训练中存在的“层搭便车”问题，并提出了三种局部修复方法，虽显著改善了层分离指标，但对最终精度影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward训练的累积良好度变体中存在一种现象：后层可以继承前层已部分分离的任务。本文旨在形式化这一被称为“层搭便车”的现象，即在softplus FF准则下，到达块$d$的类区分梯度会随着前序块累积的正边际呈指数衰减。

Method: 研究了三种局部修复方法：逐块（per-block）、难度门控（hardness-gated）和深度缩放（depth-scaled）。这些方法旨在不依赖反向传播梯度的情况下恢复当前层的分离度量。研究在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet数据集上进行了实验验证。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，这些修复方法显著改善了层分离统计量，深层增益达4倍至45倍，同时精度变化小于一个百分点。Tiny ImageNet验证显示层健康诊断与最终精度之间存在相同的定性差距。校准实验表明，架构和数据增强选择对最终精度的影响大于所研究的训练规则修改。

Conclusion: 累积搭便车是一种真实存在且可修复的优化病理。然而，在所研究的FF训练规则、架构和数据集中，它并不是限制可达到精度的主导因素。架构和数据增强等选择对最终精度有更大影响。

Abstract: Forward-Forward (FF) training allows each layer to learn from a local goodness criterion. In cumulative-goodness variants, however, later layers can inherit a task that earlier layers have already partially separated. We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy. Calibration experiments further show that architecture and augmentation choices have a larger effect on final accuracy than the training-rule modifications studied here. Cumulative free-riding is therefore a real and repairable optimization pathology. Nonetheless, for the FF training rules, architectures, and datasets we study, it is not the dominant factor limiting achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [154] [Structure-Preserving Gaussian Processes Via Discrete Euler-Lagrange Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06246)
*Jan-Hendrik Ewering,Kathrin Flaßkamp,Niklas Wahlström,Thomas B. Schön,Thomas Seel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出拉格朗日高斯过程（LGPs），仅从稀疏位置数据学习物理一致的动力学模型，实现稳定长期预测与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学学习方法常违背物理守恒结构导致能量漂移，且通常需要速度或动量数据；许多实际场景（如动作捕捉、视觉伺服）仅有位置测量。需要一种既能保持几何结构、又能仅用位置数据学习的方法。

Method: 基于离散受迫Euler-Lagrange方程与变分离散化方案构建高斯过程条件算子，构造拉格朗日高斯过程（LGPs），在无外力时保持Lagrange-d'Alembert原理的几何结构，从离散位置快照学习动力学。

Result: 在合成与真实案例（含具有迟滞特性的软体机器人）上验证：LGPs仅用稀疏位置数据即可学习物理一致的动力学，提供稳定长期预测与不确定性量化，展现数据效率与泛化能力。

Conclusion: LGPs为仅含位置测量的实际场景提供物理一致、数据高效的动力学学习框架，克服能量漂移问题，实现稳定长期预测与不确定性量化。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Lagrangian Gaussian Processes (LGPs) for probabilistic and data-efficient learning of dynamics via discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations. Importantly, the geometric structure of the Lagrange-d'Alembert principle, which governs the motion of dynamical systems, is preserved by construction in the absence of external forces. This allows learning physically consistent models that overcome erroneous drift in the system's energy, thereby providing stable long-term predictions. At the core of our approach lie linear operators for Gaussian process conditioning, constructed from discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations and variational discretization schemes. Thereby and unlike prior work, the method enables learning dynamics from discrete position snapshots, i.e., without access to a system's velocities or momenta. This is particularly relevant for a large class of practical scenarios where only position measurements are available, for instance, in motion capture or visual servoing applications. We demonstrate the data-efficiency and generalization capabilities of the LGPs in various synthetic and real-world case studies, including a real-world soft robot with hysteresis. The experimental results underscore that the LGPs learn physically consistent dynamics with uncertainty quantification solely from sparse positional data and enable stable long-term predictions.

</details>


### [155] [The Role of Node Features in Graph Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06250)
*Jan von Pichowski,Alžbeta Hrabošová,Ingo Scholtes,Christopher Blöcker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 图池化在图分类中的性能增益往往有限，研究发现其有效性依赖于节点特征与图拓扑结构的良好对齐，提出了特征质量度量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图池化在图分类任务中被广泛使用，但相比标准的WL-1表达力GNN，其经验增益往往微乎其微或不一致。作者旨在研究这一差距背后的原因，分析节点特征与图拓扑结构之间的相互作用及其对池化目标的影响。

Method: 通过分析节点特征与图拓扑结构的交互作用及其对池化目标的影响，研究揭示了池化操作需要与图拓扑结构良好对齐的节点特征。作者形式化了节点特征实现有效池化的基本要求，并引入了特征质量的定量度量方法。

Result: 研究发现池化算子要求节点特征与图的拓扑结构良好对齐，而这一条件在经验网络中常被忽视且无法保证。当提出的要求得到满足时，池化操作可以在适当的数据集上带来益处并提升性能。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了对图池化有效性理解的理论空白，为实践中如何选择合适的池化策略提供了指导。研究结果表明，节点特征与图拓扑结构的对齐程度是池化效果的关键因素，这一发现对图神经网络的设计和应用具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Graph pooling is commonly applied in graph classification, yet its empirical gains over standard WL-1 expressive GNNs are often marginal or inconsistent. We study this gap by analysing the interaction between node features and graph topology and their effect on pooling objectives. Our analysis reveals that pooling operators require node features that are well-aligned with the graph's topology -- a condition often overlooked and not guaranteed in empirical networks. We formalise fundamental requirements for node features to enable effective pooling, and introduce a quantitative measure of feature quality. Our empirical evaluation shows that, when these requirements are satisfied, pooling can be beneficial and improve performance on appropriate datasets.

</details>


### [156] [The Weight Gram Matrix Captures Sequential Feature Linearization in Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06258)
*Taehun Cha,Daniel Beaglehole,Adityanarayanan Radhakrishnan,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出特征学习方程，揭示权重Gram矩阵是特征演化的关键，将梯度下降解释为虚拟协方差驱动的特征演化过程，并提出目标线性度概念，统一解释了神经网络训练中的多种现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度神经网络如何学习表示仍然是机器学习理论的核心挑战，现有方法缺乏对权重更新与特征演化之间关系的深入理解。

Method: 提出特征学习方程，将权重Gram矩阵作为刻画特征动态的关键对象；引入虚拟协方差概念，将梯度下降解释为隐式诱导特征演化；提出目标线性度指标量化特征与目标之间的线性对齐程度。

Result: 发现深度网络通过顺序变换表示使其趋向目标线性结构；该线性化视角能够统一解释神经坍缩和生成模型中的线性插值等多个经验现象。

Conclusion: 特征中心的框架为理解神经网络训练提供了新视角，特征学习方程和目标线性概念建立了权重更新与特征演化之间的桥梁，为深度学习理论提供了统一的解释框架。

Abstract: Understanding how deep neural networks learn representations remains a central challenge in machine learning theory. In this work, we propose a feature-centric framework for analyzing neural network training by relating weight updates to feature evolution. We introduce a simple identity, the Feature Learning Equation, which identifies the weight Gram matrix as the key object capturing feature dynamics. This enables us to interpret gradient descent as implicitly inducing a hypothetical evolution of features, whose covariance structure - termed the Virtual Covariance - characterizes how representations evolve during training. Building on this perspective, we introduce Target Linearity, a measure quantifying the linear alignment between features and targets. By analyzing the training and layer-wise dynamics, we show that deep networks learn to sequentially transform representations toward target-linear structure. This linearization perspective provides a unified interpretation of several empirical phenomena, including Neural Collapse and linear interpolation in generative models.

</details>


### [157] [Trade-off Functions for DP-SGD with Subsampling based on Random Shuffling: Tight Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06259)
*Marten van Dijk,Murat Bilgehan Ertan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在$f$-DP框架下，对基于随机洗牌采样的DP-SGD进行了紧致分析，得出了封闭形式的透明隐私界限，并与泊松采样进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于泊松采样的$f$-DP分析通常产生非封闭的隐式公式，虽然可计算但缺乏透明性和可解释性。作者旨在针对随机洗牌这一替代采样机制，推导出封闭形式、直观且紧致的隐私界限，以弥补现有方法的不足。

Method: 作者在$f$-DP框架下，利用Berry-Esseen定理对基于随机洗牌采样的DP-SGD进行了理论分析，推导出了紧致的封闭形式界限。此外，还引入了一种基于大数定律推广的新证明技术，用于分析渐进情况下的收敛性质。

Result: 在$σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$的条件下，推导出了紧致的封闭形式隐私界限。对于单轮训练（$E=1$），当$δ=1/100$、$σ=1$时，约$M \approx 1.14\times 10^6$轮迭代和$N \approx 1.14\times 10^7$样本即可实现有意义的差分隐私。在渐进情况下，当$E=c_M^2M$且$c_M\to 0$时，组合后的权衡函数一致收敛于理想随机猜测对角线$1-a$，且$δ$仅具有$O(\sqrt{E})$依赖性。

Conclusion: 随机洗牌采样相比于泊松采样，在$f$-DP框架下能够获得更透明、可解释的封闭形式界限，且在特定条件下能够逼近理想的随机猜测边界。本文为DP-SGD的理论分析提供了新的见解和工具，但关于明确的收敛速率刻画仍是一个开放性问题。

Abstract: We derive a tight analysis of the trade-off function for Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) with subsampling based on random shuffling within the $f$-DP framework. Our analysis covers the regime $σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$, where $σ$ is the noise multiplier and $M$ is the number of rounds within a single epoch. Unlike $f$-DP analyses for Poisson subsampling, which yield non-closed implicit formulas that can be machine computed but are non-transparent, random shuffling admits a tight analysis yielding transparent and interpretable closed-form bounds. Our concrete bounds, derived via the Berry-Esseen theorem, are tight up to constant factors within the proof framework. We demonstrate worked parameter settings for a single epoch ($E=1$) with a corresponding trade-off function $\geq 1-a-δ$, that is, only $δ$ below the ideal random guessing diagonal $1-a$: For $δ= 1/100$ and $σ= 1$, roughly $M \approx 1.14\times 10^6$ rounds and $N \approx 1.14\times 10^7$ training samples suffice to achieve meaningful differential privacy. This is in contrast to recent negative results for the regime $σ\leq 1/\sqrt{2 \ln M}$. Our concrete bounds can be composed over multiple epochs leading to $δ$ having a linear in $E$ dependency, which restricts $E=O(\sqrt{M})$. To go beyond Berry--Esseen, we introduce a new proof technique based on a generalization of the law of large numbers that yields an asymptotic random guessing diagonal-limit result: if $E=c_M^2M$ with $c_M\to 0$, then the $E$-fold composed trade-off function satisfies $f^{\otimes E}(a)\to 1-a$ uniformly in $a\in[0,1]$ with $δ$ having only an $O(\sqrt{E})$ dependency. We compare this asymptotic regime with the corresponding Poisson subsampling asymptotic, and highlight the characterization of explicit convergence rates as an open question.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Rigid Alignment: Graph Federated Learning via Dual Manifold Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06260)
*Wentao Yu,Bo Han,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedGMC（联邦图流形校准）方法，通过双重流形校准机制解决图联邦学习中的语义和结构异质性问题，在保留全局共性的同时最大化本地个性化表示空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图联邦学习方法通过强制对齐客户端与服务器的模型参数或原型来解决异质性问题，但这种对齐隐含了受限的全局线性假设，即用单一的全局一致表示空间来概括本地数据分布。这严重压缩了客户端的个性化表示空间，无法保留多样化的本地图分布。因此需要一种新方法在保留全局共性的同时实现本地个性化。

Method: 提出FedGMC方法，采用双重流形校准机制：1）针对语义异质性，服务器通过等距语义锚点构建几何最优语义流形，指导本地语义流形的校准；2）针对结构异质性，服务器通过构建全局结构模板来构建全局结构流形，指导本地结构流形的校准；3）服务器通过聚合本地流形动态优化全局语义流形和结构流形。该方法避免了刚性对齐，在保留全局共性的同时最大化本地客户端的个性化表示空间。

Result: 在11个同配性和异配性图数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明FedGMC有效平衡了全局共性和本地个性化，显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedGMC从统一的流形视角解决了语义异质性和结构异质性问题，通过双重流形校准机制成功平衡了全局共性和本地个性化，为图联邦学习中的异质性挑战提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Federated Learning (GFL) enables collaborative representation learning across distributed subgraphs while preserving privacy. However, heterogeneity remains a critical challenge, as subgraphs across clients typically differ significantly in both semantics and structures. Existing methods address heterogeneity by enforcing the rigid alignment of model parameters or prototypes between clients and the server. However, these alignments implicitly rely on a restrictive global linearity assumption that summarizes local data distributions using a single and globally consistent representation space. This severely compresses the personalized representation space of clients and fails to preserve diverse local graph distributions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC), a novel paradigm that tackles semantic heterogeneity and structural heterogeneity from a unified manifold perspective. Instead of enforcing rigid alignment, FedGMC introduces a dual manifold calibration mechanism that preserves global commonalities while maximizing the personalized representation space of local clients. Specifically, for semantic heterogeneity, the server constructs a geometrically optimal semantic manifold via equidistant semantic anchors, so as to guide the calibration of local semantic manifolds. For structural heterogeneity, the server constructs a global structural manifold by building global structural templates, so as to guide the calibration of local structural manifolds. Finally, the server dynamically refines both global semantic manifolds and structural manifolds by aggregating local manifolds. Extensive experiments on eleven homophilic and heterophilic graphs demonstrate that FedGMC effectively balances global commonality and local personalization, thereby significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [159] [Inference-Time Refinement Closes the Synthetic-Real Gap in Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06261)
*Eugenio Lomurno,Filippo Balzarini,Francesco Benelle,Francesca Pia Panaccione,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TARDIS框架，通过推理时优化预训练表格扩散模型，在15个基准上实现了比真实数据中位数提升8.6%的下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的表格数据生成方法虽然接近但很少超越真实数据的效用，且弥补这一差距的方法主要局限于训练时的架构改进和模型扩展。推理时优化预训练模型的潜力在表格数据合成领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出TARDIS推理时优化框架，基于冻结的预训练主干网络。核心包括：1) 通过树形Parzen估计器搜索分数级引导配置；2) 引入双向Chamfer优化(BCR)模式，同时在连续域(分数级梯度)和离散域(批量排序)最小化对称Chamfer泛函；3) 可选的软标签蒸馏步骤。每个数据集独立搜索最优配置。

Result: 在15个二分类、多分类和回归基准上，相比真实数据训练模型实现中位数+8.6%下游任务提升(95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], 11/15严格胜出)；相比TabDiff主干在所有15个数据集上平均提升+12.9%；在流形保真度、多样性和样本级隐私方面与主干模型持平。单块消费级GPU上1-80分钟内完成推理。

Conclusion: 推理时优化预训练表格扩散主干能够达到并超越真实数据效用，双向Chamfer优化在大多数数据集上被识别为主导优化模式，为表格数据生成提供了除训练时改进外的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generators set the current state of the art for synthetic tabular data. These methods approach but rarely exceed real-data utility, and closing this synthetic-real gap has so far been pursued exclusively at training time, via architectural advances, scaling, and retraining of monolithic generators. The inference-time alternative, i.e., refining the outputs of a pre-trained backbone with parameters left untouched, has remained largely unexplored for tabular synthesis. We introduce TARDIS (Tabular generation through Refinement, Distillation, and Inference-time Sampling), an inference-time refinement framework that operates on a frozen pre-trained backbone, configured per dataset by a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator search over score-level guidance during reverse diffusion, with each trial's objective set by an inner grid search over post-hoc sample selectors and an optional soft-label distillation step. The search space encodes a single mathematical pattern we name Bidirectional Chamfer Refinement (BCR): the symmetric Chamfer functional between synthetic and real samples is minimized both continuously, via a score-level gradient, and discretely, via batch-ranking post-generation. The per-dataset search recovers BCR-aligned configurations on most datasets, evidence for BCR as the dominant refinement pattern. Across 15 binary, multiclass, and regression benchmarks TARDIS achieves a median +8.6% downstream-task improvement over models trained on real data (95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], Wilcoxon p=0.016, 11/15 strict wins) and improves over the TabDiff backbone on all 15 datasets (mean +12.9%, p<10^-4), matching the backbone on manifold fidelity, diversity, and sample-level privacy. Inference-time refinement of a pre-trained tabular diffusion backbone reaches and exceeds real-data utility in 1 to 80 minutes on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>


### [160] [Can Attribution Predict Risk? From Multi-View Attribution to Planning Risk Signals in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06264)
*Le Yang,Ruoyu Chen,Haijun Liu,Jiawei Liang,ShangQuan Sun,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端自动驾驶规划的分层归因框架，通过从粗到细的区域归因策略识别关键视图和区域，并提取归因熵、相机内空间方差和跨相机基尼系数三个统计量作为规划风险预测信号，在碰撞检测上达到0.77的AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型从多视图输入生成未来轨迹，虽然改善了系统集成，但引入了不透明的决策和难以定位的风险。现有方法依赖于辅助监控模型或生成文本解释，但与规划过程解耦，无法揭示轨迹生成背后的视觉证据。归因方法虽是直接替代方案，但规划任务与图像分类不同，需要处理六视图相机输入并预测连续多步轨迹，要求归因能捕捉关键视图、区域及其对输出的影响。此外，归因图是否能支持风险识别也尚未探索。

Method: 提出一种端到端规划的分层归因框架。以与原始轨迹的L2一致性为目标，设计从粗到细的区域归因策略，在全六视图输入中搜索候选区域并在其中细化归因。进一步提取三个归因统计量作为规划风险的预测信号：归因熵（衡量规划器在联合视觉空间上的依赖集中度）、相机内空间方差（表征每个视图中归因的分散程度）和跨相机基尼系数（量化六个相机间归因分布的不均匀程度）。

Result: 在BridgeAD、UniAD和GenAD上的实验表明，这些统计量与规划风险相关，与轨迹误差的Spearman相关系数为0.30±0.07，碰撞检测的AUROC为0.77±0.04。该信号可泛化到留出场景且性能下降可忽略，并在替代归因基线下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层归因框架能够有效识别端到端规划中的关键视觉区域，提取的归因统计量可作为规划风险的有效预测信号，为端到端自动驾驶的可解释性和风险识别提供了新途径。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models generate future trajectories from multi-view inputs, improving system integration but introducing opaque decisions and hard-to-localize risks. Existing methods either rely on auxiliary monitoring models or generate textual explanations, but are decoupled from the planning process and fail to reveal the visual evidence underlying trajectory generation. While attribution offers a direct alternative, planning differs from image classification by taking six-view camera images as input and predicting continuous multi-step trajectories, requiring attribution to capture both critical views and regions and their influence on outputs. Moreover, whether attribution maps can support risk identification remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a hierarchical attribution framework for end-to-end planning. Specifically, using L2 consistency with the original trajectory as the objective, we design a coarse-to-fine region attribution strategy that searches candidate regions across the full six-view input and refines attribution within them. We further extract three attribution statistics as predictive signals for planning risk, including attribution entropy to measure how concentrated the planner's reliance is over the joint visual space, within-camera spatial variance to characterize how spread out the attribution is within each view, and cross-camera Gini coefficient to quantify how unevenly attribution is distributed across the six cameras. Experiments on BridgeAD, UniAD, and GenAD show that these statistics correlate with planning risk, achieving Spearman correlations of $0.30 \pm 0.07$ with trajectory error and AUROC of $0.77 \pm 0.04$ for collision detection. The signal generalizes to held-out scenes with negligible degradation and remains stable under an alternative attribution baseline.

</details>


### [161] [A Flow Matching Algorithm for Many-Shot Adaptation to Unseen Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06272)
*Tyler Ingebrand,Ruihan Zhao,Kushagra Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil,Sandeep P. Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FP-FM算法，通过学习基函数来表示速度场，并利用最小二乘投影实现从样本到目标分布的高效适应，无需在推理时进行额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型在自然语言条件图像生成等任务上取得了显著成功，但如何仅从示例数据点实现模型适应仍是一个相对未被探索且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 提出Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM)算法，学习基函数以张成对应于训练分布集的速度场，通过计算到该基底的简单最小二乘投影来适应新分布。同时引入多个FP-FM变体，通过丰富系数计算（如使系数依赖时间）来平衡表达能力与计算开销。

Result: 在合成数据集和图像数据集上，FP-FM相比基线方法在精确率和召回率上均取得了显著提升，尤其是在未见过的分布上表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: FP-FM实现了无需额外推理训练即可高效生成来自多样化目标分布的样本，为生成模型的分布适应问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: While generative modeling has achieved remarkable success on tasks like natural language-conditioned image generation, enabling model adaptation from example data points remains a relatively underexplored and challenging problem. To this end, we propose Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM), an algorithm that directly conditions generation on samples from the target distribution. FP-FM learns basis functions to span the velocity fields corresponding to a set of training distributions, and adapts to new distributions by computing a simple least-squares projection onto this basis. This enables efficient generation of samples from diverse target distributions without additional training at inference time. We further introduce multiple variants of FP-FM that provide a trade-off in expressivity and compute by enriching the coefficient calculation, e.g., by making the coefficients dependent on time. FP-FM achieves greatly improved precision and recall relative to baselines across synthetic and image-based datasets, with especially strong gains on unseen distributions.

</details>


### [162] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HACE（层次感知交叉熵），一种用类别层次结构增强标准交叉熵的损失函数，在端到端训练和线性探测中均显著提升了分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失将所有错误分类视为同等重要，忽略了类别层次结构中编码的语义距离信息，导致模型未能利用类别间的层次关系。

Method: HACE包含两个核心组件：（1）预测聚合，将模型的概率质量沿类层次向上传播，确保父节点累积其子节点的置信度；（2）祖先标签平滑，将真实标签信号沿着从真实类别到根节点的路径进行分布。这是一种可直接替换标准交叉熵的损失函数。

Result: 在端到端训练中，HACE在18个架构-数据集组合中的15个上提升了准确率，平均提升4.66%；在使用冻结DINOv2-Large特征的线性探测中，HACE在三个数据集上均优于所有竞争方法，相比次优基线平均提升2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE通过显式引入类层次结构信息，有效提升了分类性能，证明了在损失函数中利用层次结构语义距离的重要性，为层次化分类任务提供了一种简单有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

</details>


### [163] [PACE: Prune-And-Compress Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06278)
*Fabian Akkerman,Julien Ferry,Théo Guyard,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PACE框架，通过交替进行压缩（生成新学习器）和剪枝（剔除冗余学习器）两个阶段，在保证对原始集成模型忠实度的前提下，有效减少集成模型规模，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成模型虽然预测性能强大，但通常需要聚合大量弱学习器，这导致了模型部署困难、可解释性差以及难以进行鲁棒性验证等问题。现有的解决方案（剪枝和压缩）各自独立，存在局限性。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用两阶段策略交替结合压缩与剪枝范式：第一阶段通过理论驱动的主动生成过程创建新学习器，增强初始集成的多样性；第二阶段当无法找到相关新学习器时，对丰富后的集成进行剪枝。整个过程允许对原始集成的忠实度进行精细控制。

Result: 实验表明，PACE方法在性能上优于现有的剪枝和压缩方法，同时提供了对忠实度保证的原则性控制。

Conclusion: PACE成功地将剪枝和压缩两种范式有机结合，在保持模型性能和忠实度的同时有效缩减了集成模型规模，为集成模型的实际应用提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance on prediction tasks, but usually require aggregating a large number of weak learners. This can hinder deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks such as robustness verification. Remedies to this issue fall into two main camps: pruning, which discards redundant learners, and compression, which generates new ones from scratch. We introduce PACE, a framework that interleaves these paradigms in a two-phase strategy. First, new learners are actively generated via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance the diversity of the initial ensemble. When no more relevant learners can be found, a second phase of pruning is performed on this enriched ensemble. During both operations, PACE allows fine control on the faithfulness to the original ensemble. Experiments show that our method outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

</details>


### [164] [INEUS: Iterative Neural Solver for High-Dimensional PIDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06281)
*Jean-Loup Dupret,Davide Gallon,Patrick Cheridito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了INEUS，一种用于求解偏积分微分方程(PIDEs)的无网格迭代神经求解器，通过单跳跃采样替代非局部跳跃积分的显式评估，实现了高效且可扩展的高维问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理信息神经网络(PINNs)在处理PIDEs时，非局部跳跃积分的显式评估计算成本高，且需要对完整PIDE残差进行昂贵的微分操作，这限制了其在高维问题上的应用效率。

Method: 提出INEUS方法，利用单跳跃采样替代非局部跳跃积分的显式评估，将PIDE求解问题重构为一系列递归回归问题；该方法在全域上学习全局解，避免了对完整PIDE残差的昂贵微分。

Result: 在收缩收敛证明支持下，数值实验表明INEUS能够为各类高维线性和非线性PIDEs提供准确且可扩展的解。

Conclusion: INEUS相比PINNs在处理非局部项方面更高效，特别适合高维偏微分方程和偏积分微分方程的求解，具有良好的收敛性保证和数值表现。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce INEUS, a meshfree iterative neural solver for partial integro-differential equations (PIDEs). The method replaces the explicit evaluation of nonlocal jump integrals with single-jump sampling and reformulates PIDE solving as a sequence of recursive regression problems. Like Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), INEUS learns global solutions over the entire space-time domain, yet it offers a more efficient treatment of nonlocal terms and avoids the computationally expensive differentiation of full PIDE residuals. These features make INEUS particularly well suited for high-dimensional PDEs and PIDEs. Supported by a contraction-based convergence proof for linear PIDEs, our numerical experiments show that INEUS delivers accurate and scalable solutions for various high-dimensional linear and nonlinear examples.

</details>


### [165] [Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“元博弈”（metagame）框架，通过将归因方法视为合作博弈并计算其Shapley值，量化特征对模型解释的二级交互效应（元归因），并在多种多模态应用中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型解释方法（一阶归因）无法捕捉特征之间对归因结果本身的交互影响，缺乏对解释方法内部机制的深层理解，因此需要一种方法来量化和解释这种二阶交互效应。

Method: 提出了元博弈概念框架，将归因方法本身视为合作博弈；定义元归因$\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$表示特征$j$对特征$i$归因的方向性影响；通过计算Shapley值量化这种二阶交互效应；理论上证明归因可分层分解为元归因。

Result: 理论上证明了归因分层分解定理，建立了元归因与现有交互指标的联系；实证方面在三个应用场景验证有效性：(i)量化指令微调语言模型的token交互，(ii)解释视觉-语言编码器的跨模态相似性，(iii)解释多模态扩散Transformer的文本到图像概念。

Conclusion: 元博弈框架成功填补了二阶解释效应的理论空白，提供了方向性的特征交互分析工具，在多种多模态解释任务中展现出优越性，为深入理解模型解释机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce the metagame, a conceptual framework for quantifying second-order interaction effects of model explanations. For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices. Empirically, we demonstrate that the metagame delivers insights across diverse interpretability applications: (i) quantifying token interactions in instruction-tuned language models, (ii) explaining cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and (iii) interpreting text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [166] [Region Seeding via Pre-Activation Regularization: A Geometric View from Piecewise Affine Nerual Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06300)
*Yi Wei,Xuan Qi,Furao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种区域播种正则化方法，通过在训练早期鼓励神经元切换面靠近数据点来增加局部仿射区域数量，从而提高深度网络的非线性近似能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论主要关注架构本身的区域计数能力，但实际训练过程中，标准训练在数据访问邻域内实现的区域细化远少于架构理论上支持的数量，且缺乏关于优化过程如何塑造数据附近实现的分区的理论指导。

Method: 提出了一个充分条件：当神经元切换面充分接近数据点时，确保它们与局部邻域相交，从而严格增加局部仿射区域计数；基于此理论，设计了一个即插即用的区域播种正则化器，在训练早期鼓励分区，同时允许后续任务驱动的细化占主导地位。

Result: 通过精确枚举验证，该正则化器增加了实现的仿射区域数量，在玩具数据集上提高了整体性能，在ImageNet-1k上的经典模型中提高了早期准确率并获得了相当（或略好）的最终准确率。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了关于优化过程如何塑造局部分区的理论见解，并提出了一种实用的正则化方法，能够在不影响最终性能的前提下有效提升深度网络的早期表达能力。

Abstract: Deep networks with continuous piecewise affine activations induce polyhedral partitions of the input space, making the number of realized affine regions a natural measure of expressive capacity and a key determinant of how well the model can approximate nonlinear target functions. In practice, standard training realizes far fewer region refinements in data-visited neighborhoods than the architecture could in principle support, while existing region-count theory is primarily architectural and offers little guidance on how optimization shapes the realized partition near the data. Our theory provides a sufficient condition under which bringing neuron switching surfaces sufficiently close to data points ensures their intersection with local neighborhoods, which in turn implies a strict increase in the local affine-region count, yielding a principled training-time handle for seeding data-relevant partitions early in optimization. Guided by these results, we propose a plug-and-play region-seeding regularizer that encourages early partitioning while allowing task-driven refinement to dominate later in training. Experiments show that the regularizer increases the number of realized affine regions via exact enumeration and improves overall performance on toy datasets, while also improving early-stage accuracy and achieving comparable (or slightly improved) final accuracy on ImageNet-1k for classical models.

</details>


### [167] [Molecules Meet Language: Confound-Aware Representation Learning and Chemical Property Steering in Transformer-VAE Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06303)
*Zakaria Elabid,Jan Andrzejewski,Bartosz Brzoza,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 分子生成模型的潜在空间中确实存在化学上有意义的全局操控方向，但这需要通过解码分子验证并控制表征层面的混淆因素（如SELFIES序列特征）才能被发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生成模型通常假设潜在空间具有有意义的几何结构，但属性的可预测性可能仅反映了序列层面的“捷径”而非真实的化学结构信息。研究旨在探究潜在空间中究竟编码了化学信号还是分子表征（如SELFIES）的伪影。

Method: 在SELFIES上训练无监督自回归Transformer-VAE；冻结模型后，使用RDKit描述符拟合线性探针，将探针权重作为候选全局导向方向；提出混淆感知评估框架，通过残差化、混淆方向对齐分析和解码分子遍历来分离化学信号与SELFIES伪影。

Result: 发现SELFIES的长度、分支标记、环标记和标记熵等特征被强烈编码在潜在空间中。在控制这些混淆因素后，证实了cLogP、FractionCSP3、HeavyAtomCount、TPSA、BertzCT和HBA等属性存在稳健的单调导向性。非线性探针表明部分属性具有稳定全局方向，而其他属性更适合用局部潜在梯度描述。

Conclusion: 化学上有意义的导向可以在纠缠的分子潜在空间中产生，但必须通过解码分子进行验证并控制表征层面的混淆因素，才能确保其真实有效性。

Abstract: Molecular generative models often assume meaningful latent geometry, but apparent property predictability can reflect sequence-level shortcuts rather than chemical organization. We study this issue in an unsupervised autoregressive Transformer-VAE trained on SELFIES. After training, we freeze the model, fit linear probes to RDKit descriptors, and use the probe weights as candidate global steering directions. To separate chemical signal from SELFIES artifacts, we introduce a confound-aware evaluation based on residualization, confound-direction alignment analysis, and decoded-molecule traversal. This is necessary because SELFIES length, branch tokens, ring tokens, and token entropy are strongly encoded in the latent space. Under this confound-aware evaluation, we find robust monotonic steering for cLogP, FractionCSP3, HeavyAtomCount, TPSA, BertzCT, and HBA. Nonlinear probes further show that some properties admit stable global directions, while others are better described by local latent gradients. Overall, our results show that chemically meaningful steering can emerge in entangled molecular latent spaces, but only when validated through decoded molecules and controlled for representation-level confounds.

</details>


### [168] [Perceive, Route and Modulate: Dynamic Pattern Recalibration for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06310)
*Siru Zhong,Zhao Meng,Haohuan Fu,Haoyang Li,Qingsong Wen,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态模式重校准机制(DPR)，通过轻量级“感知-路由-调制”流程实现时间序列的token级动态适配，作为骨干网络无关的适配器或独立模型(DPRNet)显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列的局部时间模式持续变化，而现有深度预测模型使用全局共享的固定权重矩阵处理所有时间token，导致模型陷入折中的平均状态，无法适应变化的局部动态特性。

Method: 提出骨干网络无关的动态模式重校准机制(DPR)，采用轻量级“感知-路由-调制”流程：通过学习的自适应响应模式基计算软路由分布，生成时间感知调制向量，并通过残差Hadamard积重校准隐藏状态。

Result: 作为适配器，DPR以最小开销在多种架构上提升预测性能；作为独立模型(DPRNet)，在12个基准上达到竞争性能，验证了动态重校准优于宏观参数扩展。

Conclusion: DPR有效解决了全局共享变换导致的静态模式响应瓶颈，证明了token级动态重校准在时间序列预测中的有效性和通用性，为模型设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Local temporal patterns in real-world time series continuously shift, rendering globally shared transformations suboptimal. Current deep forecasting models, despite their scale and complexity, rely on fixed weight matrices applied uniformly to all temporal tokens. This creates a static pattern response: models settle into a compromised average, unable to adapt to changing local dynamics. We introduce Dynamic Pattern Recalibration (DPR), a backbone-agnostic mechanism that resolves this via token-level recalibration. Through a lightweight "Perceive-Route-Modulate" pipeline, DPR computes a soft-routing distribution over a learned basis of adaptive response patterns, generating a time-aware modulation vector that recalibrates hidden states via a residual Hadamard product. As a backbone-agnostic adapter, DPR enhances forecasting across diverse architectures with minimal overhead, confirming it addresses a general bottleneck. As a minimalist standalone model, DPRNet achieves competitive performance across 12 benchmarks, validating dynamic recalibration against macroscopic parameter scaling.

</details>


### [169] [When Does $\ell_2$-Boosting Overfit Benignly? High-Dimensional Risk Asymptotics and the $\ell_1$ Implicit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06314)
*Ye Su,Jian Li,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了ℓ1隐式偏置下的良性过拟合问题，发现其收敛速度远慢于ℓ2情形，并提出了一种免调参早停规则以恢复最优预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 良性过拟合在ℓ2几何中已被充分刻画，但在贪婪集成算法的ℓ1隐式偏置下的行为仍难以分析。主要障碍在于坐标选择阈值的非线性耦合导致标准谱分解工具失效，因此需要专门针对该算法偏置进行分析。

Method: 通过分析连续时间ℓ2-Boosting在高维情形下的风险，将凸高斯极小极大定理与双侧截断高斯矩的渐近展开相结合，解析求解非光滑ℓ1插值解。进一步分析Boosting流的非光滑次微分动力学。

Result: 在各项同性纯噪声模型下，良性过拟合以线性速率失效，贪婪选择将噪声局部化到稀疏活跃集中，过量方差以对数速率Θ(σ²/log(p/n))衰减。在带k*个头特征值的spiked-isotropic设计中，当r₂=p-k*≫n时风险收敛为零，但仅以对数速率Θ(σ²/log(r₂/n))衰减，慢于ℓ2几何中观察到的线性衰减。

Conclusion: ℓ1偏置下的过拟合收敛速度显著慢于ℓ2情形。为避免这种慢收敛，本文基于有界ℓ1路径条件提出了免调参早停规则，该规则恢复了Lasso基本不等式，并针对ℓ1有界信号达到了极小极大最优经验预测速率。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is well-characterized in $\ell_2$ geometries, but its behavior under the $\ell_1$ implicit bias of greedy ensembles remains challenging. The analytical barrier stems from the non-linear coupling of coordinate selection thresholds, which invalidates standard spectral resolvent tools. To isolate this algorithmic bias, we characterize the high-dimensional risk of continuous-time $\ell_2$-Boosting over $p$ features and $n$ samples. By coupling the Convex Gaussian Minimax Theorem with delicate asymptotic expansions of double-sided truncated Gaussian moments, we analytically resolve the non-smooth $\ell_1$ interpolant. Under an isotropic pure-noise model, we prove that benign overfitting fails at the linear rate: greedy selection localizes noise into sparse active sets, and the excess variance decays at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$ for noise variance $σ^2$. We remark that while this localization mechanism should persist in the presence of signals, the exact signal-noise decomposition remains an open problem. For spiked-isotropic designs with $k^*$ head eigenvalues and $r_2 = p - k^*$ tail dimensions, the risk converges to zero when $r_{2} \gg n$, but only at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(r_2/n))$, which is slower than the linear decay observed in $\ell_2$ geometries. To avoid this slow convergence, we analyze the non-smooth subdifferential dynamics of the boosting flow. This yields a tuning-free early stopping rule that, under a bounded $\ell_1$-path condition, recovers the Lasso basic inequality and attains the minimax-optimal empirical prediction rate for $\ell_1$-bounded signals.

</details>


### [170] [Pro-KLShampoo: Projected KL-Shampoo with Whitening Recovered by Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06316)
*Ruotong Sun,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pro-KLShampoo优化器，通过利用KL-Shampoo预条件子特征谱的spike-and-flat结构，结合Kronecker分解与正交化方法，在多个LLM预训练任务上实现了性能、内存和训练速度的全面提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预训练优化器分为两条孤立路线：基于KL散度最小化的Kronecker分解预条件（如KL-Shampoo）和梯度动量正交化（如Muon）。本文观察到KL-Shampoo的Kronecker预条件子特征谱呈现spike-and-flat形状（少数主导特征值后接近似均匀尾部），这一结构在层级和训练阶段中普遍存在，并在秩为ρ的信号加噪声梯度模型下严格成立。该观察促使作者探索如何利用这一谱结构来改进优化器。

Method: 提出Pro-KLShampoo算法：将KL-Shampoo的一个Kronecker因子限制为与spike-and-flat形状对齐的参数化族——在跟踪的r维子空间上保持完整谱结构，在剩余n-r维方向上使用单一共享特征值。在这些方向上应用正交化，通过数学恒等式证明该正交化能够恢复完整KL-Shampoo预条件子的代数形式，从而有效结合两条优化路线。

Result: 在GPT-2 124M/350M和LLaMA 134M/450M四种预训练规模上，Pro-KLShampoo在所有测试的子空间秩下均一致优于KL-Shampoo，在验证损失、峰值GPU内存占用和达到各损失水平的实际训练时间三个指标上都取得改善。

Conclusion: KL-Shampoo预条件子的spike-and-flat谱结构可以被有效利用来设计更高效的优化器；Pro-KLShampoo通过将Kronecker分解与正交化技术有机结合，成功统一了先前孤立发展的两条优化路线，在多个LLM预训练任务上实现了性能与效率的双重提升。

Abstract: Optimizers that exploit the matrix structure of gradients are central to modern LLM pre-training, with two distinct frontiers: explicit Kronecker-factored preconditioning -- most recently KL-Shampoo, which estimates the preconditioner via KL divergence minimization -- and orthogonalization of the gradient momentum, exemplified by Muon and analyzed as steepest descent under the spectral norm. The two routes are typically developed in isolation. We make a structural observation about KL-Shampoo's Kronecker preconditioners: their eigenvalue spectra exhibit a \emph{spike-and-flat} shape -- a few dominant eigenvalues followed by an approximately uniform tail -- across layers and training stages, holding exactly under a rank-$ρ$ signal-plus-noise gradient model. We exploit this structure by restricting one of KL-Shampoo's Kronecker factors to a parametric family aligned with the spike-and-flat shape: full spectral structure on a tracked $r$-dimensional subspace, single shared eigenvalue across the remaining $n-r$ directions. On these directions, we apply orthogonalization. An identity shows that this orthogonalization recovers the algebraic form of full KL-Shampoo's preconditioner. On four pre-training scales (GPT-2 124M / 350M, LLaMA 134M / 450M), Pro-KLShampoo consistently outperforms KL-Shampoo at every subspace rank we test in validation loss, peak per-GPU memory, and wallclock time to reach each loss level.

</details>


### [171] [SMolLM: Small Language Models Learn Small Molecular Grammar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06322)
*Akhil Jindal,Harang Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SMolLM，一个仅53K参数的权重共享Transformer，在ZINC-250K基准上生成SMILES的有效率达到95%，性能超越参数量10倍的标准GPT，并从机制层面揭示了模型按固定顺序（括号→环→化合价）学习化学语法的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于分子设计的语言模型参数规模已达数亿，但学界对其如何学习和掌握化学语法（Chemical Grammar）的内在机制知之甚少。研究旨在构建一个高效的分子生成器，并深入解析模型在形式语言领域中进行迭代计算的机理。

Method: 训练了一个名为SMolLM的53K参数权重共享Transformer用于生成SMILES字符串；采用错误分类、线性探测和稀疏自编码器等技术手段分析模型行为；通过注意力头和passes的系统消融实验定位关键功能组件。

Result: SMolLM在ZINC-250K药物分子基准测试上实现了95%的生成有效率，优于参数量多10倍的标准GPT；机制分析发现同一模块按固定顺序分阶段解决SMILES约束：首先匹配括号，其次处理环，最后验证化合价；消融实验将第一步括号匹配功能定位到了单个注意力头上。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅提供了一个紧凑且机械可解释性强的分子生成模型，还为研究形式语言领域中的迭代计算提供了理想的实验平台。

Abstract: Language models for molecular design have scaled to hundreds of millions of parameters, yet how they learn chemical grammar is poorly understood. We train SMolLM, a 53K-parameter weight-shared transformer, to generate novel SMILES with 95% validity on the ZINC-250K drug-like-molecule benchmark, outperforming a standard GPT with 10 times more parameters. Mechanistically, the same block resolves SMILES constraints across passes in a fixed order: brackets first, rings second, and valence last, as shown by error classification, linear probing, and sparse autoencoders. A systematic ablation across attention heads and passes further localizes the first bracket-matching step to a single attention head. Together, these results yield a compact, mechanistically interpretable molecular generator and a testbed for studying iterative computation in formal-language domains.

</details>


### [172] [LINC: Decoupling Local Consequence Scoring from Hidden Matching in Constructive Neural Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06332)
*Shaofeng Qin,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LINC解码器架构，通过显式计算局部推理后果（如行驶、等待、松弛和容量变化），显著提升神经路由求解器性能，在CVRPTW上将PolyNet的误差差距减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的构造性神经路由求解器在评分下一个动作时，通常将解码器上下文与候选嵌入进行匹配，而隐藏了确定性的单步后果（如行驶、等待、松弛和容量变化）。这导致模型需要从隐状态中重新发现转移算术，限制了性能提升。

Method: 提出LINC（Local Inference via Normed Comparison）解码器候选决策架构。LINC显式计算确定性单步后果，并根据其决策角色进行使用：居中的相对后果通过共享的线性局部评分器进行比较，而可行集摘要则调节解码器上下文。这种方法保留了标准的全局匹配，同时释放了隐状态重新发现转移算术的负担。

Result: 在带时间窗的容量车辆路径问题（CVRPTW）上，LINC将PolyNet在Solomon/Homberger基准上的误差差距从13.83%/38.15%降低至7.26%/14.71%。同样的接口扩展到容量车辆路径问题（CVRP）和旅行商问题（TSP）时，也改善了外部基准误差差距。

Conclusion: LINC通过显式建模局部推理后果，有效解决了传统方法隐藏确定性转移信息的问题，在多个车辆路径规划问题上取得了显著性能提升，证明了显式计算局部推理后果的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Constructive neural routing solvers usually score the next action by matching a decoder context to candidate embeddings, hiding deterministic one-step consequences such as travel, waiting, slack, and capacity changes. We propose LINC (Local Inference via Normed Comparison), a decoder-side candidate decision architecture that computes these consequences explicitly. LINC uses them according to their decision role: centered relative consequences are compared by a shared linear local scorer, while feasible-set summaries modulate the decoder context. This preserves standard global matching and relieves the hidden state from rediscovering transition arithmetic. The Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW) serves as the main constrained-routing stress test; the same interface extends to the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP) and Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). In particular, for CVRPTW, LINC reduces PolyNet's Solomon/Homberger gaps from 13.83\%/38.15\% to 7.26\%/14.71\%; for TSP and CVRP, it also improves external-benchmark gaps.

</details>


### [173] [Eliciting associations between clinical variables from LLMs via comparison questions across populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06335)
*Fabian Kabus,Kian Kordtomeikel,Thomas Brox,Heinz Wiendl,Daiana Stolz,Harald Binder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于患者对比三元组问题的间接诱导方法，能够从大语言模型中恢复有意义的医学关联结构和保守的因果候选链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的训练数据包含大量生物医学文献，反映了不同患者群体的信息。研究者希望探索如何从中恢复患者特征之间的相关性和因果联系，作为医疗决策的关键基础，同时避免直接诱导的陷阱。

Method: 提出基于结构化对比问题的方法：1）设计患者对比三元组问题；2）构建统计模型估计相关性（无需访问模型内部激活）；3）通过提示级别的环境变化诱导不同子群体的相关性估计；4）应用不变因果预测（ICP）方法获得保守的候选父节点链接。

Result: 在慢性阻塞性肺疾病（COPD）和多发性硬化症（MS）两个临床领域验证了方法。诱导出的相关性在不同提示环境下平滑、稳定且具有临床可解释性，同时表现出统计显著的差异，支持下游的不变性测试，ICP能够提供少量候选不变父节点链接。

Conclusion: 通过三元组比较的间接诱导方法能够从LLMs中恢复有意义的关联结构，并提供了一条从隐式相关性到因果陈述的谨慎路径，所得因果结论与LLM的回答模式相一致。

Abstract: The training data of large language models (LLMs) comprises a wide range of biomedical literature, reflecting data from many different patient populations. We investigate how it might be possible to recover information on correlation and causal links between patient characteristics, as a key building block for medical decision making. To avoid the pitfalls of direct elicitation, we propose an approach based on structured comparison questions, specifically patient comparison triplet questions. This is combined with a statistical model for the LLM representation that provides estimates of correlations without access to activations or model internals. Intuitively, we consider how similarity decisions of LLMs based on a first variable are affected by providing information on a second variable for one of the patients being assessed. We then induce prompt-level environment shifts to obtain correlation estimates for different subpopulations, which enables an invariant causal prediction (ICP) approach to obtain conservative candidate parent links. We demonstrate the method in two clinical domains, chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) and multiple sclerosis (MS). Across prompted environments, the elicited correlations are smooth, stable, and clinically interpretable, yet vary in a statistically significant way that supports downstream invariance testing, such that ICP provides a small set of candidate invariant parent links. These results show that indirect elicitation via triplet comparisons can recover meaningful association structure from LLMs and offer a cautious route from implicit correlations to causal statements that are congruent with LLM answering patterns.

</details>


### [174] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个决策理论框架来分析模型级联的成本-质量权衡，发现确定性阈值级联的性能主要受限于结构成本（必须先支付便宜模型的生成成本），而非中间阶段不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型级联方法将延迟阈值视为经验超参数，缺乏对模型池上成本-质量前沿几何特性的理论指导。需要建立一个理论基础来理解和优化级联策略的成本-质量权衡。

Method: 提出了一个基于约束优化和对偶性的决策理论框架。对于两模型级联，证明了成本-质量前沿在置信度支持的递减效益区域上的分段凹性；对于k个模型的池，将确定性两模型阈值级联的前沿表征为C(k,2)个成对级联的逐点包络；对于k模型级联，推导了一阶条件，其中单一影子价格使跨阶段边界的边际质量成本相等。

Result: 在五个基准测试（MATH、MMLU、TriviaQA、SimpleQA、LiveCodeBench）和五个提供商的八个模型上验证了框架。确定性阈值级联类中，全固定链表现不如成对包络，优化子序列级联也未带来实际有意义的保留增益。轻量级预生成路由器在五个数据集中的四个上超越了最佳级联策略，主要因为它避免了直接发送到大型模型的查询在便宜模型上的生成成本。

Conclusion: 级联性能主要受限于结构成本——级联在做出升级决策前必须支付便宜模型的成本，而非中间阶段的短缺。这为理解级联策略的局限性提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [175] [Topological Signatures of Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06352)
*Yifan Tang,Qiquan Wang,Inés García-Redondo,Anthea Monod*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用持久同调（Persistent Homology）分析神经网络的嵌入矩阵，发现Grokking现象具有清晰的拓扑特征：一阶同调（H1）的最大持久性和总持久性急剧增加，表明网络正在学习任务内在的循环结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking现象（模型在长时间过拟合后突然实现泛化）的内在机制尚不清晰。现有基于傅里叶分析和局部内蕴维度的诊断方法存在局限，研究旨在寻找一种能同时捕捉局部与全局、多尺度结构的统一表征框架，以深入理解神经网络如何在训练中内化潜在结构。

Method: 研究者针对在模运算任务上训练的多种模型，提取其嵌入矩阵生成点云数据。应用持久同调方法计算拓扑特征，重点关注一阶同调（H1）的最大持久性和总持久性，并通过持久图分析拓扑特征的演变。同时对比了傅里叶分析和局部内蕴维度等现有方法，并在不同数据机制和控制设置下进行了消融实验。

Result: 实验发现Grokking现象具有一致的拓扑特征：随着泛化的发生，H1的最大持久性和总持久性出现急剧上升。持久图显示出现了一个主导的长寿命拓扑特征，反映了模运算任务的循环结构。相比现有诊断方法，持久同调提供了统一的几何与拓扑表征。消融实验证明该拓扑转变与泛化而非记忆相关。

Conclusion: 持久同调为分析神经网络在训练中内化潜在结构提供了一个有原则且可解释的框架。该方法能有效捕捉表示学习中的局部与全局多尺度结构，为理解Grokking现象和神经网络学习机制提供了新的拓扑学视角。

Abstract: We study the grokking phenomenon through the lens of topology. Using persistent homology on point clouds derived from the embedding matrices of a range of models trained on modular arithmetic with varying primes, we identify a clear and consistent topological signature of grokking: a sharp increase in both the maximum and total persistence of first homology ($H_1$). Persistence diagrams reveal the emergence of a dominant long-lived topological feature together with increasingly structured secondary features, reflecting the underlying cyclic structure of the task. Compared to existing spectral and geometric diagnostics -- specifically, Fourier analysis and local intrinsic dimension -- persistent homology provides a unified geometric and topological characterization of representation learning, capturing both local and global multi-scale structure. Ablations across data regimes and control settings show that these topological transitions are tied to generalization rather than memorization. Our results suggest that persistent homology offers a principled and interpretable framework for analyzing how neural networks internalize latent structure during training.

</details>


### [176] [Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Modelling with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06355)
*Ignacio Peis,Pablo M. Olmos,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM)，这是首个能够在不完整数据集上直接训练的Order-Agnostic自回归模型框架，在插补任务和主动信息获取方面均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Order-Agnostic自回归模型在深度生成建模中表现出色，但其在不完整数据场景下的应用尚未被充分探索。现有方法缺乏在不完整数据集上直接训练的原则性框架，且未能充分利用该类模型在处理缺失数据方面的潜力。

Method: (1) 揭示标准训练过程在完全观测数据上隐式地执行了完全随机缺失(MCAR)机制下的插补；(2) 提出首个原则性框架，用于在一般缺失机制下直接在不完整数据集上训练该类模型；(3) 利用摊销条件密度估计进行主动信息获取，即顺序选择对下游预测或推断最具信息量的缺失变量。

Result: 在一系列真实世界基准测试中，MO-ARM模型在插补任务上持续优于现有的插补基线方法，特别是在高缺失率设置下表现出色。模型还展示了在主动信息获取任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了Order-Agnostic自回归模型在不完整数据处理领域的空白，提供了理论洞察和实用的训练框架，证明了该类模型在处理缺失数据问题上的优势和广泛适用性。

Abstract: Order-Agnostic autoregressive models have demonstrated strong performance in deep generative modeling, yet their use in settings with incomplete data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reinterpret them through the lens of missing data. First, we show that their standard training procedure on fully observed data implicitly performs imputation under a missing completely at random mechanism, resulting in robust out-of-sample imputation performance in settings with high missingness. Second, we introduce the first principled framework for training them directly on incomplete datasets under general missingness mechanisms. Third, we leverage their amortized conditional density estimation to perform active information acquisition, i.e., sequentially selecting the most informative missing variables for downstream prediction or inference. Across a suite of real-world benchmarks, our Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM) consistently outperforms established imputation baselines.

</details>


### [177] [Memory Efficient Full-gradient Attacks (MEFA) Framework for Adversarial Defense Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06357)
*Yuan Du,Mitchel Hill,HanQin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于梯度检查点的内存高效全梯度评估框架，实现了对迭代随机净化防御的精确白盒攻击评估，发现现有近似梯度评估方法高估了模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对迭代随机净化防御（如扩散模型和Langevin采样）的鲁棒性评估通常因内存限制而采用近似反向传播，这会削弱攻击信号并导致高估鲁棒性；同时净化轨迹的随机性也缺乏控制，影响评估可靠性。

Method: 提出结合梯度检查点技术（gradient checkpointing）的内存高效全梯度评估框架，通过增加重计算换取更低内存使用，实现端到端精确梯度计算；同时设计控制随机变异的评估协议。

Result: 在基于扩散模型的净化方法和基于能量模型（EBM）的Langevin采样上的实验表明：全梯度攻击能发现近似梯度评估遗漏的漏洞，在$\ell_{\infty}$和$\ell_{2}$范数下实现了更强的白盒攻击，并支持分布外鲁棒性探测。

Conclusion: 精确梯度评估对于迭代随机防御的可靠基准测试至关重要，现有基于近似梯度的评估方法可能显著高估模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: This work studies the robust evaluation of iterative stochastic purification defenses under white-box adversarial attacks. Our key technical insight is that gradient checkpointing makes exact end-to-end gradient computation through long purification trajectories practical by trading additional recomputation for substantially lower memory usage. This enables full-gradient adaptive attacks against diffusion- and Langevin-based purification defenses, where prior evaluations often resort to approximate backpropagation due to memory constraints. These approximations can weaken the attack signal and risk overestimating robustness. In parallel, stochasticity in iterative purification is frequently under-controlled, even though different purification trajectories can substantially change reported robustness metrics. Building on this insight, we introduce a memory-efficient full-gradient evaluation framework for stochastic purification defenses. The framework combines checkpointed backpropagation with evaluation protocols that control stochastic variability, thereby reducing memory bottlenecks while preserving exact gradients. We evaluate diffusion-based purification and Langevin sampling with Energy-Based Models (EBMs), demonstrating that full-gradient attacks uncover vulnerabilities missed by approximate-gradient evaluations.
