More importantly, our results identify regimes in which the specific strengths of each paradigm make it best suited: CP is most versatile overall, MaxSAT handles hard-voting ensembles particularly well, and MILP remains competitive in amortized inference settings with a moderate number of split levels.

</details>


### [214] [Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06562)
*Meena Al Hasani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，评估了模型复杂度和特征选择对乳腺癌亚型分类的影响。结果表明，逻辑回归在亚型分类中表现最稳定均衡，能更好地识别罕见类别；随机森林在少数亚型上表现欠佳；SVM对特征维度敏感。研究强调了在高维生物分类任务中模型简洁性、评估指标和特征选择的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌亚型的准确分类对诊断和治疗选择至关重要，但基因表达数据具有高维度和样本量有限的特点，给机器学习模型带来了挑战。因此需要系统评估模型复杂度和特征选择对分类性能的影响。

Method: 使用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，采用不同数量的高变基因（50至20,518个）训练逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机（SVM）模型。通过分层5折交叉验证进行性能评估，使用准确率和宏观F1分数作为评价指标。

Result: 所有模型均实现了较高的准确率，但宏观F1分析揭示了亚型水平上的显著差异。逻辑回归在各亚型上表现最稳定均衡，对罕见类别的检测能力有所提升。随机森林尽管整体准确率较高，但在少数亚型上表现不佳。SVM则表现出对特征维度的敏感性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在高维生物分类任务中，模型简洁性、评估指标的选择以及特征选择至关重要。简单的模型（如逻辑回归）可能比复杂模型更适合处理类别不平衡的乳腺癌亚型分类问题。

Abstract: Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models.
  In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

</details>


### [215] [Criticality and Saturation in Orthogonal Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06563)
*Max Guillen,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了正交初始化神经网络有限宽度修正的张量递推关系，从理论上解释了正交初始化相比高斯初始化在深层网络中的稳定性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来已知正交权重初始化相比独立同分布（i.i.d.）高斯初始化能改善训练性能，Day等人近期观察到正交初始化的有限宽度张量在大深度下趋于稳定，而i.i.d.初始化则不然，但缺乏理论解释。

Method: 推导正交初始化下网络统计量有限宽度展开中张量的显式逐层递推关系；将i.i.d.情况下使用的费曼图方法推广到适用于任意1/宽度阶数；针对具有消失不动点的激活函数分析递推关系的大深度行为。

Result: 推导出的递推关系成功复现了实验观察到的正交初始化有限宽度张量在大深度下的稳定性；理论预测的数值解与大深度渐近展开式与蒙特卡洛网络集合估计高度吻合。

Conclusion: 本文为有限宽度正交权重初始化非线性网络的稳定性提供了完整的理论解释，填补了该领域长期存在的理论空白。

Abstract: It has been known for a long time that initializing weight matrices to be orthogonal instead of having i.i.d. Gaussian components can improve training performance. This phenomenon can be analyzed using finite-width corrections, where the infinite-width statistics are supplemented by a power series in $1/\mathrm{width}$. In particular, recent empirical results by Day et al. show that the tensors appearing in this treatment stabilize for large depth, as opposed to the tensors of i.i.d.-initialized networks. In this article, we derive explicit layer-wise recursion relations for the tensors appearing in the finite-width expansion of the network statistics in the case of orthogonal initializations. We also provide an extension of recently-introduced Feynman diagrams for the corresponding recursions in the i.i.d.-case which are valid to all orders in $1/\mathrm{width}$. Finally, we show explicitly that the recursions we derive reproduce the stability of the finite-width tensors which was observed for activation functions with vanishing fixed point. This work therefore provides a theoretical explanation for the stability of nonlinear networks of finite width initialized with orthogonal weights, closing a long-standing gap in the literature. We validate our theoretical results experimentally by showing that numerical solutions of our recursion relations and their analytical large-depth expansions agree excellently with Monte-Carlo estimates from network ensembles.

</details>


### [216] [SNAPO: Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization for Optimal Control via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06570)
*Dmitri Goloubentsev,Natalija Karpichina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SNAPO框架，通过将神经策略嵌入可微分模拟器并利用伴随方法，实现了高效的策略优化与大规模敏感性分析，在三个工业领域验证了其显著的速度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的序列决策问题（如天然气储存、养老金管理、制药生产）面临高维状态空间的挑战。传统的动态规划方法随状态维度呈指数级扩展，无法处理大规模问题；而黑箱强化学习虽然能处理高维状态，但训练缓慢且无法提供敏感性分析（即模型输出对输入参数的梯度）。因此需要一种既能高效处理高维问题，又能快速计算敏感性的方法。

Method: 提出了SNAPO（Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization）框架：1) 将神经策略嵌入已知的可微分模拟器中；2) 使用平滑近似替代硬约束以确保可微分性；3) 利用伴随方法（adjoint method）在单次反向传播中计算目标函数对所有策略参数和输入的精确梯度。敏感性分析的成本仅相当于一次反向传播，与敏感性数量无关。

Result: 在三个领域验证了方法的有效性：1) 天然气储存：训练时间不到1分钟，可零额外成本计算365个远期曲线敏感性；2) 养老金资产负债管理：相比传统的扰动重估法（bump-and-revalue），敏感性计算速度提升6.5-200倍，提升倍数随风险因子数量增加而增长；3) 制药生产：通过4单元流程链计算跨单元敏感性，仅需5次伴随传播即可在74.5毫秒内获得20个ICH Q8监管敏感性。所有敏感性均由训练策略的同一次反向传播产生。

Conclusion: SNAPO框架成功将深度学习与可微分模拟结合，解决了传统方法在高维序列决策问题中的计算瓶颈。其核心优势在于敏感性分析的边际成本极低（仅需一次反向传播），使得该方法特别适合需要大量风险分析的实际工业应用。该研究为物理/工程约束下的深度强化学习提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Many real-world problems require sequential decisions under uncertainty: when to inject or withdraw gas from storage, how to rebalance a pension portfolio each month, what temperature profile to run through a pharmaceutical reactor chain. Dynamic programming solves small instances exactly but scales exponentially in state dimensions. Black-box reinforcement learning handles high-dimensional states but trains slowly and produces no sensitivities. We introduce SNAPO (Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization), a framework that embeds a neural policy inside a known, differentiable simulator, replaces hard constraints with smooth approximations, and computes exact gradients of the objective with respect to all policy parameters and all inputs in a single adjoint pass. We demonstrate SNAPO on three domains: natural gas storage (training in under a minute, 365 forward curve sensitivities at no additional cost per sensitivity), pension fund asset-liability management (6.5x-200x sensitivity speedup over bump-and-revalue, scaling with the number of risk factors), and pharmaceutical manufacturing (cross-unit sensitivities through a 4-unit process chain, with 20 ICH Q8 regulatory sensitivities from 5 adjoint passes in 74.5 milliseconds). All sensitivities are produced by the same backward pass that trains the policy, at a cost proportional to one reverse pass regardless of how many sensitivities are computed.

</details>


### [217] [Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06575)
*Victor Daniel Gera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度学习优化中方向对齐与损失收敛可解耦，提出GONO优化器，利用梯度方向一致性自适应调节动量，理论收敛率与Adam一致，实验表现竞争力强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者识别并形式化了一个未被充分研究的现象：方向对齐与损失收敛可以解耦。优化器可能表现出近乎完美的方向一致性（连续梯度余弦相似度cc_t趋近于1），但损失仍保持高位或下降缓慢。这揭示现有优化器（如Adam、SGD、RMSprop）缺乏利用梯度方向时间一致性的显式机制，仅依赖基于幅值的信号，无法区分平台期、鞍点和真正的收敛。

Method: 提出GONO（Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer），基于cc_t（连续梯度余弦相似度）自适应调节Adam的动量系数beta_1：在方向一致时放大动量，在振荡时抑制动量。理论上证明GONO匹配Adam的O(1/sqrt(T))收敛率，且当信号无信息时精确退化为Adam。

Result: cc_t在振荡检测上达到F1=1.00（梯度范数仅为0.45）。GONO在MNIST（98.15%）、CIFAR-10（43.14%）和ResNet-18（75.44%）上与AdamW表现相当，确立了方向对齐作为理论扎实、实际可行的优化信号。

Conclusion: 方向对齐是一个有价值但未被充分利用的优化信号。GONO通过利用梯度方向时间一致性，为优化器设计提供了新的思路，在保持理论保证的同时展现了实际竞争力。

Abstract: We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer

</details>


### [218] [On the Safety of Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06576)
*Xiaoguang Guo,Zehong Wang,Ziming Li,Shawn Spitzel,Soonwoo Kwon,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRL-Safety，一个多轴安全评估基准，用于评估图表示学习方法在五类安全轴（鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性、可解释性）下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图表示学习评估主要关注干净场景下的迁移、适应和任务覆盖，但尚不清楚当部署压力影响图信号、图上下文、标签支持、结构组或预测证据时，这些方法是否依然可靠。

Method: 提出了GRL-Safety基准，对12种代表性方法（涵盖拓扑嵌入、监督GNN、自监督图模型和图基础模型）在25个图数据集上进行标准化评估。评估覆盖五个安全轴：损坏鲁棒性、分布外泛化、类别不平衡、公平性和可解释性，采用分轴和分子条件报告而非单一聚合分数。

Result: 研究发现三个跨轴洞察：1）安全行为由表示设计与受压图因素的交互决定，而非仅由方法家族决定；2）基础时代方法展现轴特异性优势，而非广泛的安全主导；3）即使最佳方法在某些部署场景下仍面临困难，揭示出需要新的鲁棒性、适应或训练目标。

Conclusion: GRL-Safety基准揭示了图表示学习方法在安全维度的能力和局限，强调了需要超越模型选择的新研究来填补能力缺口，为未来研究提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) has evolved from topology-only graph embeddings to task-specific supervised GNNs, and more recently to reusable representations and graph foundation models (GFMs). However, existing evaluations mainly measure clean transfer, adaptation, and task coverage. It remains unclear whether GRL methods stay reliable when deployment stresses affect graph signals, graph contexts, label support, structural groups, or predictive evidence. We introduce GRL-Safety, a multi-axis safety evaluation benchmark for GRL. GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.

</details>


### [219] [PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06582)
*Adhiraj Banerjee,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PairAlign框架，通过序列级自对齐实现紧凑音频标记化，将标记化视为条件序列生成任务，显著减少了存档标记数量并保持编辑距离检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频标记器依赖量化、聚类或编解码器重建进行局部标记分配，难以优化序列一致性、紧凑性、长度控制、终止和编辑相似性。音频领域缺乏类似语言领域tokens的自然接口，需要学习能够支持比较、记忆、检索和推理操作的离散符号结构。

Method: 将标记化建模为条件序列生成：编码器将语音映射为连续条件，自回归解码器从BOS生成标记，学习标记身份、顺序、长度和EOS位置。采用EMA教师目标、交叉配对教师强制、前缀损坏、似然对比和长度控制技术，使用内容保留视图对进行训练，使每个视图的序列在另一视图表示下概率最大化，同时使用不相关样本提供竞争序列，避免多对一坍缩。

Result: 在3秒语音上学习了紧凑、非退化的序列，词汇使用广泛且跨视图一致性强；在TIMIT检索任务中，存档标记数量减少55%同时保持编辑距离搜索；连续扫描探测显示比稠密几何标记器局部重叠更低，但长度控制更强，在100ms偏移下编辑轨迹有界。

Conclusion: PairAlign作为序列符号预测学习器，类似JEPA风格目标，从一个视图预测抽象目标为可学习变长符号序列而非连续潜在表示，实现了音频的紧凑符号化表示，为下游比较、记忆、检索和推理任务提供了有效接口。

Abstract: Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly.
  We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse.
  PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control.
  On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency. On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.

</details>


### [220] [Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06585)
*Vinit Ranjan,Jisun Park,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布鲁棒的一阶优化算法超参数学习方法，通过最小化Wasserstein分布鲁棒的性能估计问题，在经典L2O和最坏情况最优算法设计之间取得平衡，在保持可证明鲁棒性的同时实现了优异的实际性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一阶优化算法的超参数学习方法要么专注于平均性能（L2O），要么专注于最坏情况性能（PEP），缺乏在这两种极端之间取得平衡并兼具泛化能力和鲁棒性保证的统一框架。

Method: 基于给定的问题实例数据集，将性能估计问题（PEP）扩展为Wasserstein分布鲁棒形式，通过随机梯度下降求解该问题，在每一步中对内部半定规划问题进行微分以优化步长等算法参数。

Result: 理论方面证明了学习算法的真实风险不超过样本内L2O最优值加上随样本量减小的松弛项，且不会比最坏情况PEP界限更差；实验方面在无约束二次最小化、LASSO和线性规划基准测试中，学习得到的算法在保持可证明鲁棒性的同时，显著优于最坏情况最优和普通L2O基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法成功统一了L2O和最坏情况最优算法设计两个极端，通过调节鲁棒半径可以在平均性能和最坏情况性能之间进行权衡，为学习优化算法提供了一个兼具泛化能力和鲁棒性保证的框架。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.

</details>


### [221] [Towards Metric-Faithful Neural Graph Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06588)
*Jyotirmaya Shivottam,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将双Lipschitz编码器几何特性作为设计原则，用于改进神经图匹配中的图编辑距离（GED）估计。通过理论框架连接编码器几何与估计质量，并使用FSW-GNN作为即插即用替代，在多个基准上显著提升了GED预测与排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图编辑距离（GED）是衡量图结构相似性的基础但NP难的度量。现有神经图匹配架构通过GNN编码器结合回归头或对齐模块来近似GED，但编码器几何特性在神经GED估计中的作用尚未被深入理解。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 建立了理论框架，将编码器几何特性与两类神经GED估计器（图相似性预测器和对齐方法）的估计质量联系起来。证明了在固定图集合上，图级双Lipschitz编码器能产生受控的GED代理并提高排序稳定性；对于基于匹配的估计器，节点级双Lipschitz几何特性可传播到编码器诱导的对齐代价。采用FSW-GNN（一种双Lipschitz、WL等价编码器）作为代表性神经GED架构中的即插即用替代组件。

Result: 在代表性基线和基准数据集上，几何感知变体显著改进了GED预测和排序指标。未训练编码器的忠实度案例研究、消融实验和迁移实验支持了这些增益源于改进的表示几何特性的观点。

Conclusion: 编码器几何特性应作为神经图匹配的有用设计原则。双Lipschitz编码器能够提供理论保证并带来实际性能提升，为未来神经图匹配架构设计提供了新的理论指导方向。

Abstract: Graph Edit Distance (GED) is a fundamental, albeit NP-hard, metric for structural graph similarity. Recent neural graph matching architectures approximate GED by first encoding graphs with a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and then applying either a graph-level regression head or a matching-based alignment module. Despite substantial architectural progress, the role of encoder geometry in neural GED estimation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that connects encoder geometry to GED estimation quality for two broad classes of neural GED estimators: graph similarity predictors and alignment-based methods. On fixed graph collections, where the doubly-stochastic metric $d_{\mathrm{DS}}$ is comparable to GED, we show that graph-level bi-Lipschitz encoders yield controlled GED surrogates and improved ranking stability; for matching-based estimators, node-level bi-Lipschitz geometry propagates to encoder-induced alignment costs and the resulting optimized alignment objective. We instantiate this perspective using FSW-GNN, a bi-Lipschitz WL-equivalent encoder, as a drop-in replacement in representative neural GED architectures. Across representative baselines and benchmark datasets, the resulting geometry-aware variants significantly improve GED prediction and ranking metrics. A faithfulness case study of untrained encoders, together with ablations and transfer experiments, supports the view that these gains arise from improved representation geometry, positioning encoder geometry as a useful design principle for neural graph matching.

</details>


### [222] [BRICKS: Compositional Neural Markov Kernels for Zero-Shot Radiation-Matter Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06591)
*Richard Hildebrandt,Evangelos Kourlitis,Baran Hashemi,Manuel Bünstorf,Thierry Meyer,Nikola Boskov,Michael Kagan,Dan Rosenbaum,Sanmay Ganguly,Lukas Heinrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于混合离散-连续Transformer和黎曼流匹配的组合式神经替代模型，用于辐射-物质相互作用模拟，在GPU上实现显著加速，并支持零样本泛化和可微推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射-物质相互作用模拟是粒子物理、核工程、空间工程和医学物理等领域的关键任务。传统的机械化模拟器计算成本高、不可微且难以提供可处理的似然，限制了其在下游应用中的效率。作者旨在利用粒子相互作用的局域性和马尔可夫性质，构建一种高效、可微、可组合的神经替代模型。

Method: 基于粒子相互作用的局域性和马尔可夫性质，构建了“下一粒子预测”内核。该模型采用混合离散-连续Transformer，基于乘积流形上的黎曼流匹配进行训练。模型输入为入射粒子和材料体积信息，输出为变长的粒子集合及辐射副产物。该内核可组合以模拟未见的大规模材料分布。

Result: 在GPU上相比CPU运行的机械化模拟实现了显著的计算加速。在内核级别评估中表现出良好的预测性能，并在多轮自回归推演中保持了预测稳定性。作者还发布了一个包含2000万事件的辐射-物质相互作用数据集。

Conclusion: 该方法为辐射-物质相互作用提供了一种高效、可微的神经替代方案，具备零样本组合泛化能力，在保证预测稳定性的同时大幅提升了计算效率，为相关领域的模拟研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce a new strategy for compositional neural surrogates for radiation-matter interactions, a key task spanning domains from particle physics through nuclear and space engineering to medical physics. Exploiting the locality and the Markov nature of particle interactions, we create a \emph{next-particle prediction} kernel using hybrid discrete-continuous transformer models based on Riemannian Flow Matching on product manifolds. The model generates variable-sized typed sets of particles and radiation side effects that are the result of the interaction of an incident particle with a material volume. The resulting kernel can be composed to simulate unseen large-scale material distributions in a zero-shot manner. Unlike mechanistic simulators, our model is designed to be differentiable, provides tractable likelihoods for future downstream applications. A significant computational speed-up on GPU compared to CPU-bound mechanistic simulation is observed for single-kernel execution. We evaluate the model at the kernel level and demonstrate predictive stability over multi-round autoregressive rollouts. We additionally release a novel 20M-event radiation-matter interaction dataset for further research.

</details>


### [223] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从泛函分析角度证明了带L2正则化的Transformer损失函数满足Villani能量准则，推导出了Log-Sobolev和Poincaré常数，建立了权重衰减与SGD收敛性及泛化界的理论联系，并在GPT-Neo-125M上验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减在大语言模型中被广泛用作正则化手段，但其在塑造Transformer损失曲面中的确切作用在理论上尚未得到充分探索，缺乏严格的数学刻画。

Method: 证明正则化损失函数满足Villani粗糙能量函数准则（无限可微、至少二次增长、高斯可积尾部、满足微分增长条件）；推导显式Log-Sobolev和Poincaré常数；提出可扩展的Villani诊断量并使用Hutchinson迹探针在超大模型上高效估计。

Result: 理论上：建立了正则化强度λ和模型维度d与有限时间收敛保证的联系，推导出PAC-Bayes泛化界随λ增大而收紧。实验上：在GPT-Neo-125M（Penn Treebank和WikiText-103）上验证了Ψ_s的二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀以及符合Log-Sobolev分析的指数收敛行为。

Conclusion: 权重衰减不仅经验上改善泛化，更从数学上确立了深度学习中快速Langevin混合和理论驱动的曲率感知优化所需的条件，为大语言模型的优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [224] [How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06605)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAPRO框架，通过动态预算分配实现对多轮LLM交互中关键事件（如越狱攻击、任务完成）发生时间的可靠预测下界，在有限计算资源下提供更紧的无分布覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估多轮对话中LLM性能（如越狱攻击、智能体任务完成）需大量交互迭代，计算成本极高；关键事件往往在多次交互后才出现且可能很罕见，在有限预算下难以观测。现有共形生存框架采用静态预算分配，在多轮场景中效率低下。

Method: 提出动态分配投影优化方法(DAPRO)：1) 设计理论上有效的动态预算分配框架；2) 无需先前方法中事件时间与删失时间条件独立的假设；3) 推导新型覆盖界，其尺度与平均删失权重的平方根而非最坏情况权重相关，获得更紧的理论保证。

Result: 在Llama 3.1和Qwen 2.5等模型上，针对智能体任务成功、对抗性越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉等场景进行实验：1) DAPRO在满足预算约束的同时，覆盖率更接近标称水平；2) 方差低于静态基线方法；3) 可用于获得无偏、低方差的整体评估指标估计（如越狱率）。

Conclusion: DAPRO是首个针对多轮LLM交互的动态预算分配框架，提供了严格的理论保证和覆盖界改进，在有限计算资源下实现了更高效、更准确的LLM性能评估，为LLM安全性评估和性能预测提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved.
  Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions.
  We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches.
  A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources.
  Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.

</details>


### [225] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上揭示了Softmax Transformer如何实现上下文学习：证明了多层Transformer可以精确执行上下文逻辑回归，每层对应一步归一化梯度下降；同时发现这类模型可通过训练单层自注意力并循环应用来构造，并提供了收敛性与泛化性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer展现出了强大的上下文学习（ICL）能力，普遍认为这是由于其能在上下文中隐式执行某些算法。然而，对于Transformer（特别是使用Softmax注意力的模型）如何具体实现ICL的机制，理论理解仍然有限。本文旨在探究Softmax Transformer在线性分类数据上如何执行上下文学习，以深化对ICL机制的理论认知。

Method: 作者首先构造了一类多层Transformer，证明其能执行上下文逻辑回归，且每一层精确执行一步基于上下文损失的归一化梯度下降。随后，展示了该构造可通过两步获得：(i) 在单步梯度下降的监督下训练单个自注意力层；(ii) 循环应用该训练好的层以得到循环模型（looped model）。同时，作者为单层训练的收敛性以及循环模型的分布外泛化性提供了理论保证。

Result: 研究发现，所构造的多层Transformer能够有效地在上下文中执行逻辑回归；通过训练单层并循环使用的方式可以复现该结构，并且理论证明了单层自注意力层的训练收敛性以及所得循环模型的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了对ICL机制的理论理解，明确展示了Softmax Transformer如何通过模拟梯度下降算法有效充当上下文学习器，为理解Transformer的内在工作机制提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [226] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSAE，一种具有动态Top-K选择机制的稀疏自编码器，能够根据输入复杂度自适应调整激活特征数量，解决了传统TopK SAE固定稀疏度无法适应数据流形局部内在维度变化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TopK SAE架构采用固定稀疏度K，对所有输入强制相同数量的激活特征，忽略了现实数据流形具有变化的局部内在维度。简单输入可能只需少量特征，而复杂输入需要更多特征表达；固定K会导致简单情况引入噪声或复杂情况遗漏重要结构。

Method: 提出SoftSAE，引入可微分Soft Top-K算子学习输入依赖的稀疏度k。该方法根据每个输入的复杂度动态调整激活特征数量，使表示更好匹配数据结构，解释长度反映输入信息量。

Result: 实验结果证实SoftSAE不仅能发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择正确数量的特征，有效验证了动态稀疏度的优越性。

Conclusion: SoftSAE成功实现了基于输入复杂度的自适应特征选择，为稀疏自编码器在可解释性研究中的应用提供了更灵活、更匹配数据本质的解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [227] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM中“注意力汇”现象的深层结构成因：自注意力值聚合过程中的方差差异被FFN层的超神经元激活剧烈放大，导致首token表示的维度失衡。作者通过干预实验验证了该因果链，并提出了头级RMSNorm架构改进，通过恢复位置间的统计均衡显著加速训练收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管“注意力汇”现象在大型语言模型中普遍存在，即初始token不成比例地占据注意力分数，但其结构起源仍然难以捉摸。本文旨在为这一现象提供机制性解释，填补对其成因理解的空白。

Method: 1. 追溯注意力汇的根源至自注意力内在的值聚合过程，该过程引入了系统性的方差差异。
2. 揭示FFN层中超神经元的激活（特别是通道稀疏的下投影）会急剧放大这种差异，导致首token表示的维度失衡，从而迫使模型形成注意力汇作为结构锚点。
3. 通过两组受控干预实验验证该因果链：(i) 通过注意力掩码修改隔离聚合效应；(ii) 放大目标token表示的方差。两种干预均能在任意位置复现注意力汇。
4. 提出架构改进方案——头级RMSNorm，用于在预训练过程中稳定值聚合输出。

Result: 实验证明：通过修改注意力掩码或调整目标token方差，可以在任意位置人为诱导注意力汇的形成。采用头级RMSNorm恢复位置间的统计均衡后，模型预训练的收敛速度得到显著提升，验证了该机制解释的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功提供了注意力汇现象的机制性解释，建立了从值聚合方差差异到FFN超神经元激活再到注意力汇形成的完整因果链。基于此理解提出的架构改进方案能够系统性地控制汇的形成，并为未来LLM架构设计提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [228] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在线贝叶斯校准框架（BRPC），解决了传统方法中参数与偏差混淆的问题，并能有效处理系统渐变和突变情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的贝叶斯校准方法存在参数-偏差混淆问题，且通常假设数据生成过程是平稳的，无法适应现代数字孪生应用中系统随时间演变、出现渐变漂移和突变的情况。虽然数据同化方法支持序贯更新，但通常不显式建模系统偏差，且在突变情况下效果较差。

Method: 提出贝叶斯递归投影校准（BRPC）框架，将投影校准扩展到在线设置：通过分离用于校准参数的无偏差粒子更新和用于偏差的条件高斯过程更新，保持可识别性并实现偏差感知的自适应。对于突变情况，集成重启机制检测机制转换并重置校准过程。

Result: 在合成数据和工厂仿真基准测试中，BRPC在渐变情况下提高了校准精度；带有重启机制的BRPC在突变情况下相比滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线方法，进一步提升了鲁棒性和预测性能。

Conclusion: BRPC框架成功解决了在线贝叶斯校准中的关键挑战，为数字孪生应用中的动态系统校准提供了有效解决方案，理论上证明了在渐变下的跟踪性能和重启机制的虚警与检测行为。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [229] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为SignSGD和Muon等符号优化算法建立了理论基础，证明在稀疏噪声和特定问题几何条件下，SignSGD相比SGD可将复杂度降低d倍，并在GPT-2预训练中验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号优化算法（如SignSGD和Muon）在训练大型基础模型时表现出色，但缺乏关于其何时以及为何优于SGD的理论解释。核心障碍在于标准设置下SGD具有极小极大最优性，这从根本上排除了符号方法在标准条件下获得复杂度优势的可能性。

Method: 作者采用了一种独特的分析框架：使用$\ell_1$-范数平稳性、$\ell_\infty$-平滑性和可分离噪声模型来捕捉符号更新的坐标特性。通过推导SignSGD的匹配上下界，并将其上界与SGD的下界进行比较，量化性能差距。同时将框架扩展到矩阵域，分析Muon优化器。

Result: 在稀疏噪声条件下，SignSGD相比SGD将复杂度降低了d倍（d为问题维度）。Muon优化器在矩阵域中保持了这种最优的维度扩展性。在124M参数GPT-2模型的预训练中，理论预测的SignSGD更快收敛速度得到了实际验证。

Conclusion: 符号优化方法的优势源于坐标级的问题几何特性（$\ell_1$-平稳性、$\ell_\infty$-平滑性和可分离噪声），而非标准的$\ell_2$-范数设置。这为理解和设计更优的优化器提供了新的理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [230] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical GANs for the Generation of Adversarial Network Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06629)
*Prateek Paudel,Nitin Jha,Abhishek Parakh,Mahadevan Subramaniam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合量子-经典生成对抗网络（QC-GAN）框架，利用变分量子生成器生成能够绕过入侵检测系统（IDS）的对抗性网络流量，验证了量子机器学习在网络安全攻击领域的潜在威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典生成对抗网络（GAN）在生成针对入侵检测系统的对抗性网络流量时存在显著缺陷，包括对大量高维数据集的依赖、模式崩溃问题以及高昂的计算开销，这限制了其攻击效果与应用效率。

Method: 提出混合量子-经典GAN（QC-GAN）框架。核心在于使用变分量子生成器替代经典生成器，将潜变量编码为量子态以获得更具表达力的潜在表示并降低计算开销。判别器采用经典模型，使用UNSW-NB15数据集进行对抗训练。在攻击模型假设下，攻击者拥有有限量子计算能力，防御方使用经典IDS（如随机森林和CNN分类器）进行检测。

Result: 生成的合成网络流量成功绕过了随机森林和基于卷积神经网络的经典入侵检测系统的检测。同时，研究还评估了硬件噪声对攻击效果的影响，验证了量子计算优势在生成对抗攻击中的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了量子机器学习可作为生成高级攻击流量的有效手段，能够用于对经典IDS进行压力测试。研究结果强调了开发具备量子弹性的防御系统的必要性与紧迫性，为未来网络安全防御提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Classical generative adversarial networks (GANs) have been applied to generate adversarial network traffic capable of attacking intrusion detection systems, but they suffer from shortcomings such as the need for large amounts of high-dimensional datasets, mode collapse, and high computational overhead. In this work, we propose a hybrid quantum-classical GAN (QC-GAN) framework where a variational quantum generator is used to generate synthetic network traffic flows mimicking malicious traffic using latent representations. Instead of sampling classical noise vectors, we encode the latent vector (the hidden features) as a quantum state, which is the basis for claiming more expressive latent representations and reducing computational overhead. A classical discriminator will be trained on real-world datasets (UNSW-NB15) and the proposed QC-GAN-generated fake network flows. In this configuration, the generator aims to minimize the discriminator's ability to distinguish real from fake traffic, while the discriminator aims to maximize its classification accuracy, in an iterative manner. In our attack model, we assume that the attacker is a state actor with access to limited quantum computing power, whereas the discriminator is chosen to be classical, as will likely be the case for most end users and organizations. We test the generated flows using classical intrusion detection system (IDS) models, such as a random forest classifier and a convolutional neural network-based classifier, for their ability to bypass the detection process. This work aims to highlight the possibilities of quantum machine learning as a means of generating advanced attack flows and stress testing classical IDS. Lastly, we further evaluate how hardware-based noise affects these attacks to offer a new perspective on IDS, highlighting the need for a quantum resilient defense system.

</details>


### [231] [Crafting Reversible SFT Behaviors in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06632)
*Yuping Lin,Pengfei He,Yue Xing,Yingqian Cui,Jiayuan Ding,Subhabrata Mukherjee,Hui Liu,Zhen Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LCDD方法，将SFT引发的行为压缩到稀疏的“载体”子网络中，并通过SFT-Eraser实现在推理时无需修改权重即可选择性地逆转这些行为，验证了稀疏结构对行为的因果必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的行为解释方法（如电路归因）只能事后识别与SFT行为相关的稀疏子网络，但这种相关性并不等同于因果必要性，限制了在推理时选择性地控制SFT引发行为的能力。作者旨在探索是否可以将SFT引发的行为有意识地压缩到一个稀疏且具有机制必要性的子网络中，从而实现推理时的可控性。

Method: 提出了两种方法：(1) 损失约束双重下降（LCDD），通过在显式效用预算下联合优化路由掩码和模型权重，构建稀疏载体子网络；(2) SFT-Eraser，一种通过在提取的载体通道上进行激活匹配优化的软提示，用于逆转SFT引发的行为。

Result: 在多个模型家族的安全性、固定响应和风格行为测试中，LCDD生成的稀疏载体在保留目标行为的同时，能够通过SFT-Eraser触发实现强逆转。消融实验证明稀疏结构是逆转的关键前提：相同的触发器优化在标准SFT模型上失败，证实了结构而非触发器设计是关键因素。

Conclusion: 实验结果直接证明了学习到的载体对行为具有因果必要性，为在部署模型中系统性地定位和选择性抑制SFT引发的行为提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) induces new behaviors in large language models, yet imposes no structural constraint on how these behaviors are distributed within the model. Existing behavior interpretation methods, such as circuit attribution approaches, identify sparse subnetworks correlated with SFT-induced behaviors post-hoc. However, such correlations do not imply *causal necessity*, limiting the ability to selectively control SFT-induced behaviors at inference time. We pursue an alternative by asking: can an SFT-induced behavior be deliberately compressed into a sparse, mechanistically necessary subnetwork, termed a *carrier*, while remaining controllable at inference time without weight modification? We propose (a) **Loss-Constrained Dual Descent (LCDD)**, which constructs such carriers by jointly optimizing routing masks and model weights under an explicit utility budget, and (b) **SFT-Eraser**, a soft prompt optimized via activation matching on extracted carrier channels, to reverse the SFT-induced behavior. Across safety, fixed-response, and style behaviors on multiple model families, LCDD yields sparse carriers that preserve target behaviors while enabling strong reversion when triggered by SFT-Eraser. Ablations further establish that the sparse structure is the key precondition for reversal: the same trigger optimization fails on standard SFT models, confirming that structure rather than trigger design is the operative factor. These results provide direct evidence that the learned carriers are causally necessary for the behaviors, pointing to a new direction for systematically localizing and selectively suppressing SFT-induced behaviors in deployed models.

</details>


### [232] [Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06640)
*Ronaldo Canizales,Divya Gopinath,Corina Păsăreanu,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概念的反事实和对比解释方法，能够识别导致模型预测结果的最小高层概念集合，在个体图像和群体行为层面都提供了有效的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念解释方法要么无法建立概念与模型预测之间的因果关系，要么表达能力有限只能推断单个概念的因果解释；同时，形式化的反事实和对比解释方法虽然能计算因果相关的最小输入特征集，但只考虑像素级别的低层特征。因此需要一种能融合两者优势的方法。

Method: 提出基于概念的反事实和对比解释方法，捕捉因果相关的最小高层概念集合；开发了一系列算法来枚举所有最小解释，并使用概念擦除程序来建立因果关系；通过适当聚合解释，不仅能理解个体图像的模型预测，还能理解模型表现出用户指定共同行为的图像集合。

Result: 在多个模型、数据集和行为上评估了该方法，证明了其在计算有帮助的、用户友好的解释方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功融合了概念解释和形式化因果解释的优势，能够提供高层概念级别的因果解释，既适用于个体样本也适用于群体行为的分析，为深度神经网络的可解释性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: *Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.

</details>


### [233] [Edge-specific signal propagation on mature chromophore-region 3D mechanism graphs for fluorescent protein quantum-yield prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06644)
*Yuchen Xiong,Swee Keong Yeap,Steven Aw Yoong Kit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种以发色团为中心的机制图算法用于荧光蛋白量子产率预测，在531个蛋白质基准测试中达到最佳性能（R=0.772），并揭示了不同波段蛋白的特异性机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质语言模型和发射带平均值仅能捕捉全局趋势，无法建模局部物理信号如何作用于特定发色团区域，而量子产率实际上由成熟发色团及其三维微环境共同决定。

Method: 将PDB结构转换为带类型的3D残基图，配准到成熟发色团状态，划分为酚盐、桥接和咪唑啉酮三个区域，通过通道-信号-区域传播变换生成121个富集特征（去除身份捷径后使用52个非身份特征），进行波段特定的ExtraTrees回归。

Result: 在531个蛋白质基准测试中达到最佳随机交叉验证性能（R=0.772±0.008，MAE=0.131±0.002），优于Band均值（R=0.632）、ESM-C（R=0.734）和SaProt（R=0.731）；在亮蛋白筛选中排名第一（Bright P@5=0.704）；在同源性控制下，远程同源组（<50%相似性）优势最明显（R=0.697 vs 0.633, 0.575, 0.408）；筛选出的稳定特征恢复了波段特异性机制：GFP类蛋白中的芳香堆积和夹钳不对称、红光蛋白中的电荷/夹钳平衡、远红光蛋白中的柔性风险/大体积接触特征。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了量子产率预测和机制解释的统一，每个特征都编码了接触通道、种子信号和目标发色团区域，使解释成为内在属性而非事后分析，为荧光蛋白工程提供了可解释的预测工具。

Abstract: Fluorescent protein quantum yield (QY) is governed by the mature chromophore and its three-dimensional microenvironment rather than sequence identity alone. Protein language models and emission-band averages capture global trends, but do not model how local physical signals act on specific chromophore regions.
  We present a chromophore-centred mechanism graph algorithm for QY prediction. Each PDB structure is converted into a typed 3D residue graph, registered to a mature-CRO state, partitioned into phenolate, bridge and imidazolinone regions, and transformed by channel-signal-region propagation. The representation contains 121 enrichment features; after removing identity shortcuts, 52 non-identity features are used for band-specific ExtraTrees regression. Because each feature encodes a contact channel, seed signal and target CRO region, interpretation is intrinsic rather than post hoc. On a 531-protein benchmark, the method achieved the best random-CV performance among model-based baselines (R = 0.772 +/- 0.008, MAE = 0.131 +/- 0.002), exceeding Band mean (R = 0.632), ESM-C (R = 0.734) and SaProt (R = 0.731), and ranked first in bright screening (Bright P@5 = 0.704). Under homology control, the advantage was clearest in the remote bucket (<50% similarity; R = 0.697 versus 0.633, 0.575 and 0.408), with the strongest overall bright/dark Top-K screening. Stable selected features recovered band-specific mechanisms: aromatic packing and clamp asymmetry in GFP-like proteins, charge/clamp balance in Red proteins, and flexibility-risk/bulky-contact features in Far-red proteins.
  Source code, feature tables and evaluation scripts are available from the first author upon request. Contact: yuchenak05@gmail.com

</details>


### [234] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将原本仅适用于二分类任务的Venn-Abers预测器扩展到了无界回归场景，并结合了共形预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers预测器虽然具有概率预测有效性等优良性质，但其主要局限在于仅适用于二分类任务（近期虽有扩展至有界回归的研究），作者旨在将其推广至无界回归这一更普遍的场景。

Method: 通过引入共形预测的元素，将Venn-Abers预测器的框架从二分类推广至无界回归任务，构建了Venn-Abers回归器。

Result: 仿真与实证研究表明，由Venn-Abers回归器推导出的点回归器在较大量训练集上，相比标准回归器在预测效率上有一定程度的提升。

Conclusion: 成功将Venn-Abers预测器推广至无界回归问题，验证了其在保持有效性的同时能提升大样本情况下的预测效率。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [235] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了无基准比较安全评分方法，用于在缺乏标注数据时比较语言模型的安全性。通过SimpleAudit工具验证了方法有效性（AUROC 0.89-1.00），并在挪威公共部门采购案例中实际应用，强调需完整报告评分、审计员和判断器等全部信息而非简单排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际部署场景需要在相关语言、领域或监管框架的标注基准数据存在之前，就必须对候选语言模型进行安全性比较。这一现实需求推动了无基准比较安全评分方法的研究。

Method: 形式化定义了无基准比较安全评分设置，指定了基于场景审计可解释为部署证据的条件。构建了工具效度链来替代真实标签：对安全-消融对比的响应性、目标驱动方差对审计员和判断器伪迹的主导性、以及重运行的稳定性。开发了SimpleAudit本地优先评分工具，并在挪威安全包上进行验证。

Result: 在SimpleAudit工具上验证，安全目标与消融目标分离的AUROC值达0.89-1.00；目标身份是主导方差成分（η²≈0.52）；严重性分布约10次重运行后趋于稳定。Petri工具也适用该效度链。挪威公共部门采购案例比较Borealis和Gemma 3模型时，发现更安全的模型取决于场景类别和风险度量。

Conclusion: 评分仅在固定的场景包、评分标准、审计员、判断器、采样配置和重运行预算条件下有效。评分、匹配差值、临界比率、不确定性以及所用的审计员和判断器必须一并报告，而不能简化为单一排名。实质性差异源于效度链上游——即声明-契约强制执行和部署适配。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [236] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在全参数微调阶段，使用与预训练相同的优化器能实现更好的学习-遗忘权衡，这种现象被称为“优化器-模型一致性”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在预训练和微调阶段都依赖优化器，但不同优化器对模型知识保留的影响尚不明确。作者旨在探究优化器选择如何影响模型在微调时的性能与遗忘权衡。

Method: 通过受控实验和理论分析，研究优化器对激活的正则化效应及其对预训练检查点周围损失景观的影响；比较Muon和AdamW在预训练和SFT阶段的表现；设计合成语言建模实验以验证Muon在推理任务上的表现差异。

Result: 使用与预训练相同的优化器进行全参数微调，相比其他优化器（甚至包括LoRA），能在达到相同或更好性能的同时减少对预训练知识的遗忘。Muon在推理任务微调上表现较差，可能源于其强记忆倾向，在小数据量下会妨碍模式习得。

Conclusion: 优化器会塑造模型并对激活产生正则化效应，微调时的权重更新应遵循特定结构以降低预训练知识的遗忘，使用相同的优化器是实现这一目标的有效途径。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [237] [Why Global LLM Leaderboards Are Misleading: Small Portfolios for Heterogeneous Supervised ML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06656)
*Jai Moondra,Ayela Chughtai,Bhargavi Lanka,Swati Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM排行榜中全局Bradley-Terry排名的误导性，发现语言等异质性导致排名失效。提出了(λ, ν)-投资组合框架，用少量模型覆盖大部分用户偏好，在Arena数据上5个排名即可覆盖96%的投票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM排行榜基于成对人类反馈的全局排名存在根本性问题。作者分析了Arena中116种语言、52个LLM的约89K次比较，发现全局Bradley-Terry排名具有误导性：近2/3的决定性投票相互抵消，前50名模型在统计上无法区分（成对获胜概率最高仅0.53）。问题根源在于跨语言、任务和时间的强烈结构性意见异质性，特别是语言因素起着关键作用。按语言分组后，ELO分数的分布增加两个数量级，排名一致性大幅提高。

Method: 作者提出(λ, ν)-投资组合框架来解决异质性问题。该框架寻找一小部分模型集合，在预测误差不超过λ的情况下"覆盖"至少ν比例的用户。作者将其建模为集合覆盖问题的变体，并利用底层集合系统的VC维提供理论保证。算法在Arena数据上识别出5个不同的BT排名，以及6个LLM的投资组合。此外，作者在COMPAS数据集的公平性分类问题上应用该方法，使用公平性正则化分类模型的集成来构建投资组合。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 在Arena数据上，算法仅用5个不同的BT排名就以适度的λ覆盖了超过96%的投票，而全局排名仅覆盖21%；(2) 6个LLM的投资组合覆盖的投票数是全局排名前6名LLM的两倍；(3) 在COMPAS数据集上，投资组合方法能够检测数据中的盲点，这对政策制定者具有独立价值。全局噪声实际上是多个连贯但相互冲突的子群体的混合。

Conclusion: 全局LLM排名掩盖了用户群体的异质性偏好，投资组合方法提供了更有效的解决方案。该框架不仅在LLM评估中有用，还可用于检测数据中的盲点，为政策制定提供支持。研究表明，表面上的全局噪声实际上是多个连贯子群体的混合，需要采用差异化的评估方法。

Abstract: Ranking LLMs via pairwise human feedback underpins current leaderboards for open-ended tasks, such as creative writing and problem-solving. We analyze ~89K comparisons in 116 languages from 52 LLMs from Arena, and show that the best-fit global Bradley-Terry (BT) ranking is misleading. Nearly 2/3 of the decisive votes cancel out, and even the top 50 models according to the global BT ranking are statistically indistinguishable (pairwise win probabilities are at most 0.53 within the top 50 models). We trace this failure to strong, structured heterogeneity of opinions across language, task, and time. Moreover, we find an important characteristic - *language* plays a key role. Grouping by language (and families) increases the agreement of votes massively, resulting in two orders of magnitude higher spread in the ELO scores (i.e., very consistent rankings). What appears as global noise is in fact a mixture of coherent but conflicting subpopulations.
  To address such heterogeneity in supervised machine learning, we introduce the framework of $(λ, ν)$-portfolios, which are small sets of models that achieve a prediction error at most $λ$, "covering" at least a $ν$ fraction of users. We formulate this as a variant of the set cover problem and provide guarantees using the VC dimension of the underlying set system. On the Arena data, our algorithms recover just 5 distinct BT rankings that cover over 96% of votes at a modest $λ$, compared to the 21% coverage by the global ranking. We also provide a portfolio of 6 LLMs that cover twice as many votes as the top-6 LLMs from a global ranking. We further construct portfolios for a classification problem on the COMPAS dataset using an ensemble of fairness-regularized classification models and show that these portfolios can be used to detect blind spots in the data, which might be of independent interest to policymakers.

</details>


### [238] [Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06660)
*Yuhang Lai,Jiazhan Feng,Yee Whye Teh,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了VHG框架，通过在传统的设置者-求解者博弈中引入独立的验证者，解决了大语言模型生成有效、高难度新问题时的reward hacking问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在生成科学和数学问题方面存在困难，无法有效产生有效、具有挑战性和新颖性的问题，这对于推进LLM训练和实现自主科学研究至关重要。现有方法要么依赖昂贵的人类专家参与，要么采用简单的自博弈范式，常因reward hacking导致生成无效问题。

Method: 提出了VHG（Verifier-Enhanced Hard Problem Generation）框架，基于三方自博弈机制，在传统的设置者-求解者二元结构中引入独立的验证者。设计约束设置者的奖励由问题有效性（由验证者评估）和问题难度（由求解者评估）共同决定。实例化了两种验证者变体：Hard symbolic verifier和Soft LLM-based verifier。

Result: 在不定积分任务和一般数学推理任务上进行的实验表明，VHG框架在性能上显著优于所有基线方法，能够有效生成有效且具有挑战性的数学问题。

Conclusion: 通过引入独立验证者的三方自博弈机制，VHG成功解决了现有问题生成方法中的关键缺陷，为大语言模型训练和自主科学研究提供了有效的问题生成解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.

</details>


### [239] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UniPool架构，通过全局共享专家池替代传统的层级独立专家分配，在减少专家参数至原有的41.6%-66.7%的同时，实现性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE架构采用每层独立专家集的刚性分配规则，导致专家参数随深度线性增长。作者通过路由探针实验发现，更深层路由器的冗余性很高（替换为随机路由仅导致1.0-1.6点的准确率下降），表明层级专家分配存在资源浪费。

Method: 提出UniPool架构：(1) 用单一全局共享专家池替代层级专家所有权，各层通过独立路由器访问；(2) 引入池级辅助损失实现全局专家利用平衡；(3) 采用NormRouter提供稀疏且尺度稳定的路由。

Result: 在5种LLaMA规模（182M-978M参数）的实验中，UniPool相比基线MoE降低验证损失达0.0386。仅使用41.6%-66.7%专家参数的精简版UniPool即可匹配或超越标准MoE性能，实现专家参数的次线性深度扩展。

Conclusion: 共享专家池设计打破了专家参数必须随深度线性增长的限制，实现了更高效灵活的专家容量分配，且该优势可与细粒度专家分解方法叠加。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Active Learning for Communication Structure Optimization in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05703)
*Huchen Yang,Xinghao Dong,Dan Negrut,Jin-Long Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 针对LLM多智能体系统通信结构优化中随机采样任务存在的问题，提出了一个基于集成和信息论的任务选择框架，通过主动识别最有价值的任务来提升优化的稳定性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM多智能体系统通信结构优化方法通常依赖随机采样的训练任务，但任务在难度和领域上存在显著差异，导致其对通信结构更新的信息量不等。在有限的训练预算下，这种方法往往不稳定且对训练集高度敏感。

Method: 提出了一个基于集成的信息论任务选择框架。该方法通过候选任务对图参数分布的改变程度来估计任务信息量，并使用集成卡尔曼反演作为相应贝叶斯更新的高效无导数近似。为提高可扩展性，通过基于嵌入的代表性选择构建紧凑的候选池，并结合代理模型和批量Thompson采样。

Result: 在良性环境和存在智能体攻击的环境下均验证了方法的有效性，证明了该方法能够在受限的计算预算下有效优化通信结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的信息论任务选择框架能够有效解决随机采样带来的不稳定性问题，通过主动选择高信息量任务，在有限预算下实现了更优的通信结构优化，为LLM多智能体系统的结构优化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Optimizing the communication structure of large language model based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) has been shown to improve downstream performance and reduce token usage. Existing methods typically rely on randomly sampled training tasks. However, tasks may differ substantially in difficulty and domain, and thus they are not equally informative for updating communication structure, making optimization under limited training budgets often unstable and highly sensitive to the particular training set. To actively identify the most valuable tasks for communication-structure optimization, we propose an ensemble-based information-theoretic task selection framework. The proposed method estimates task informativeness by how much a candidate task changes the distribution over graph parameters, using ensemble Kalman inversion as an efficient and derivative-free approximation of the corresponding Bayesian update. The resulting estimator is especially suitable for black-box and noisy multi-agent systems. To enhance scalability, we construct a compact candidate pool through embedding-based representative selection and combine the informative selection with surrogate modeling and batch Thompson sampling. We validate our method in both benign settings and settings with agent attacks, demonstrating its effectiveness for communication-structure optimization under constrained computational budgets.

</details>


### [241] [Auto Research with Specialist Agents Develops Effective and Non-Trivial Training Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05724)
*Jingjie Ning,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Hao Kang,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由外部评估驱动的自动化研究闭环系统，通过谱系反馈让智能体自主完成代码编写、实验提交和模型优化，在三项任务上无需人工干预即取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化研究方法往往生成孤立的论文或模型，缺乏持续迭代和可审计性；研究过程需要人类干预来处理失败案例，未能形成闭环反馈，限制了自动化研究的效率和自主性。

Method: 构建一个封闭的实证研究闭环，每次提交试验包含假设、可执行代码编辑、评估结果和反馈；使用专用智能体划分配方表面并跨试验共享测量谱系；通过谱系反馈机制，智能体将评估器结果（包括崩溃、预算超限、规模失败、准确率未达标等）转化为后续程序级别的配方编辑，而非一次性建议。

Result: 在1,197次主运行试验和600次参数高尔夫控制试验中，无需人工选择提案、编辑配方、覆盖分数或修复失败试验；三项主运行任务中：参数高尔夫验证bpb降低0.81%，NanoChat-D12 CORE提升38.7%，CIFAR-10 Airbench96运行时间减少4.59%；系统自主完成代码编写、实验提交、反馈吸收、技术组合与配方改进。

Conclusion: 谱系反馈机制使智能体能够将各类评估结果转化为持续优化，实现了真正自主的自动化研究闭环；系统生成的可审计轨迹包含提案、代码差异、实验、分数和失败标签，为自动化研究提供了可追溯性；该方法在多个领域均展现出改进公共起始配方的能力。

Abstract: We study auto research as a closed empirical loop driven by external measurement. Each submitted trial carries a hypothesis, an executable code edit, an evaluator-owned outcome, and feedback that shapes the next proposal. The output is not a generated paper or a single model checkpoint, but an auditable trajectory of proposals, code diffs, experiments, scores, and failure labels. We instantiate this loop with specialist agents that partition recipe surfaces and share measured lineage across trials. The central empirical finding is that lineage feedback lets agents turn evaluator outcomes, including crashes, budget overruns, size failures, and accuracy-gate misses, into later program-level recipe edits rather than one-shot suggestions. Across 1,197 headline-run trials plus 600 Parameter Golf control trials after one-time setup and launch, humans did not choose proposals, edit recipes, override scores, or repair failed trials during the search. In the three headline runs, the same submitted-trial loop reduces Parameter Golf validation bpb by $0.81\%$, raises NanoChat-D12 CORE by $38.7\%$, and reduces CIFAR-10 Airbench96 wallclock by $4.59\%$, with each task measured by its own external evaluator and legality checks. The trace includes a strict architecture-domain audit of 157 headline-run submissions and program rewrites such as a NanoChat attention-kernel path change. Within this scope the loop autonomously writes code, submits experiments, absorbs feedback, applies and combines known techniques inside each environment, and improves public starting recipes.

</details>


### [242] [Multiagent Stochastic Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06056)
*Martin Jonáš,Antonín Kučera,Vojtěch Kůr,Jan Mačák,Vojtěch Řehák*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了多智能体随机最短路径问题（MSSP），分析了其在自主与协调场景下的计算复杂度和策略复杂度，设计了高效的策略合成算法，并进行了实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决多智能体环境下，如何让k个智能体以最短期望时间到达目标状态的问题。现有研究多集中于单智能体或非随机场景，缺乏对多智能体随机环境中策略合成与复杂度分析的系统研究。

Method: 形式化定义MSSP问题，分别研究自主（autonomous）与协调（coordinated）两种设定下的策略类型；通过理论分析给出问题的计算复杂度和策略复杂度上界；设计多项式时间或伪多项式时间的策略合成算法；在不同规模实例上与自然基线进行对比实验。

Result: 在理论层面，给出了MSSP问题在自主和协调设定下的复杂度刻画与策略表示界；在算法层面，提出了可高效求解的策略合成方法；实验结果显示，所提算法在实例规模增大时优于自然基线，具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: MSSP问题在自主与协调设定下均可在合理复杂度内求解，所提算法能有效生成近优策略，为多智能体随机规划提供了理论基础与实用工具。

Abstract: We introduce and study the multi-agent stochastic shortest path (MSSP) problem, in which $k$ agents strive to reach a target state, aiming to minimize the expected time to reach the target by any agent. We analyze the computational and strategy-complexity of the problem in both autonomous and coordinated settings, and we design efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms. The algorithms are experimentally evaluated on instances of increasing size against natural baselines.

</details>


### [243] [Power-Efficiency and Scalability Analysis of Magnetically-Actuated Satellite Swarms via Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06286)
*Yuta Takahashi,Seang Shim,Hiraku Sakamoto,Shin-ichiro Sakai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于凸优化的评估框架，用于分析由磁场相互作用维持的卫星群天线阵的功率消耗，研究表明增加卫星数量可以提高编队维持的功率效率，为大规模空间系统提供了一种高效的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天基分布式天线阵由多颗卫星组成，具有高增益、窄波束和大孔径能力，但其长期维护需要在非稳定的轨道动力学环境中进行持续的编队控制。磁力相互作用提供了一种无需推进剂的位置控制策略，但电磁力和力矩模型的非线性导致功率消耗约束呈非凸性，使系统级配置分析变得困难。

Method: 开发了一个基于凸优化的框架，用于分析大型磁驱动卫星群的功率消耗，解决了电磁力和力矩模型非线性导致的非凸功率约束问题。

Result: 分析结果表明，增加卫星数量可以提高编队维持的功率效率，说明磁驱动卫星群架构在构建大规模空间系统方面，相比传统的少数卫星电磁编队飞行概念具有更高的功率效率。

Conclusion: 磁驱动卫星群架构为构建大规模空间系统提供了一种功率高效的替代方案，相比传统的少数卫星电磁编队飞行概念具有明显优势。

Abstract: This correspondence presents a convex-optimization-based evaluation framework of satellite-swarm-based apertures maintained by magnetic-field interactions. Spaceborne distributed apertures are composed of multiple satellites and are attractive for scientific and commercial missions because their scalability enables high-gain, narrow-beam, and large-aperture capabilities beyond the launch-size limitations. A key challenge is that the long-term maintenance of such virtual structures requires consistent formation control amid unstable orbital dynamics, and magnetic interactions generated by satellite-mounted magnetorquers offer a desirable propellant-free position-control strategy. However, the nonlinearities of the electromagnetic force and torque model lead to a nonconvex power-consumption constraint, making system-level configuration analysis difficult. To address this issue, we develop a convex optimization-based framework to analyze the power consumption of large magnetically actuated satellite swarms. The resulting analysis shows that increasing the number of satellites can improve formation-keeping power efficiency. This indicates that magnetically actuated swarm architectures provide a power-efficient alternative to the conventional few-satellite electromagnetic formation-flight concept for constructing large-scale space systems.

</details>


### [244] [Improving the Efficiency of Language Agent Teams with Adaptive Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06320)
*Elizabeth Mieczkowski,Alexander Ku,Tiwalayo Eisape,Dilip Arumugam,John Matters,Katherine M. Collins,Ilia Sucholutsky,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了LATTE框架，一种受分布式系统启发的LLM团队协调方法，通过构建共享的协调图来动态管理任务依赖和代理分配，在保持准确性的同时显著降低了资源消耗和协调失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM团队协调方法存在两个极端：高度结构化的方法依赖固定的角色和任务分解，缺乏灵活性；完全非结构化的团队虽然具有适应性，但存在错误传播、代理冲突和资源浪费等问题。需要在两者之间找到平衡。

Method: 提出LATTE（Language Agent Teams for Task Evolution）框架，受分布式系统启发，让代理团队协作构建和维护一个共享的、不断演化的协调图。该图编码了子任务依赖关系、个体代理分配和子任务进度状态，使代理能够在保持一致性的同时动态分配工作、调整协调并发现新任务。

Result: 在多个协作任务和多种基础模型上的实验表明，LATTE在token使用量、运行时间、通信开销和协调失败（如文件冲突和冗余输出）方面均有所减少，同时在准确性上匹配或超过了MetaGPT、去中心化团队、自上而下的Leader-Worker层级结构和静态分解等标准设计。

Conclusion: LATTE成功地在结构化和非结构化协调方法之间取得了平衡，通过动态协调图机制实现了高效的LLM团队协作，证明了受分布式系统启发的协调协议在多代理系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in teams, yet existing coordination approaches often occupy two extremes. Highly structured methods rely on fixed roles, pipelines, or task decompositions assigned a priori. In contrast, fully unstructured teams enable adaptability and exploration but suffer from inefficiencies such as error propagation, inter-agent conflicts, and wasted resources (measured in time, tokens, or file operations). We introduce Language Agent Teams for Task Evolution (LATTE), a framework for coordinating LLM teams inspired by distributed systems, where processors must operate under partial observability and communication constraints. In LATTE, a team of agents collaboratively construct and maintain a shared, evolving coordination graph which encodes sub-task dependencies, individual agent assignment, and the current state of sub-task progress. This protocol maintains consistency while empowering agents to dynamically allocate work, adapt coordination, and discover new tasks. Across multiple collaborative tasks and a variety of base models, we demonstrate how LATTE reduces token usage, wall-clock time, communication, and coordination failures (e.g. file conflicts and redundant outputs) while matching or exceeding the accuracy of standard designs including MetaGPT, decentralized teams, top-down Leader-Worker hierarchies, and static decompositions.

</details>


### [245] [AgenticPrecoding: LLM-Empowered Multi-Agent System for Precoding Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06443)
*Zijiu Yang,Zixiang Zhang,Shunpu Tang,Qianqian Yang,Zhiguo Shi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgenticPrecoding，一个通用的多智能体框架，能够根据用户级通信需求自动推导端到端预编码方案，在跨场景适应性上优于传统优化和LLM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预编码方法通常针对特定的系统模型、目标和约束集开发，这限制了它们在未来6G网络中异构和演进场景下的适应能力。

Method: 提出AgenticPrecoding多智能体框架，将推导过程分解为问题建模、求解器选择、提示上采样和代码生成四个协调阶段。使用两个LoRA适应的推理智能体注入预编码领域知识，两个通用LLM处理提示优化和可执行代码生成，并引入反馈驱动的优化机制。

Result: 在10个代表性预编码场景上的大量实验表明，AgenticPrecoding相比传统优化方法和LLM基线方法具有更优越的跨场景适应能力。

Conclusion: AgenticPrecoding成功实现了预编码推导的自动化，为未来6G网络中异构和演进场景下的干扰管理和性能提升提供了通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Precoding is a key technique for interference management and performance improvement in multi-antenna wireless systems. However, existing precoding methods are typically developed for specific system models, objectives, and constraint sets, which limits their adaptability to the heterogeneous and evolving scenarios expected in future 6G networks. To address this limitation, we propose AgenticPrecoding, a universal multi-agent framework that automates end-to-end precoding derivation directly from user-level communication requirements. Specifically, AgenticPrecoding decomposes the derivation process into four coordinated stages: problem formulation, solver selection, prompt upsampling, and code generation, assigning each stage to a specialized agent tailored to its specific reasoning demands. We employ two LoRA-adapted reasoning agents to inject precoding-specific domain knowledge for problem formulation and solver selection, while two general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) handle prompt refinement and executable code generation. Furthermore, a feedback-driven refinement mechanism is incorporated to enhance code executability, constraint feasibility, and solution quality. Extensive experiments across 10 representative precoding scenarios demonstrate that AgenticPrecoding achieves superior cross-scenario adaptability compared to conventional optimization-based and LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [246] [Coordination Matters: Evaluation of Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06557)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Matthew Landers,Afsaneh Doryab*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协调感知的评估视角，通过过程级诊断补充传统的回报指标，用于更深入地分析多智能体强化学习（MARL）中的协调机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合作式多智能体强化学习基准主要关注回报、成功率或完成时间等聚合结果，但这些指标往往无法揭示智能体如何协调，特别是在智能体、任务和联合分配选择呈组合级增长的场景中。

Method: 提出了协调感知评估视角，并引入STAT测试平台（一种受承诺约束的空间任务分配环境），在固定观测权限和任务规则的前提下，系统性地变化智能体数量、任务和环境规模，以此对六种代表性基于价值的MARL方法进行评估。

Result: 研究发现，相似的回报趋势可能反映截然不同的协调机制，包括冗余分配、分配多样性和任务完成效率的差异。在承诺约束的任务分配中，规模化下的性能不仅受名义动作空间大小影响，还受分配压力、稀疏决策机会以及相互依赖智能体间的冗余选择影响。

Conclusion: 协调感知评估是合作式MARL中基于回报的基准测试的必要补充，能够更全面地揭示智能体的协调行为与性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) benchmarks commonly emphasize aggregate outcomes such as return, success rate, or completion time. While essential, these metrics often fail to reveal how agents coordinate, particularly in settings where agents, tasks, and joint assignment choices scale combinatorially. We propose a coordination-aware evaluation perspective that supplements return with process-level diagnostics. We instantiate this perspective using STAT, a controlled commitment-constrained spatial task-allocation testbed that systematically varies agents, tasks, and environment size while holding observation access and task rules fixed. We evaluate six representative value-based MARL methods across varying levels of centralization. Our results show that similar return trends can reflect distinct coordination mechanisms, including differences in redundant assignment, assignment diversity, and task-completion efficiency. We find that in commitment-constrained task allocation, performance under scale is shaped not only by nominal action-space size, but also by assignment pressure, sparse decision opportunities, and redundant choices among interdependent agents. Our findings motivate coordination-aware evaluation as a necessary complement to return-based benchmarking for cooperative MARL.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了标注员策略模型（APMs），这是一种可解释模型，能够仅从标注行为中学习标注员的内部安全策略，无需额外标注成本即可揭示标注分歧的来源（操作失误、策略歧义或价值多元主义），支持更透明和包容的AI安全策略设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全策略定义了安全和不安全输出的边界，指导数据标注和模型开发。然而，标注分歧普遍存在且来源多样：操作失误（标注员误解或误执行任务）、策略歧义（策略措辞留有解释空间）或价值多元主义（不同标注员对安全持不同观点）。区分这些来源至关重要，因为不同来源需要不同的解决方案（质量控制、策略澄清或纳入多元视角），但直接询问标注员推理过程成本高昂且不可靠。

Method: 提出标注员策略模型（APMs），这是一种可解释模型，仅从标注行为中学习标注员的内部安全策略，无需额外标注成本即可使标注员推理过程可见且可比较。该方法能够忠实预测对反事实编辑的响应，并在受控环境中恢复已知的策略差异。

Result: APMs能够准确建模标注员安全策略（准确率超过80%），忠实预测对反事实编辑的响应，并在受控环境中成功恢复已知策略差异。应用于LLM和人类标注时，展示了两个核心应用：（1）揭示策略歧义——展示标注员如何不同地解释安全指令；（2）揭示价值多元主义——发现不同人口群体在安全优先级上的系统性差异。

Conclusion: APMs的能力支持更有针对性、透明和包容的安全策略设计，能够有效识别标注分歧的根本原因，为制定相应的改进措施提供了有力工具，有助于提升AI安全策略的质量和公平性。

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [248] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ZAYA1-8B，一个在AMD全栈平台上训练的、具有700M激活参数和8B总参数的推理导向MoE模型。通过推理数据前置和四级RL级联训练，在不到1B激活参数下，ZAYA1-8B在多个数学和编程基准上匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528，并引入的Markovian RSA方法在测试时计算中将AIME'25和HMMT'25分数提升至91.9%和89.6%，缩小了与更大规模推理模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个高效且专注于推理能力的大语言模型，通过MoE架构在保持较小激活参数（<1B）的同时，实现与更大规模模型相当的推理性能，并探索如何从预训练开始就融入推理数据以提升模型的推理能力。
