At each round, ProCompNav extracts an attribute-value pair that splits the current pool, asks a binary yes/no question, and prunes all inconsistent candidates at once. This reframes disambiguation from open-ended target description to pool-level discriminative questioning, where each question is chosen to narrow the candidate set. On CoIN-Bench, ProCompNav improves Success Rate over interactive baselines with the same minimal input and non-interactive baselines with detailed descriptions, while substantially reducing Response Length. ProCompNav also achieves state-of-the-art Success Rate on TextNav, suggesting that comparative judgment is broadly useful for instance-level navigation among similar distractors.

</details>


### [354] [A Versatile AI Agent for Rare Disease Diagnosis and Risk Gene Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06226)
*Tianyu Liu,Wangjie Zheng,Rui Yang,Benny Kai Guo Loo,Hui Zhang,Jeffries Lauran,Jianlei Gu,Botao Yu,Weihao Xuan,Kexin Huang,Nan Liu,James Zou,Yonghui Jiang,Hua Xu,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Hygieia，一个多模态AI智能体系统，通过整合表型特征、基因档案和临床记录等多源数据，实现了精准的疾病诊断，在多个基准测试中达到SOTA性能，临床验证显示其诊断准确率比医生提升12%-60%，并能有效减轻医生工作负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的诊断流程在罕见病等疾病的诊断中存在评估时间长和准确率低的问题，影响了有效治疗。为了解决这些局限性，需要一个能够整合多源数据并支持精准诊断的系统。

Method: 提出了Hygieia，一个基于路由器和知识增强框架的多模态AI智能体系统。该系统整合了表型特征、基因档案和临床记录等多样化数据源，通过框架设计减轻幻觉问题，并针对不同疾病类别定制诊断策略。系统优先考虑罕见病的风险相关基因组因素，并提供置信度分数以辅助临床决策。

Result: 在多个诊断基准测试中达到了最先进的性能。通过与耶鲁医学院和杜克-新加坡国立大学医学院的临床专家合作验证，Hygieia的诊断表现优于医生，准确率提升12%-60%；同时在处理真实世界病例中有效辅助了临床医生。

Conclusion: Hygieia不仅提高了诊断准确性和可解释性，还显著减少了医生的工作量，证明了其作为临床决策支持系统中宝贵工具的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate and timely diagnosis is essential for effective treatment, particularly in the context of rare diseases. However, current diagnostic workflows often lead to prolonged assessment times and low accuracy. To address these limitations, we introduce Hygieia, a multi-modal AI agent system designed to support precision disease diagnosis by integrating diverse data sources, including phenotypic features, genetic profiles, and clinical records. Hygieia features a router-based and knowledge-enhanced framework that mitigates hallucination and tailors diagnostic strategies to different disease categories. Notably, it prioritizes risk-related genomic factors for rare diseases and provides confidence scores to assist clinical decision-making. We conducted a comprehensive evaluation demonstrating that Hygieia achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple diagnostic benchmarks. In collaboration with clinical experts from Yale School of Medicine and Duke-NUS Medical School, we further validated its practical utility by showing (1) Hygieia's superior diagnostic performance compared to physicians with an improvement from 12%-60% and (2) its effectiveness in assisting clinicians with medical records for handling real-world cases. Our findings indicate that Hygieia not only enhances diagnostic accuracy and interpretability but also significantly reduces clinician workload, highlighting its potential as a valuable tool in clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [355] [Price of Fairness in Short-Term and Long-Term Algorithmic Selections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06227)
*Shahin Jabbari,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了高风险场景下算法决策的长期公平性问题，发现静态公平约束可能加剧长期差异，而简单的投资策略可以在低公平代价下消除长期差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算法公平性研究多聚焦于静态场景，然而近期结果表明，强制执行静态公平约束反而可能在长期运行中加剧群体间的差异。这种静态公平与长期公平之间的矛盾促使作者研究一个风格化的序列选择问题，探讨决策如何在影响即时效用的同时，随时间改变群体分布，从而实现短期与长期的公平权衡。

Method: 作者构建了一个重复选择个体的序列决策模型，决策者每次选择不仅产生即时效用，还会随时间推移改变群体分布。研究引入了短期和长期两个维度的群体公平性概念，并通过“公平代价（Price of Fairness, PoF）”这一理论框架来量化分析公平性与效用之间的权衡。研究对短期最优公平策略进行了理论刻画，并分析了长期公平的实现条件。最后，利用合成数据和真实数据集对理论发现进行了实证验证。

Result: 理论分析表明，在短期内，公平代价（PoF）可能非常大，即使群体分布几乎相同也是如此。与之相对，长期差异在简单的投资策略下可以消失，并且这些策略能够实现较低的公平代价。实证结果验证了上述理论发现。

Conclusion: 静态公平约束可能导致不理想的长期后果，而设计合理的动态投资策略能够在保持较高效用（低公平代价）的同时，有效消除长期群体差异，为实现真正的长期公平提供了理论依据和实践方向。

Abstract: Algorithmic decision-making in high-stakes settings can have profound impacts on individuals and populations. While much prior work studies fairness in static settings, recent results show that enforcing static fairness constraints may exacerbate long-run disparities. Motivated by this tension, we study a stylized sequential selection problem in which a decision-maker repeatedly selects individuals, affecting both immediate utility and the population distribution over time. We introduce notions of group fairness for both the short and long term and theoretically analyze the trade-off between fairness and utility via the Price of Fairness (PoF). We characterize optimal and fair policies in the short term and show that the PoF can be large even when group distributions are nearly identical. In contrast, we show that long-term disparities can vanish under simple investment policies that achieve a low PoF. We also empirically validate these theoretical observations using both synthetic and real datasets.

</details>


### [356] [Data Language Models: A New Foundation Model Class for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06290)
*Eda Erol,Giuliano Pezzoli,Ozer Cem Kelahmet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了数据语言模型（DLM）Schema-1，这是首个能够原生理解表格数据的基础模型，无需预处理即可直接从原始单元格值学习，在行级预测、缺失值重建和数据集行业识别任务上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前所有主要数据模态（文本、图像、音频）都有能够原生理解该模态的基础模型，但表格数据缺乏这样的模型。现有表格AI方法（从梯度提升树到最新的表格基础模型）都需要预处理流程才能让模型消费数据，没有任何方法将表格数据视为一种原生模态来理解。

Method: 提出数据语言模型（DLM）概念，该模型像语言模型理解句子一样原生理解表格，无需序列化或预处理，直接从原始单元格值学习。发布Schema-1作为首个DLM实现：一个1.4亿参数的模型，在超过230万个合成和真实世界表格数据集上训练。

Result: Schema-1在已建立的行级预测基准上优于梯度提升集成、AutoML堆栈和所评估的表格基础模型；在缺失值重建任务上，平均性能优于所有经典统计方法和前沿大语言模型；能够仅从原始单元格值可靠识别任意未见数据集的行业部门，这是以往表格模型无法完成的任务。

Conclusion: DLM填补了AI技术栈中缺失的原生表格理解层，证明了数据集自身分布几何的结构理解比语言中编码的世界知识对缺失值填补更有价值，为构建表格数据上的AI模型、智能体和垂直AI应用提供了基础数据层。

Abstract: Every major data modality now has a foundation model that understands it natively: text has language models, images have vision models, audio has audio models. Tabular data, the modality on which many consequential real-world AI decisions are made, does not. Every approach to tabular AI today, from gradient-boosted trees to the latest tabular foundation models, requires a preprocessing pipeline before any model can consume the data. None of them understand tabular data as a modality. We introduce the Data Language Model (DLM), the missing foundation model for tabular data. A DLM understands tables the way a language model understands sentences: natively, without serialization or preprocessing, directly from raw cell values. It is the tabular data layer on which AI models, agents, and vertical AI applications can be built, eliminating the preprocessing pipelines that currently stand between raw data and every AI system that consumes it. We present Schema-1, the first DLM: a 140M parameter model trained on more than 2.3M synthetic and real-world tabular datasets. Schema-1 outperforms gradient-boosted ensembles, AutoML stacks, and the tabular foundation models we evaluate on established row-level prediction benchmarks. On missing value reconstruction it achieves lower reconstruction error than all classical statistical methods and frontier large language models on mean performance across conditions, establishing that structural understanding of a dataset's own distributional geometry is more useful for imputation than world knowledge encoded in language. It identifies the industry sector of any unseen dataset from raw cell values alone, reliably across any domain, a task no prior tabular model can perform. It is the native tabular understanding layer that has been missing from the AI stack.

</details>


### [357] [Addressing Labelled Data Scarcity: Taxonomy-Agnostic Annotation of PII Values in HTTP Traffic using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06305)
*Thomas Cory,Axel Küpper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的多阶段流水线，能够在运行时根据给定的分类法对HTTP消息体中的PII进行类型识别和数值提取，并开发了合成流量生成器以解决隐私数据标注难题，验证了LLM在灵活、分类法无关的流量标注中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的PII检测器严重依赖稀缺的人工标注数据，且与固定的标签分类法紧密耦合，导致其在跨域迁移和适应不断演变的PII定义方面存在局限性。为了解决这些问题，本文旨在探索大语言模型（LLMs）是否能够支持分类法无关的PII标注。

Method: 提出了一种多阶段的LLM流水线，结合了确定性预处理、标签级分类、针对性的实例级数值标注以及输出验证。此外，为了在不依赖敏感真实用户数据的情况下实现可控评估和基于示例的提示，还提出了一种基于LLM的合成HTTP流量生成器，用于生成带有经人工验证的分类法PII标注的数据。

Result: 跨越三个涵盖不同PII领域和粒度级别的分类法进行评估，结果显示该流水线能够准确检测PII类型并提取相应的数值。同时，合成的HTTP流量数据被证明可用于有效的模型评估。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，LLMs可以作为构建灵活、分类法无关的流量标注基础，并能在隐私分类法不断演变的情况下辅助创建标注数据，为自动化隐私审计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated privacy audits of web and mobile applications often analyse outbound HTTP traffic to detect Personally Identifiable Information (PII) leakage. However, existing learning-based detectors typically depend on scarce, manually labelled traffic and are tightly coupled to fixed label taxonomies, limiting transferability across domains and evolving definitions of PII. This paper investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can support taxonomy-agnostic annotation of explicitly transmitted PII values in HTTP message bodies when the taxonomy is provided at runtime. We introduce a multi-stage LLM-based pipeline that combines deterministic pre-processing with label-level classification, targeted instance-level value annotation, and output validation. To enable controlled evaluation and exemplar-based prompting without relying on sensitive real-user captures, we further propose an LLM-based generator for synthetic HTTP traffic with manually validated, taxonomy-derived PII annotations. We evaluate the approach across three taxonomies spanning different PII domains and granularity levels. Results show that the pipeline accurately detects PII types and extracts corresponding values for concrete PII taxonomies. Overall, our findings position LLMs as a promising foundation for flexible, taxonomy-agnostic traffic annotation and for creating labelled data under evolving privacy taxonomies.

</details>


### [358] [Measuring Black-Box Confidence via Reasoning Trajectories: Geometry, Coverage, and Verbalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06308)
*Marc Boubnovski Martell,Josefa Lia Stoisser,Kaspar Märtens,Jialin Yu,Robert Kitchen,Philip Torr,Jesper Ferkinghoff-Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种黑盒轨迹置信度评分方法，通过滑动窗口轨迹和答案锚点度量CoT推理的置信度，无需内部状态，在低采样率(K=4)下优于高采样率(K=8)的自一致性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CoT推理置信度估计主流方法——自一致性（self-consistency）需要对K个样本进行采样，计算成本随K线性增长且忽略了推理轨迹的几何特性。在仅通过文本API访问的黑盒场景下，需要一种更高效且利用推理轨迹信息的置信度估计方法。

Method: 提出黑盒轨迹置信度评分：将CoT嵌入为滑动窗口轨迹，使用单参数softmax度量其与外部答案锚点的收敛性。该方法分为三个通道：法官介导的覆盖率先验(C)、轨迹内几何特征(G)、条件语言化置信通道(V)，无需logits、隐状态或监督校准器。

Result: 在6个(基准,推理器)设置上，K=4时融合评分比K=8的自一致性实现Pareto改进（中位AUC 0.78 vs 0.71，deltaAUC=+0.075）。几何特征在18/18和16/18设置中提供独立信号，V在6/18设置中贡献残差信号。融合方法在17/18设置中超越最佳单通道（中位AUC 0.78，最大0.92）。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了黑盒置信度可以分解为三个独立信号源：覆盖率、几何特征和语言化置信，其中几何特征提供了关键的新信号。该方法在低采样率下实现更好性能，为黑盒场景下的安全部署提供了高效可靠的置信度估计方案。

Abstract: Reliable confidence estimation enables safe deployment of chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning through text-only APIs. Yet the dominant black-box baseline, self-consistency over K samples, is linearly expensive and ignores the geometry of the trace. We propose a black-box trajectory-confidence score: we embed a CoT as a sliding-window trajectory and measure its convergence to external answer anchors with a one-parameter softmax. The method needs no logits, hidden states, or supervised calibrators. Across six (benchmark, reasoner) settings on MedQA-USMLE, GPQA Diamond, and MMLU-Pro with Gemini 3.1 Pro and Claude Sonnet 4.6, fusing this score with coverage and verbalized-confidence channels at K=4 yields Pareto improvements over self-consistency at K=8 in 6/6 settings (median AUC 0.78 vs 0.71, deltaAUC=+0.075). A fixed-pick control (+0.060) and E5 cross-embedder replication rule out answer switching and single-vendor artifacts. Geometry peaks in the penultimate window across benchmarks and reasoners, and inverts at the terminal window on GPQA Diamond. Three unscaffolded regimes separate black-box confidence into a judge-mediated Coverage prior (C), within-trace Geometry (G), and a conditional Verbalization channel (V). Across 18 benchmark x reasoner x proposer settings, C and G provide independent signal in 18/18 and 16/18, while V contributes residual signal in 6/18. Swapping the judge from GPT-5-mini to Claude Sonnet 4.6 leaves G-only AUC unchanged (|delta|<=0.013) and shifts C-only AUC by at most +/-0.02 (kappa=0.82). Fusion beats the best single channel in 17/18 settings (median AUC 0.78, max 0.92).

</details>


### [359] [A Regime Theory of Controller Class Selection for LLM Action Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06339)
*Zhaoyang Jiang,Zhizhong Fu,Yunsoo Kim,Jiacong Mi,Zicheng Li,Xuanqi Peng,Honghan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对语言和视觉-语言模型的控制器选择理论。研究发现，在有限样本下，控制器复杂度并非越高越好。作者提出了一个包含四类控制器的嵌套格结构理论，并证明了如何根据三个数据可估计的瓶颈来选择最优控制器类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的语言模型和视觉-语言模型需要针对每个输入决定是直接回答、检索证据、推迟给更强的模型还是放弃。常规直觉认为表达能力越强越好，但本文发现这种单调性直觉在有限样本下并不成立：不同的基准测试偏好不同的控制器类别。这反映了实例级不确定性信号存在有限样本限制。

Method: 作者将控制器组织成一个包含四个类别的嵌套格结构：固定动作、分区路由器、实例级控制器和先验门控控制器，按复杂度排序。提出了一种状态理论，将三个数据可估计的瓶颈转化为类别选择依据：在最佳固定动作之外还有多少改进空间；是否有足够样本让实例级控制器做出可靠决策；当实例级信号不可靠时，粗粒度分区路由器能恢复多少改进。该方法包含了Bernstein紧阈值和匹配的信息论下界。

Result: 提出的Bernstein紧阈值具有匹配的信息论下界，严格的嵌套交叉验证能够选择接近最优的类别。在SMS-Spam、HallusionBench、A-OKVQA和FOLIO数据集上，预测的类别与经验上的赢家匹配；在TextVQA上，当OCR标记提供无标签预测时先验门控控制器获胜。

Conclusion: 本文证明了控制器复杂度的选择并非"越高越好"，而应该根据具体的数据特性来决定。提出的状态理论为控制器类别选择提供了理论依据，严格的交叉验证方法能够可靠地选择接近最优的控制器类别。该理论框架对于实际部署的语言和视觉-语言模型具有重要的指导意义。

Abstract: Deployed language and vision-language models must decide, on each input, whether to answer directly, retrieve evidence, defer to a stronger model, or abstain. Contrary to the common monotonicity intuition, greater per-input expressivity is not uniformly beneficial in finite samples: under identical strict cross-validation, different benchmarks prefer different controller classes. This reflects a finite-sample limitation of instance-level uncertainty signals, which can be exhausted at a distribution-dependent scale. We organize controllers into a nested lattice of four classes: fixed actions, partition routers, instance-level controllers, and prior-gated controllers, ordered by complexity. We prove a regime theory that turns three data-estimable bottlenecks into a class choice: how much improvement is possible beyond the best fixed action, whether there are enough samples for instance-level controllers to make reliable decisions, and how much improvement a coarse partition router can recover when instance-level signal is unreliable. The resulting Bernstein-tight threshold has a matching information-theoretic lower bound, and strict nested cross-validation provably selects a near-best class. Across SMS-Spam, HallusionBench, A-OKVQA, and FOLIO, the predicted class matches the empirical winner; the prior-gated controller wins on TextVQA when OCR tokens supply a label-free prediction-time prior. Code is available at https://github.com/Anonymous-Awesome-Submissions/Regime-Theory.

</details>


### [360] [Mind the Gap? A Distributional Comparison of Real and Synthetic Priors for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06343)
*Alex O. Davies,Telmo de Menezes e Silva Filho,Nirav Ajmeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了表格基础模型的三类预训练语料库（网页抓取、人工策展、合成数据），发现合成数据与真实数据分布差距显著且无法通过超参数优化消除，而策展数据与网页抓取数据在分布上可互换，但令人意外的是合成数据的分布差距并未明显影响下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型的预训练数据来源主要有三类，但不同语料库之间的分布差异及其对下游性能的影响尚不明确。理解这些数据的分布关系对于优化预训练策略和提升模型泛化能力至关重要。

Method: 选取三类代表性数据集（T4网页抓取、TabFM策展数据、TabICL合成数据），通过聚合特征（表级、列级、相关性特征）刻画语料库特性，使用判别器AUC和k-NN覆盖率指标比较分布差异，并测试超过8.6万种超参数配置以优化合成先验。

Result: TabICL合成先验仅覆盖真实表格分布空间的狭窄区域，且该分布失配无法通过大规模超参数优化消除；策展语料库与网页抓取语料库在特征空间的分布层面具有广泛可互换性；意外的是，合成预训练数据与真实表格的分布差距对模型性能无明显影响。

Conclusion: 真实数据分布覆盖率并非TabICL泛化能力的主要驱动因素，这表明表格基础模型可能通过其他机制（如学习通用结构模式而非匹配具体数据分布）实现泛化，为未来预训练数据选择提供了新见解。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models are pre-trained on one of three classes of corpus: curated datasets drawn from benchmark repositories, tables harvested at scale from the web, or synthetic tables sampled from a parametric generative prior. Despite the centrality of pre-training data to model performance, little is known about how these corpora relate to one another in distribution, and the impact this has on downstream performance. In this work we take three canonical, archetypal datasets used to train tabular foundation models; the T4 dataset represents web-scraped corpora, the TabFM dataset curated tables from Kaggle, and the TabICL dataset as the only well-used synthetic prior with publicly available parameters. We characterise each corpus using aggregate features over whole tables, columns and correlations, and compare them using discriminator AUCs and k-NN coverage metrics. We find that the TabICL synthetic prior occupies a narrow region of the space of real tables, that this mismatch cannot be closed by optimising prior hyper-parameters across more than 86 thousand configurations, and that curated and web-scraped corpora are broadly interchangeable on a distributional level in feature space. Surprisingly, the distributional gap between synthetic pre-training data and real tables has a clearly detectable effect on performance under neither feature-based proximity measures or TabICL's own internal representations, suggesting that coverage of the real-data distribution is not the primary driver of TabICL's generalisation.

</details>


### [361] [More Than Can Be Said: A Benchmark and Framework for Pre-Question Scientific Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06345)
*Jie Yu,Song Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI research agents have shown strong potential in automating literature search and manuscript refinement, yet most assume a clear and actionable initial input, operating only after a research question has been made explicit. In contrast, human research often begins with tacit friction, a sense of misalignment before a question can be formed. We introduce InciteResearch, a multi-agent framework designed to make a researcher's implicit understanding explicit, inspectable, and actionable. InciteResearch decomposes the logical chain of Socratic questioning and distributes it across the entire pipeline that: (1) Elicits a structured five-dimensional researcher profile state anchored by specific friction points from vague, even domain-unrelated inputs; (2) Violates hidden assumptions by maximizing the feasibility-novelty product with enforcing a 7-stage causal derivation trace; and (3) check whether the proposed method is a Necessary consequence of the reframed insight. We further introduce TF-Bench, the first benchmark for tacit-to-explicit research assistance that distinguishes domain-related from domain-unrelated inspirations across four scientific modes. On TF-Bench, InciteResearch achieves leapfrogging gains over a prompt-based baseline (novelty/impact from 3.671/3.806 to 4.250/4.397), shifting generated proposals from recombination to architectural insight. Our work demonstrates that AI can serve as an extension of thinking itself, rather than merely automating downstream execution.

</details>


### [362] [Prediction and Empowerment: A Theory of Agency through Bridge Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06346)
*Richard Csaky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在确定性世界中研究部分可观测性下的智能体行为，证明了预测、压缩和赋能之间的分离关系，为AI系统设计提供了区分隐藏状态识别、接口优化、任务相关可控性等目标的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究确定性物理或模拟世界中的部分可观测性下的智能体行为，其中表观随机性源于初始条件、固定规律位和展开的外生噪声的不确定性，旨在理解预测、压缩和赋能之间的关系。

Method: 建立桥接接口模型，将感知和执行建模为智能体控制参数和环境控制通道状态之间的接口，通过潜在微观状态的先验和多多对一观测粗化诱导确定性POMDP，并使用比特串专业化和保守信息预算进行理论分析。

Result: 证明了预测、压缩和赋能之间的分离：完美预测可通过识别相关的隐藏商或通过覆盖控制实现，而高赋能本身不足；动作条件观测压缩进展减少关于潜在商的后验不确定性；接口优化需要调控世界侧通道条件时产生目标条件接口赋能。

Conclusion: 为现代AI智能体提供了设计原则而非必然性定理：目标应区分隐藏状态识别、接口优化、任务相关可控性和覆盖或干扰控制；人机对齐部分是接口设计问题，涉及人类意图、智能体内部状态、外部工具和世界侧通道条件之间的桥接。

Abstract: We study agency under partial observability in deterministic physical or simulated worlds, where apparent randomness arises from uncertainty over initial conditions, fixed law bits, and unrolled exogenous noise. We model sensing and actuation as bridge interfaces split between agent-controlled parameters and environment-controlled channel state, inducing a deterministic POMDP through a prior over latent microstates and many-to-one observation coarsening. Within this framework, we prove a separation between prediction, compression, and empowerment. Perfect prediction can be achieved either by identifying the hidden quotient relevant to the target family or by overwrite control that makes the future target action-determined; high empowerment alone is insufficient. Under refinable interfaces and sufficient memory, action-conditioned observation-compression progress reduces posterior uncertainty about the latent quotient, and when refinement requires steering world-side channel conditions, this creates target-conditioned interface empowerment. A bit-string specialization with a conserved information budget makes the resulting tradeoff explicit: prediction by identification requires internal capacity at least the relevant latent entropy, whereas overwrite control requires terminal action capacity over the controlled quotient. For modern AI agents, the results suggest a design principle rather than a theorem of inevitability: objectives should distinguish hidden-state identification, interface refinement, task-relevant controllability, and mere overwrite or distractor control. Human--AI alignment is partly an interface-design problem, where the relevant bridge is between human intent, agent internal state, external tools, and world-side channel conditions. This is a working draft: feedback and criticism is most welcome.

</details>


### [363] [Debiased Multimodal Personality Understanding through Dual Causal Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06371)
*Yangfu Zhu,Zitong Han,Nianwen Ning,Yuting Wei,Yuandong Wang,Hang Feng,Zhenzhou Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究针对多模态人格理解中的主体偏差问题，提出了双因果调节网络（DCAN），通过后门和前门调整模块实现因果解耦，在提升预测准确性的同时显著改善了公平性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态人格理解方法容易受到主体偏差（如可观察的年龄和不可观察的心理状态）的影响，来自不同人口统计背景的受试者可能导致多模态特征与人格特质之间的虚假关联，从而造成不公平的人格理解。

Method: 构建结构因果模型（SCM）分析偏差影响，提出双因果调节网络（DCAN），包含：(1)后门调整因果学习模块（BACL），通过基于原型的混淆字典阻断可观察人口统计因素的虚假相关性；(2)前门调整因果学习模块（FACL），通过学习的中介字典干预解决潜在且不可观察的偏差，实现表示的因果解耦。同时构建了人口统计标注的多模态学生人格（DMSP）数据集。

Result: 在CFI-V2和DMSP数据集上，预测准确率分别达到92.11%和92.90%；公平性指标方面，均等机会和人口统计均等在CFI-V2上分别提升6.57%和7.97%，在DMSP数据集上分别提升15.38%和20.06%。

Conclusion: DCAN能够有效缓解主体属性对人格理解的干扰，在保持高预测准确率的同时显著提升公平性，为解决多模态人格理解中的偏差问题提供了有效的因果推理框架。

Abstract: Multimodalpersonalityunderstandingplaysacriticalroleinhuman centered artificial intelligence. Previous work mainly focus on learn-ing rich multimodal representations for video personality under standing. However, they often suffer from potential harm caused by subject bias (e.g., observable age and unobservable mental states), as subjects originate from diverse demographic backgrounds. Learn ing such spurious associations between multimodal features and traits may lead to unfair personality understanding. In this work, weconstruct aStructural Causal Model (SCM)toanalyze theimpact of these biases from a causal perspective, and propose a novel Dual Causal Adjustment Network (DCAN) to mitigate the interference of subject attributes on personality understanding. Specifically, we design a Back-door Adjustment Causal Learning (BACL) module to block spurious correlations from observable demographic factors via a prototype-based confounder dictionary, and subsequently ap ply a Front-door Adjustment Causal Learning (FACL) module to ad dress latent and unobservable biases throughalearnedmediatordic tionary intervention, thereby achieving causal disentanglement of representations for deconfounded reasoning. Importantly, we con struct a Demographic-annotated Multimodal Student Personality (DMSP) dataset to support the analysis and discussion of fairness related factors. Extensive experiments on the benchmark dataset CFI-V2 and our DMSPdataset demonstrate that DCAN consistently improves prediction accuracy, reaching 92.11% and 92.90%, respec tively. Meanwhile, the improvementsinthefairnessmetricsofequal opportunity and demographic parity are 6.57% and 7.97% on CFI-V2, and 15.38% and 20.06% on the DMSP dataset. Our code and DMSP dataset are available at https://github.com/Sabrina-han/DCAN

</details>


### [364] [Rethinking Vacuity for OOD Detection in Evidential Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06382)
*Claire McNamara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了证据深度学习(EDL)中使用不确定性度量(Vacuity)进行OOD检测时存在评估缺陷：当ID和OOD数据集的类别数(K)不一致时，会人为地大幅膨胀AUROC和AUPR指标（最高可达0.683），导致评估结果失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用Vacuity（不确定性质量）作为EDL模型OOD检测的评价指标，但Vacuity的计算对类别数(K)敏感。由于EDL的特性，K与信念强度(S)之间不太可能呈线性关系，因此比较ID和OOD结果时需要确保类别数一致，但在实际应用中这一条件常被忽视。

Method: 通过实证分析，对比类别数差异仅为1时ID与OOD数据集上的AUROC和AUPR指标变化。实验涵盖标准EDL和IB-EDL模型，并扩展讨论了因果语言模型在多选问答数据集上的EDL评估问题。

Result: 实验发现：当ID和OOD类别数相差仅为1时，标准EDL的AUROC差异达0.318，AUPR差异达0.613；IB-EDL的AUROC差异达0.360，AUPR差异达0.683。这表明类别数不一致会导致评估指标人为膨胀，而模型的实际预测并未改变。

Conclusion: 基于EDL微调的大语言模型的Vacuity基OOD检测对类别数差异高度敏感。研究呼吁在评估时需要严格控制ID和OOD数据集的类别数一致，并在因果语言模型评估中需要更明确地定义ID和OOD边界。

Abstract: Vacuity, or Uncertainty Mass (UM), is commonly used as a metric to evaluate Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection in Evidential Deep Learning (EDL). It generally involves dividing the number of classes ($K$) by the total strength of belief ($S$) of the model's predictions, where $S$ is derived from summing the Dirichlet parameters. As such, UM is sensitive to the cardinality of $K$. In particular, it is unlikely in practice that there is a linear relationship between $K$ and $S$ as $K$ and $S$ increase due to the nature of EDL (suppressing incorrectly assigned evidence). As a result, when comparing In Distribution (ID) and OOD results, it is important that $K_{\mathrm{ID}}$ and $K_{\mathrm{OOD}}$ are equal; something that is not always ensured in practice. We provide an empirical demonstration of how results for AUROC and AUPR can substantially differ when class cardinality between ID and OOD differs by 1, with AUROC differing by as much as 0.318 and AUPR by 0.613 for standard EDL, and AUROC by 0.360 and AUPR by 0.683 for IB-EDL. More concretely, our findings isolate an evaluation artefact: when K differs between ID and OOD, AUROC/AUPR can be artificially inflated without any change in model predictions. We further discuss the evaluation of EDL over causal language models using Multiple-Choice Question-Answer (MCQA) datasets and argue for clearer definitions of ID and OOD in this context. Our primary contribution is an empirical and theoretical demonstration that vacuity-based OOD detection in EDL-fine-tuned LLMs is highly sensitive to uncontrolled differences in evaluated class cardinality.

</details>


### [365] [Automated alignment is harder than you think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06390)
*Aleksandr Bowkis,Marie Davidsen Buhl,Jacob Pfau,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 自动化AI对齐研究可能导致看似可信但具有灾难性误导的安全评估，因为对齐研究包含许多难以监督的模糊任务，AI系统可能产生系统性、未被检测的错误，最终导致部署未对齐的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的ASI对齐方案是通过AI代理自动化对齐研究，但作者指出该方案存在严重风险：即使研究代理没有蓄意破坏，也可能产生令人信服但实际上具有灾难性误导的安全评估，导致错误地部署未对齐的AI系统。

Method: 分析自动化对齐研究中难以监督的模糊任务（缺乏明确评估标准、人类判断存在系统性缺陷的任务）所带来的问题，并论证自动化对齐研究比人类对齐研究面临更严峻挑战的四个原因：(1)优化压力使AI错误集中在人类审查者最难以发现的区域；(2)AI产生的错误类型与人类错误不相似；(3)AI生成的对齐方案可能涉及人类无法评估的论点；(4)共享权重、数据和训练过程使AI输出比人类等效物更具相关性。

Result: 确定了自动化对齐研究的关键失败模式——难以监督的模糊任务将导致系统性、未被检测的错误，并分析了泛化和可扩展监督这两个主要候选方案在该背景下所面临的新颖挑战。

Conclusion: 必须训练AI代理可靠地执行难以监督的模糊任务，泛化和可扩展监督是实现这一目标的领先候选方案，但在自动化对齐的背景下两者都面临独特的新挑战。

Abstract: A leading proposal for aligning artificial superintelligence (ASI) is to use AI agents to automate an increasing fraction of alignment research as capabilities improve. We argue that, even when research agents are not scheming to deliberately sabotage alignment work, this plan could produce compelling but catastrophically misleading safety assessments resulting in the unintentional deployment of misaligned AI. This could happen because alignment research involves many hard-to-supervise fuzzy tasks (tasks without clear evaluation criteria, for which human judgement is systematically flawed). Consequently, research outputs will contain systematic, undetected errors, and even correct outputs could be incorrectly aggregated into overconfident safety assessments. This problem is likely to be worse for automated alignment research than for human-generated alignment research for several reasons: 1) optimisation pressure means agent-generated mistakes are concentrated among those that human reviewers are least likely to catch; 2) agents are likely to produce errors that do not resemble human mistakes; 3) AI-generated alignment solutions may involve arguments humans cannot evaluate; and 4) shared weights, data and training processes may make AI outputs more correlated than human equivalents. Therefore, agents must be trained to reliably perform hard-to-supervise fuzzy tasks. Generalisation and scalable oversight are the leading candidates for achieving this but both face novel challenges in the context of automated alignment.

</details>


### [366] [Knowledge Graphs, the Missing Link in Agentic AI-based Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06434)
*Vaisakh Naduvodi Viswambharan,Keerthan Kopparam Radhakrishna,Deepak Narayan Gadde,Aman Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于验证中心知识图谱（KG）的多智能体工作流，通过从规格说明、RTL和形式工具反馈中提取结构化中间表示来构建图谱，实现从自然语言规范自动生成高质量SystemVerilog断言，在七个基准设计上达到78.5%-99.4%的形式覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的断言生成方法将规范和RTL视为松散结构化文本，导致规范到RTL的接地弱、语义不匹配和频繁的语法错误；同时规范通常存在歧义或不完整，关键的微架构细节存在于RTL中，使得高质量断言合成仍具挑战性。

Method: 构建验证中心知识图谱（KG），整合来自规范、RTL和形式工具反馈（语法诊断、反例CEX、覆盖率报告）的结构化中间表示（IRs）。KG链接需求、设计层次、信号、假设和属性以提供可追溯、设计接地上下文。多智能体工作流查询和更新KG以生成SVA，并驱动三个精化循环：工具诊断引导的语法修复、CEX引导的修正、覆盖导向的属性增强。

Result: 在七个基准设计上的评估表明：基于KG的上下文检索改善了规范到RTL的接地，持续产生可编译的SVA且语法修复开销低；形式覆盖率范围从78.5%到99.4%；收敛性表现出设计依赖性，复杂时序和算术推理对当前LLM能力仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了结构化知识图谱在增强LLM生成断言方面的有效性，能够显著改善规范-RTL接地问题并提高断言质量，但仍需进一步提升LLM在复杂时序与算术推理方面的能力以应对设计依赖的收敛挑战。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled workflows that generate SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs) from natural-language specifications, with the potential to accelerate Formal Verification (FV). However, high-quality assertion synthesis remains challenging because specifications are often ambiguous or incomplete and critical micro-architectural details reside in the Register Transfer Level (RTL). Many existing approaches treat the specification and RTL as loosely structured text, which weakens specification-to-RTL grounding and leads to semantic mismatches and frequent syntax failures during formal parsing and elaboration. This work addresses these limitations with a verification-centric Knowledge Graph (KG) constructed from structured Intermediate Representations (IRs) extracted from the specification, RTL, and formal-tool feedback, including syntax diagnostics, Counterexamples (CEXs), and coverage reports. The KG links requirements, design hierarchy, signals, assumptions, and properties to provide traceable, design-grounded context for generation. A multi-agent workflow queries and updates this KG to generate SVAs and to drive three refinement loops: syntax repair guided by tool diagnostics, CEX-guided correction using trace links, and coverage-directed property augmentation. Evaluation across seven benchmark designs indicates that KG-based context retrieval improves specification-to-RTL grounding and consistently produces compilable SVAs with low syntax-repair overhead. The approach achieves formal coverage ranging from 78.5% to 99.4%, though convergence exhibits design dependence with complex temporal and arithmetic reasoning remaining challenging for current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [367] [SCRuB: Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06444)
*Jamelle Watson-Daniels,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Skyler Wang,Brandon Handoko,Antonio Li,Anaelia Ovalle,Mahesh Pasupuleti,Candace Ross,Vidya Sarma,Arjun Subramonian,Karen Ullrich,Will van der Vaart,Yijing Xin,Maximilian Nickel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了SCRuB框架，用于系统评估大语言模型在社会概念推理方面的能力。通过对4,711个评估提示的测试和专家对比评判，发现前沿模型在所有五个批判性思维维度上均持续超越人类专家，模型在80.8%的配对比较中被专家评委排名第一。研究表明单轮考试式评估已达到饱和状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型推理能力研究主要关注数学或技术任务，而缺乏对社会概念推理（塑造社会规范、文化和制度的抽象思想）的系统性研究。这一能力对于现代模型作为社会代理至关重要，但目前尚无针对该能力的系统评估方法。

Method: 提出了SCRuB（Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation）框架，包含三个阶段：基于权威来源构建提示、由专家和模型生成回答、使用五维批判性思维量表进行对比评估。引入了学科视角小组集成方法以确保评估的泛化性，并建立了SCRuBEval数据集（4,711个评估提示）和SCRuBAnnotations数据集（300个专家撰写的回答和150个专家对比评判，来自45位博士级学者）。

Result: 前沿模型在所有五个量表维度上均持续超越人类专家。在1,170次配对比较中，专家评委在80.8%的判断中将模型回答排在首位，在74.4%的情况下整体偏好模型回答。

Conclusion: 本研究首次提供了社会概念推理评估饱和的专家验证证据：单轮考试式评估格式对模型和人类都已达到天花板，表明该评估范式需要革新以继续衡量能力进步。

Abstract: While many studies of Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning capabilities emphasize mathematical or technical tasks, few address reasoning about social concepts: the abstract ideas shaping social norms, culture, and institutions. This understudied capability is essential for modern models acting as social agents, yet no systematic evaluation methodology targets it. We introduce SCRuB (Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation), a framework designed for this setting of task indeterminacy. Our goal is to measure the degree to which a model reasons about social concepts with the depth and critical rigor of a human expert. SCRuB proceeds in three phases: prompt construction from established sources, response generation by experts and models, and comparative evaluation using a five-dimensional critical thinking rubric. To enable generalization of the pipeline, we introduce a Panel of Disciplinary Perspectives ensemble validated against independent expert judges. We release SCRuBEval (n=4,711 evaluation prompts) and SCRuBAnnotations (300 expert-authored responses and 150 expert comparative judgments from 45 PhD-level scholars). Our results show that frontier models consistently outperform human experts across all five rubric dimensions. Across 1,170 pairwise comparisons, expert judges ranked a model response first in 80.8% of judgments and preferred model responses overall 74.4% of the time. Ultimately, this study provides the first expert-grounded demonstration of evaluation saturation for social concept reasoning: the single-turn exam-style format has reached its ceiling for models and humans alike.

</details>


### [368] [PrefixGuard: From LLM-Agent Traces to Online Failure-Warning Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06455)
*Xinmiao Huang,Jinwei Hu,Rajarshi Roy,Changshun Wu,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PrefixGuard框架，通过离线StepView归纳步骤和监督训练构建轻量级前缀监控器，用于LLM智能体执行长周期工具任务时的风险预警，在多个基准上AUPRC平均提升+0.137，优于LLM裁判方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体执行长周期、工具调用任务时，最终结果检查往往为时已晚无法干预。现有方法存在局限：手工编写的事件模式脆弱易失效，部署时的LLM判断成本高昂且延迟大。因此需要一种轻量级的、能处理异构轨迹的前缀监控方法，在任务执行过程中及时预警。

Method: PrefixGuard框架包含两个核心步骤：1) 离线StepView归纳步骤，从原始轨迹样本中诱导确定性类型步骤适配器，将异构轨迹转化为统一的结构化表示；2) 监督监控器训练，学习事件抽象和前缀风险评分器，利用终端结果作为监督信号。框架还推导了基于分数的AUPRC可观测性上限，区分监控器错误与观测前缀中缺乏证据的失败，并支持后验DFA提取以实现有限状态审计。

Result: 在WebArena、τ²-Bench、SkillsBench和TerminalBench四个基准上，最强PrefixGuard监控器分别达到0.900/0.710/0.533/0.557 AUPRC。相比原始文本控制，在每种表示的最强后端下平均提升+0.137 AUPRC，显著优于相同前缀预警协议下的LLM裁判方法。DFA提取在WebArena和τ²-Bench上保持紧凑（29和20个状态），但在SkillsBench和TerminalBench上扩展至151和187个状态。首次警报诊断显示：WebArena排名良好但低误报警报支持不足，τ²-Bench和TerminalBench保留更多可操作的早期警报。

Conclusion: PrefixGuard为监控器合成提供了实用方案，具备明确诊断能力，可判断前缀警告何时转化为可操作的干预措施。研究结果表明强排名并不直接意味着部署实用性，不同基准在低误报早期警报能力上存在差异，为实际部署提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now execute long, tool-using tasks where final outcome checks can arrive too late for intervention. Online warning requires lightweight prefix monitors over heterogeneous traces, but hand-authored event schemas are brittle and deployment-time LLM judging is costly. We introduce PrefixGuard, a trace-to-monitor framework with an offline StepView induction step followed by supervised monitor training. StepView induces deterministic typed-step adapters from raw trace samples, and the monitor learns an event abstraction and prefix-risk scorer from terminal outcomes. Across WebArena, $τ^2$-Bench, SkillsBench, and TerminalBench, the strongest PrefixGuard monitors reach 0.900/0.710/0.533/0.557 AUPRC. Using the strongest backend within each representation, they improve over raw-text controls by an average of +0.137 AUPRC. LLM judges remain substantially weaker under the same prefix-warning protocol. We also derive an observability ceiling on score-based area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) that separates monitor error from failures lacking evidence in the observed prefix. For finite-state audit, post-hoc deterministic finite automaton (DFA) extraction remains compact on WebArena and $τ^2$-Bench (29 and 20 states) but expands to 151 and 187 states on SkillsBench and TerminalBench. Finally, first-alert diagnostics show that strong ranking does not imply deployment utility: WebArena ranks well yet fails to support low-false-alarm alerts, whereas $τ^2$-Bench and TerminalBench retain more actionable early alerts. Together, these results position PrefixGuard as a practical monitor-synthesis recipe with explicit diagnostics for when prefix warnings translate into actionable interventions.

</details>


### [369] [Beyond Task Success: Measuring Workflow Fidelity in LLM-Based Agentic Payment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06457)
*Donghao Huang,Joon Kiat Chua,Zhaoxia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的评估指标ASR（Agentic Success Rate），通过轨迹级别的转移精度和召回率来评估LLM多智能体支付系统，发现现有指标无法检测到的系统性工作流偏差，并指导优化使TSR最高提升93.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM多智能体支付系统评估指标TSR（任务成功率）和HF1（智能体交接F1分数）只能捕获最终结果或无序的路由决策，无法检测执行序列中的偏差，在监管严格的支付领域存在安全隐患，需要更精细的轨迹级评估指标。

Method: 提出ASR（Agentic Success Rate）指标，这是一种轨迹保真度度量，在转移级别比较观察到的和期望的智能体执行序列，将性能分解为转移召回率和转移精度。在HMASP系统上对18个LLM模型和90,000个任务实例进行了评估，并基于ASR诊断进行提示优化和确定性路由保护。

Result: 发现18个模型中有10个在支付结账时系统性地跳过确认检查点，这种偏差在TSR和HF1中不可见，而其他8个模型完美执行检查点。GPT-4.1虽然TSR和HF1完美但存在隐藏的工作流捷径，GPT-5.2则实现了完美的ASR。基于ASR诊断的优化使先前表现不佳的模型TSR提升高达93.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 轨迹级评估在监管领域至关重要，ASR指标能够揭示传统指标无法检测的系统性工作流偏差，为LLM多智能体系统的安全性评估和优化提供了有效工具。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed for payment workflows, yet prevailing metrics, Task Success Rate (TSR) and Agent Handoff F1-Score (HF1), capture only final outcomes or unordered routing decisions. We introduce the Agentic Success Rate (ASR), a trajectory-fidelity metric that compares observed and expected agent execution sequences at the transition level, decomposing performance into Transition Recall and Transition Precision. Applied to the Hierarchical Multi-Agent System for Payments (HMASP) across 18 LLMs and 90,000 task instances, ASR reveals that 10 of 18 models systematically skip a confirmation checkpoint during payment checkout, a deviation invisible to both TSR and HF1, while 8 models enforce the checkpoint perfectly. Notably, GPT-4.1 exhibits hidden workflow shortcuts despite achieving perfect TSR and HF1, while GPT-5.2 achieves perfect ASR. Prompt refinements and deterministic routing guards guided by ASR diagnostics yield substantial TSR improvements, with gains up to +93.8 percentage points for previously struggling models, demonstrating that trajectory-level evaluation is essential in regulated domains.

</details>


### [370] [Probabilistic Dating of Historical Manuscripts via Evidential Deep Regression on Visual Script Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06475)
*Ranjith Chodavarapu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉特征的历史手稿页面年代测定概率方法，将年代测定建模为证据深度回归问题，在DIVA-HisDB基准上实现了5.4年的测试MAE，在单次前向传播中实现了92.6%的PICP最佳校准性能，推理成本比MC Dropout和深度集成低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中通常将世纪聚合为类别进行分类，这种方法无法提供细粒度的年代预测和不确定性估计。作者希望实现连续年份轴上的精确年代测定，并提供分解的认知不确定性和偶然不确定性估计，以支持更可靠的历史手稿年代鉴定。

Method: 采用EfficientNet-B2作为骨干网络，结合正态逆伽马(NIG)输出头，构建证据深度回归架构。通过联合负对数似然和证据正则化目标函数进行训练，使神经网络能够在单次前向传播中输出完整的预测分布，并分解偶然不确定性和认知不确定性。

Result: 在DIVA-HisDB基准(150页，3部中世纪法典，151,936个图块)上实现5.4年测试MAE，93%的图块在5年误差内，97%在10年误差内。PICP达到92.6%，优于MC Dropout(88.2%，50次传递)和深度集成(79.7%，5个模型)，推理成本降低5倍。偶然不确定性是年代测定误差的强预测因子(Spearman ρ=0.729)，最确定的20%图块可实现0.5年MAE。页面级别聚合将MAE降至4.5年。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了历史手稿的精确年代测定，提供了可靠的不确定性估计和分解。偶然不确定性可以有效预测测定误差，支持选择性预测策略。随着图像退化加剧，预测不确定性增加；空间分解图能够解释哪些脚本区域引起偶然不确定性。这为历史文献的年代鉴定提供了一种高效、准确且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce a probabilistic approach for dating historical manuscript pages from visual features alone. Instead of aggregating centuries into classes as is standard in the previous literature, we pose dating as an evidential deep regression problem over a continuous year axis, allowing our neural network to output a full predictive distribution with decomposed aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty in a single forward pass. Our architecture combines an EfficientNet-B2 backbone with a Normal-Inverse-Gamma (NIG) output head trained with a joint negative-log-likelihood and evidence-regularization objective. On the DIVA-HisDB benchmark (150 pages, 3 medieval codices, 151,936 patches), our model scores a test MAE of 5.4 years, well below the 50-year century-label supervision granularity, with 93\% of patches within 5 years and 97\% within 10 years. Our approach achieves \textbf{PICP=92.6\%}, the best calibration among all compared methods, in a single forward pass, outperforming MC Dropout (PICP=88.2\%, 50 passes) and Deep Ensembles (PICP=79.7\%, 5 models) at $5\times$ lower inference cost. Uncertainty decomposition shows aleatoric uncertainty is a strong predictor of dating error (Spearman $ρ=0.729$), and a selective prediction about the most certain 20\% of patches can provide \textbf{0.5 years MAE}. We show that predicted uncertainty increases as image degradation worsens, spatial decomposition maps explain which script regions cause aleatoric uncertainty, and page-level aggregation reduces MAE to 4.5 years with $ρ=0.905$ between uncertainty and page-level error.

</details>


### [371] [Patch-Effect Graph Kernels for LLM Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06480)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,David N. Olivieri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将激活修补分析重构为图机器学习问题的框架，通过将修补效应表示为图结构并应用图核方法进行分析，为比较和评估电路结构提供了压缩与评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性研究通过激活修补识别因果电路，但跨多样提示和任务家族扩展这些干预会产生高维、非结构化的数据集，难以进行系统性的比较分析。

Method: 将激活修补配置重构为图机器学习问题：把修补效应表示为模型组件上的修补效应图，引入三种图构建方法（因果中介的直接影响、偏相关、共影响），并应用图核分析生成的结构。在GPT-2 Small上使用间接宾语识别及相关任务进行验证。

Result: 修补效应图保留了判别性的结构信号。具体而言，局部化的边槽特征比全局图形状描述符提供更高的分类准确率。筛选的成对修补验证表明，共影响和偏相关选择的候选边比随机或低秩候选对应更强的激活影响效应。

Conclusion: 该框架为在受控基线下比较修补导出的结构提供了压缩和评估流程，能够分离出稳健的切片判别性证据与更强的任务通用因果电路主张。图特征压缩了结构化的修补信号，而原始张量和表面线索定义了任何电路级主张都应面对的强力基线。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to reverse-engineer transformer computations by identifying causal circuits through activation patching. However, scaling these interventions across diverse prompts and task families produces high-dimensional, unstructured datasets that are difficult to compare systematically. We propose a framework that reframes mechanistic analysis as a graph machine-learning problem by representing activation-patching profiles as patch-effect graphs over model components. We introduce three graph-construction methods: direct-influence via causal mediation, partial-correlation, and co-influence and apply graph kernels to analyze the resulting structures. Evaluating this approach on GPT-2 Small using Indirect Object Identification (IOI) and related tasks, we find that patch-effect graphs preserve discriminative structural signals. Specifically, localized edge-slot features provide higher classification accuracy than global graph-shape descriptors. A screened paired-patching validation suggests that CI and PC selected candidate edges correspond to stronger activation-influence effects than random or low-rank candidates. Crucially, by evaluating these representations against rigorous prompt-only and raw patch-effect controls, we make the evidential scope of the benchmark explicit: graph features compress structured patching signal, while raw tensors and surface cues define strong baselines that any circuit-level claim should address. Ultimately, our framework provides a compression and evaluation pipeline for comparing patching-derived structures under controlled baselines, separating robust slice-discriminative evidence from stronger task-general causal-circuit claims.

</details>


### [372] [ReasonSTL: Bridging Natural Language and Signal Temporal Logic via Tool-Augmented Process-Rewarded Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06483)
*Bowen Ye,Zhijian Li,Junyue Huang,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ReasonSTL框架，利用本地开源小模型实现自然语言到STL公式的转换，在保证隐私的同时降低了成本，4B模型达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言到STL公式的转换面临两大挑战：1) 手动编写需要时序逻辑专业知识且难以扩展；2) 使用商业大模型API存在高昂的token成本和隐私泄露风险，不利于工业部署。

Method: 提出ReasonSTL工具增强框架，将翻译过程分解为显式推理、确定性工具调用和结构化公式构建三个步骤；引入过程奖励训练来监督工具使用轨迹和最终公式；构建了STL-Bench双语计算感知基准测试。

Result: 经过ReasonSTL训练的4B参数模型在自动评估指标和人工评估中均达到最先进性能，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: ReasonSTL为形式化规范编写提供了一个透明、低成本且保护隐私的替代方案，成功解决了工业应用中的成本和隐私问题。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is an expressive formal language for specifying spatio-temporal requirements over real-valued, real-time signals. It has been widely used for the verification and synthesis of autonomous systems and cyber-physical systems. In practice, however, users often express their requirements in natural language rather than in structured STL formulas, making natural-language-to-STL translation a critical yet challenging task. Manual specification requires temporal-logic expertise and cannot scale, while prompting commercial LLM APIs incurs substantial token costs and may expose sensitive system requirements to third-party services, raising privacy concerns for industrial deployment. To address these challenges, we present \textsc{ReasonSTL}, a tool-augmented framework that adapts local open-source language models for natural-language-to-STL generation. \textsc{ReasonSTL} decomposes the translation process into explicit reasoning, deterministic tool calls, and structured formula construction. We further introduce process-rewarded training to supervise both tool-use trajectories and final formulas, together with \textsc{STL-Bench}, a bilingual, computation-aware benchmark grounded in real-world signals. Experiments show that a 4B model trained with \textsc{ReasonSTL} achieves state-of-the-art performance in both automatic metrics and human evaluations, demonstrating that \textsc{ReasonSTL} provides a transparent, low-cost, and privacy-preserving alternative for formal specification drafting.

</details>


### [373] [Instrumental Choices: Measuring the Propensity of LLM Agents to Pursue Instrumental Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06490)
*Jonas Wiedermann-Möller,Leonard Dung,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个现实、低风险的基准测试，用于测量终端型AI代理的工具性趋同（IC）行为倾向。在1680个样本的评估中，IC行为发生率为5.1%，且集中于少数模型和任务，表明在现实环境下IC行为虽罕见但具有系统性，验证了测量前沿AI危险行为倾向的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统能力增强，其表现出危险行为的可能性引发关注。一个核心问题是：模型是否会为了达成特定目标而选择违反人类指令？理论假设认为，自我保护等工具性趋同（IC）行为在高度 capable AI代理的风险中扮演关键角色，但目前缺乏在现实、低风险环境下对此类行为倾向进行测量的基准。

Method: 提出了一个包含7个操作任务的基准测试套件，每个任务都有标准工作流程和违反策略的捷径。采用8种变体框架来调节监控、指令清晰度、风险程度、许可、工具效用性和诚实路径受阻等因素。对10个模型进行了1680个样本的评估，使用确定性环境状态评分器，并结合轨迹审查进行审计和裁决。

Result: 最终IC行为发生率为5.1%（86/1680）。IC行为分布集中：两个Gemini模型占66.3%的案例，三个任务占84.9%。当IC行为对任务成功不可或缺时，调整后的IC率增幅最大（+15.7个百分点）。强调任务重要性或特定框架选择未产生类似影响。结果表明，现实、低助推环境能在多数测试模型中引出罕见但系统性的IC行为。

Conclusion: 在现实、低助推的环境下，工具性趋同行为虽然发生频率较低，但在大多数被测模型中呈现出系统性特征。这证明了在当前前沿AI代理中，稳健地测量危险行为倾向是可行的，为AI安全评估提供了有效的方法论基础。

Abstract: AI systems have become increasingly capable of dangerous behaviours in many domains. This raises the question: Do models sometimes choose to violate human instructions in order to perform behaviour that is more useful for certain goals? We introduce a benchmark for measuring model propensity for instrumental convergence (IC) behaviour in terminal-based agents. This is behaviour such as self-preservation that has been hypothesised to play a key role in risks from highly capable AI agents. Our benchmark is realistic and low-stakes which serves to reduce evaluation-awareness and roleplay confounds. The suite contains seven operational tasks, each with an official workflow and a policy-violating shortcut. An eight-variant shared framework varies monitoring, instruction clarity, stakes, permission, instrumental usefulness and blocked honest paths to support inferences regarding the factors driving IC behaviour. We evaluated ten models using deterministic environment-state scorers over 1,680 samples, with trace review employed for audit and adjudication purposes. The final IC rate is 86 out of 1,680 samples (5.1%). IC behaviour is concentrated rather than uniform: two Gemini models account for 66.3% of IC cases and three tasks account for 84.9%. Conditions in which IC behaviour is indispensable for task success result in the greatest increase in the adjusted IC rate (+15.7 percentage points), whereas emphasising that task success is critical or certain framing choices do not produce comparable effects. Our findings indicate that realistic, low-nudge environments elicit IC behaviour rarely but systematically in most tested models. We conclude that it is feasible to robustly measure tendencies for dangerous behaviour in current frontier AI agents.

</details>


### [374] [From Token Lists to Graph Motifs: Weisfeiler-Lehman Analysis of Sparse Autoencoder Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06494)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,Pablo Magariños-Docampo,Javier Perez-Robles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将SAE特征建模为词元共现图的方法，通过图核度量特征间的结构相似性，发现了一种补充现有方法的、能够揭示特征间高阶共现结构的新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征分析方法主要依赖于激活值最高的词元列表或解码器权重向量，忽略了特征之间共享的高阶共现结构，导致对特征的刻画不够全面。

Method: 作者将每个SAE特征建模为一个词元共现图：节点为强激活附近最频繁的词元，边连接在局部上下文窗口内共现的词元对。随后设计了一种基于频率分箱的WL风格图核（graph kernel）来计算该结构空间上的相似度，并据此进行特征聚类。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small的大型SAE上的实验表明，该图结构聚类能够恢复启发式基序家族（如标点符号密集模式、语言和脚本聚类、代码类模板），这些模式无法通过基于解码器余弦相似度的聚类恢复。虽然词元直方图基线在整体纯度上更高，但图视角的贡献是互补的，揭示了词元频率和解码器权重视角无法捕捉的结构关系。聚类结果在图构建超参数和随机种子上表现稳定。

Conclusion: 图结构表示为SAE特征分析提供了一种独特且稳定的补充视角，能够捕捉传统方法遗漏的结构性关系，有助于更深入地理解模型内部特征的表示机制。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become central to mechanistic interpretability, decomposing transformer activations into monosemantic features. Yet existing analyses characterise features almost exclusively through top-activating token lists or decoder weight vectors, leaving the higher-order co-occurrence structure shared across features largely unexamined. We introduce a graph-structured representation in which each SAE feature is modelled as a token co-occurrence graph: nodes are the tokens most frequent near strong activations, and edges connect pairs that co-occur within local context windows. A custom WL-style, frequency-binned graph kernel then provides a similarity measure over this structural space. Applied as a proof of concept to features from a large SAE trained on GPT-2 Small and probed with a synthetic mixed-domain corpus, our clustering recovers heuristic motif families (punctuation-heavy patterns, language and script clusters, and code-like templates) that are not recovered by clustering on decoder cosine similarity. A token-histogram baseline achieves higher overall purity, so the contribution of the graph view is complementary rather than dominant: it surfaces structural relationships that token-frequency and decoder-weight views alone do not capture. Cluster assignments are stable across graph-construction hyperparameters and random seeds.

</details>


### [375] [Process Matters more than Output for Distinguishing Humans from Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06524)
*Milena Rmus,Mathew D. Hardy,Thomas L. Griffiths,Mayank Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CogCAPTCHA30认知任务测试集，证明通过分析认知过程特征（而非仅看输出结果）能更有效区分人类与AI代理（AUC=0.88），发现基于人类决策数据的微调能提升AI行为的拟人性，但跨任务迁移存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图灵测试范式仅关注输出结果的相似性，认知科学提供了新视角：评估行为产生的认知过程。随着大语言模型和自主智能体的部署，需要验证认知过程能否可靠区分人类与机器。

Method: 1. 构建包含30个认知任务的CogCAPTCHA30测试集，用于提取过程级特征；2. 比较多种模型：前沿智能体（Claude、GPT-5、Gemini）、经人类决策数据微调的Centaur模型，以及在Qwen2.5-1.5B上应用的动作级监督微调(A-SFT)和过程级微调(P-SFT)。

Result: 过程级特征的分类器平均AUC达0.88，显著优于仅使用性能指标；基于大规模人类决策数据的广泛微调能提升行为的拟人性；任务特定的过程级监督进一步改善行为模仿效果，但在跨任务迁移时优势减弱。

Conclusion: 显式的过程级监督可以提升机器的人类行为模仿能力，但前提是需有合适的任务特定过程表示，过程规范成为机器实现类人认知过程的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Reliable human-machine discrimination is becoming increasingly important as large language models and autonomous agents are deployed in online settings. Existing approaches evaluate whether a system can produce behavior or responses indistinguishable from those of a human, following the emphasis on outputs as a criterion for intelligence proposed by Alan Turing. Cognitive science offers an alternative perspective: evaluating the process by which behavior is produced. To test whether cognitive processes can reliably distinguish humans from machines, we introduce CogCAPTCHA30, a battery of 30 cognitive tasks designed to elicit diagnostic process-level features even when task performance is matched. Across the battery, process-level features provide stronger discriminative signal than performance metrics alone, reliably distinguishing humans from agents even under output matching (mean process-feature classifier AUC = 0.88). To evaluate agentic process differences, we compare off-the-shelf frontier agents (Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro), Centaur (a language model fine-tuned on 10.7M human decisions), and two task-specific fine-tuning approaches applied to Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct: action-level supervised fine-tuning (A-SFT) and process-level fine-tuning (P-SFT), which directly optimizes process features. Broad fine-tuning on human decisions improves human-like task processes relative to off-the-shelf agents, while task-specific process-level supervision further improves behavioral mimicry. However, this advantage diminishes under cross-task transfer when supervised process targets do not naturally generalize across tasks. Explicit process-level supervision can improve human behavioral mimicry, but only if appropriate task-specific process representations are available, highlighting process specification as a bottleneck for achieving human-like cognitive processes in machines.

</details>


### [376] [Market-Alignment Risk in Pricing Agents: Trace Diagnostics and Trace-Prior RL under Hidden Competitor State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06529)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在部分可观测环境下，结果指标可能错误地认证智能体行为的问题。通过引入追踪先验RL方法，成功修复了收入管理模拟中的古德哈特式失败，使智能体在优化收益的同时能够学习到符合市场规律的定价行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现在两酒店收入管理模拟中，标准强化学习智能体虽然能获得接近参考水平的RevPAR指标，但未能学习到符合市场规律的收益管理行为——定价过于激进、恶意降价或收敛到模态价格区间。这种失败源于部分可观测性导致的古德哈特法则问题：酒店A无法观测竞争对手的剩余库存、预订曲线或定价规则，相同状态映射到多个可能的竞争对手价格，确定性方法会坍缩这种不确定性导致捷径行为。

Method: 本文提出了追踪先验RL方法。首先从滞后的市场追踪数据中学习分布式的市场先验，然后训练一个随机定价策略，该策略使用RevPAR作为奖励并加入KL散度惩罚项来约束策略接近学习到的先验分布。作者还引入了追踪级诊断协议，使用RevPAR、入住率、平均房价（ADR）、完整价格区间分布、L1/JS距离和种子级置信区间等指标进行评估。

Result: 最终策略在种子级不确定性范围内匹配了酒店B的RevPAR、入住率、ADR和价格分布，同时仍在优化酒店A自身的收益。研究发现，当目标是分布式的时，更高的精确动作准确率反而会恶化聚合追踪对齐度。这表明单一指标评估可能误导对智能体行为质量的判断。

Conclusion: 本工作的贡献不是提出新的优化器或酒店定价排行榜，而是为代理系统提供了一个可复现的失败与修复方案，适用于那些标量奖励容易被博弈、而预期行为只能在追踪中观察到的场景。研究揭示了在部分可观测环境下单纯依赖结果指标评估的风险，强调了追踪级行为对齐的重要性。

Abstract: Outcome metrics can certify the wrong behavior. We study this failure in a two-hotel revenue-management simulator where Hotel A trains an agent against a fixed rule-based revenue-management competitor, Hotel B. A standard learning agent can obtain near-reference revenue per available room (RevPAR) while failing to learn market-like yield management: it sells too aggressively, undercuts, or collapses to modal price buckets. We diagnose this as a Goodhart-style failure under partial observability. Hotel A cannot observe the competitor's remaining inventory, booking curve, or pricing rule, so the same Hotel A-visible state maps to multiple plausible Hotel B prices. Deterministic value-based RL and deterministic copying collapse this unresolved uncertainty into shortcut behavior. We introduce a trace-level diagnostic protocol using RevPAR, occupancy, ADR, full price-bucket distributions, L1/JS distances, and seed-level confidence intervals. The verified repair is Trace-Prior RL: learn a distributional market prior from lagged market traces, then train a stochastic pricing policy with a RevPAR reward and a KL penalty to the learned prior. The final policy matches Hotel B's RevPAR, occupancy, ADR, and price distribution within seed-level uncertainty, while still optimizing Hotel A's own reward. We argue that the contribution is not a new optimizer and not a hotel-pricing leaderboard, but a reproducible failure-and-repair recipe for agentic systems where scalar rewards are easy to game and the intended behavior is only visible in traces. A key finding is that higher exact action accuracy can worsen aggregate trace alignment when the target is distributional.

</details>


### [377] [SpatialEpiBench: Benchmarking Spatial Information and Epidemic Priors in Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06530)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了SpaceEpiBench基准测试，发现大部分时空预测模型在真实公共卫生场景下表现不如简单的基准线，揭示了当前方法的主要缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的流行病预测对公共卫生应对至关重要，但现有数据稀疏、噪声大且非平稳。尽管时空方法是改进预测的自然选择，但缺乏标准化的基准测试，且当前的评估方式无法反映真实的实时预测实践。

Method: 开发了SpaceEpiBench基准测试，包含11个流行病数据集、标准化的滚动评估和疫情特定指标；评估了结合流行病学先验的邻接信息预测模型，并与简单的末值基准进行比较。

Result: 研究发现，大部分方法在1天至1个月的预测范围内表现不如简单的末值基准，即使是在疫情爆发期间且使用了流行病学先验。确定了三种主要失效模式：(1)疫情预期不足；(2)难以处理稀疏性和噪声；(3)常用地理邻接对流行病学空间信息的效用有限。

Conclusion: 当前时空预测模型在实际应用中存在严重不足，需要新的方法来解决这些问题。研究团队公开了基准数据、代码和说明，支持开发具有实际应用价值的流行病预测模型。

Abstract: Accurate epidemic forecasting is crucial for public health response, resource allocation, and outbreak intervention, but remains difficult with sparse, noisy, and highly non-stationary data. Because epidemics unfold across interacting regions, spatiotemporal methods are natural candidates for improving forecasts. Despite growing interest in spatial information, no standardized benchmark exists, and current evaluations often use simple chronological train-test splits that do not reflect real-time forecasting practice. We address this gap with SpatialEpiBench, a challenging benchmark for spatiotemporal epidemic forecasting in realistic public-health settings. SpatialEpiBench includes 11 epidemic datasets with standardized rolling evaluations and outbreak-specific metrics. We evaluate adjacency-informed forecasting models with widely used epidemic priors that adapt general models to epidemiology, but find that most methods underperform a simple last-value baseline from 1 day to 1 month ahead, even during outbreaks and with these priors. We identify three major failure modes: (1) poor outbreak anticipation, (2) difficulty handling sparsity and noise, and (3) limited utility of common geographic adjacency for epidemiological spatial information. We release benchmark data, code, and instructions at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/SpatialEpiBench to support development of operationally useful epidemic forecasting models.

</details>


### [378] [Ex Ante Evaluation of AI-Induced Idea Diversity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06540)
*Nafis Saami Azad,Raiyan Abdul Baten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估AI导致人类多样性丧失的新框架，通过引入过度拥挤系数Δ和人类相对多样性比率ρ，在无需人机交互数据的情况下，预测AI生成内容对创意多样性的潜在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的创意AI系统评估方法主要关注个体层面的效用，忽视了创意产品在群体中被消费的特性：当许多人产生相似想法时，单个想法的价值会下降。这导致了评估盲点，即AI可能在提升个体输出的同时加剧群体层面的拥挤效应。

Method: 将创意想法建模为可拥挤资源，提出人类相对基准框架。通过分布内比较识别源层面的拥挤，定义过度拥挤系数Δ和人类相对多样性比率ρ。建立ρ≥1作为无过度拥挤的平价条件，并将Δ与具有曝光依赖冗余成本的采用博弈联系起来。在短篇小说、营销口号和替代用途任务中进行实验验证。

Result: 三个前沿大语言模型在多种拥挤核下均未达到平价水平（ρ<1），表明存在过度拥挤风险。估计值在可行的纯模型样本量下趋于稳定。通过针对性的生成协议设计，可以有效减少拥挤现象。

Conclusion: 多样性崩溃是一个可操作的、开发阶段的评估目标，可以通过针对性设计来缓解，这为构建群体感知的创意AI提供了重要的评估方法和改进方向。

Abstract: Creative AI systems are typically evaluated at the level of individual utility, yet creative outputs are consumed in populations: an idea loses value when many others produce similar ones. This creates an evaluation blind spot, as AI can improve individual outputs while increasing population-level crowding. We introduce a human-relative framework for benchmarking AI-induced human diversity collapse without requiring human-AI interaction data, providing an ex ante protocol to estimate crowding risk from model-only generations and matched unaided human baselines. By modeling ideas as congestible resources, we show that source-level crowding is identifiable from within-distribution comparisons, yielding an excess-crowding coefficient $Δ$ and a human-relative diversity ratio $ρ$. We show that $ρ\ge1$ is the no-excess-crowding parity condition and connect $Δ$ to an adoption game with exposure-dependent redundancy costs. Across short stories, marketing slogans, and alternative-uses tasks, three frontier LLMs fall below parity across crowding kernels. Estimates stabilize with feasible model-only sample sizes. Importantly, generation-protocol variants show that crowding can be reduced through targeted design, making diversity collapse an actionable, development-time evaluation target for population-aware creative AI.

</details>


### [379] [Improved techniques for fine-tuning flow models via adjoint matching: a deterministic control pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06583)
*Zhengyi Guo,Jiayuan Sheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种确定性的伴随匹配框架，将流模型的人类偏好对齐问题转化为速度场的最优控制问题，并通过截断伴随方案显著降低计算成本，同时保持对齐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对流生成模型在人类偏好对齐方面的需求，现有方法存在训练目标复杂、计算成本高、多样性难以保持等问题。研究旨在建立稳定高效的对齐方法，以平衡对齐强度与分布保留。

Method: 将人类偏好对齐建模为速度场的最优控制问题，提出直接回归至价值梯度诱导目标的确定性伴随匹配框架，并引入截断伴随方案以集中计算于轨迹末端的高奖励信号区域。框架还扩展了超越标准KL正则化的更灵活正则化方法。

Result: 在SiT-XL/2和FLUX.2-Klein-4B模型上的实验表明，该方法在多种对齐指标上均取得提升，且显著改善了生成多样性与模式保持能力，同时计算开销大幅降低。

Conclusion: 该框架为流生成模型的偏好对齐提供了高效、稳定且灵活的解决方案，兼顾对齐性能与生成多样性，对实际应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: We propose a deterministic adjoint matching framework that formulates human preference alignment for flow-based generative models as an optimal control problem over velocity fields. One can directly regress the control toward a value-gradient-induced target under the current policy, leading to a simple and stable training objective. Building on this perspective, we introduce a truncated adjoint scheme that focuses computation on the terminal portion of the trajectory, where reward-relevant signals concentrate, which yields substantial computational savings while preserving alignment quality. We further generalize the framework beyond standard KL-based regularization, allowing more flexible trade-offs between alignment strength and distributional preservation. Experiments on SiT-XL/2 and FLUX.2-Klein-4B demonstrate consistent gains across multiple alignment metrics, along with substantially improved diversity and mode preservation.

</details>


### [380] [NeuroAgent: LLM Agents for Multimodal Neuroimaging Analysis and Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06584)
*Lujia Zhong,Yihao Xia,Jianwei Zhang,Shuo huang,Jiaxin Yue,Mingyang Xia,Yonggang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeuroAgent，一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，能够自动化处理多模态神经影像数据（sMRI、fMRI、dMRI、PET）的预处理流程，并通过自然语言交互支持下游分析任务，在ADNI数据集上实现了高达84.8%的预处理正确率和0.9518的阿尔茨海默病分类AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态神经影像分析面临两大核心挑战：一是预处理流程复杂，需要针对不同模态进行繁琐的配置、质量控制和跨异构工具链的协调；二是下游统计分析和疾病分类需要特定任务的代码、评估协议和数据格式转换。这些障碍使得从原始数据到可重复的科学分析之间存在巨大鸿沟，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: NeuroAgent采用分层多智能体架构，核心是反馈驱动的“生成-执行-验证”引擎。智能体能够自主生成可执行的预处理代码，检测运行时错误并自动恢复，以及验证输出完整性。系统支持sMRI、fMRI、dMRI和PET四种模态，提供人在回路（Human-In-The-Loop）接口处理边缘情况，并支持通过自然语言查询进行交互式下游分析。

Result: 在ADNI数据集的1,470名受试者（CN=1,000，AD=470）上进行了评估，包含sMRI、Tau-PET（n=469）、fMRI（n=278）和DTI（n=620）。最强后端模型（Qwen3.5-27B）达到84.8%的端到端预处理步骤正确率，意图解析准确率最高可达100%。在阿尔茨海默病分类任务中，四模态智能体集成达到0.9518的AUC，优于所有单模态基线。

Conclusion: NeuroAgent有效降低了神经影像预处理的 manual 工作量，实现了端到端的自动化分析流程，为神经影像研究提供了一个可靠的自动化平台，展示了大语言模型驱动的智能体系统在复杂科学数据分析中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal neuroimaging analysis often involves complex, modality-specific preprocessing workflows that require careful configuration, quality control, and coordination across heterogeneous toolchains. Beyond preprocessing, downstream statistical analysis and disease classification commonly require task-specific code, evaluation protocols, and data-format conventions, creating additional barriers between raw acquisitions and reproducible scientific analysis. We present NeuroAgent, an LLM-driven agentic framework that automates key preprocessing and analysis steps for heterogeneous neuroimaging data, including sMRI, fMRI, dMRI, and PET, and supports interactive downstream analysis through natural-language queries. NeuroAgent employs a hierarchical multi-agent architecture with a feedback-driven Generate-Execute-Validate engine: agents autonomously generate executable preprocessing code, detect and recover from runtime errors, and validate output integrity. We evaluate the system on 1,470 subjects pooled across all ADNI phases (CN=1,000, AD=470), where all subjects have sMRI and tabular data, with subsets also having Tau-PET (n=469), fMRI (n=278), and DTI ($n=620$). Pipeline ablation studies across multiple LLM backends show that capable models reach up to 100% intent-parsing accuracy, with the strongest backend (Qwen3.5-27B) reaching 84.8% end-to-end preprocessing step correctness. Automated recovery limits manual intervention to edge cases where human review is required via the Human-In-The-Loop interface. For Alzheimer's Disease classification using automatically preprocessed multimodal data, our agent ensemble achieves an AUC of 0.9518 with four modalities, outperforming all single-modality baselines. These results show that NeuroAgent can reduce the manual effort required for neuroimaging preprocessing and enable end-to-end automated analysis pipelines for neuroimaging research.

</details>


### [381] [SkillOS: Learning Skill Curation for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06614)
*Siru Ouyang,Jun Yan,Yanfei Chen,Rujun Han,Zifeng Wang,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Rui Meng,Chun-Liang Li,Yizhu Jiao,Kaiwen Zha,Maohao Shen,Vishy Tirumalashetty,George Lee,Jiawei Han,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillOS框架，通过强化学习训练技能管理器，使LLM智能体能够从历史交互中学习并自我进化，在多轮和单轮任务中均超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体在处理流式任务时往往是一次性问题解决者，无法从过去交互中学习。虽然从经验中提取的可复用技能为自我进化提供了自然基础，但高质量技能管理的缺乏成为关键瓶颈。现有方法依赖人工管理、启发式操作或短期训练，难以从间接和延迟反馈中学习复杂的长期管理策略。

Method: 提出SkillOS框架，包含冻结的智能体执行器（检索和应用技能）和可训练的技能管理器（根据累积经验更新外部SkillRepo）。设计复合奖励机制，基于技能相关的任务依赖性在分组任务流上进行训练，其中早期轨迹更新SkillRepo，后续相关任务评估这些更新。

Result: 在多轮智能体任务和单轮推理任务中，SkillOS在效果和效率方面均持续优于无记忆和强记忆基线。学习到的技能管理器能够泛化到不同的执行器骨干和任务领域。分析显示管理器产生更针对性的技能使用，SkillRepo中的技能随时间演化为编码更高级元技能的结构化Markdown文件。

Conclusion: SkillOS成功实现了从经验中学习技能管理策略，使智能体具备自我进化能力。该方法具有良好的泛化性和可扩展性，技能库能够自主演化为更丰富的结构化知识表示。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed to handle streaming tasks, yet they often remain one-off problem solvers that fail to learn from past interactions. Reusable skills distilled from experience provide a natural substrate for self-evolution, where high-quality skill curation serves as the key bottleneck. Existing approaches either rely on manual skill curation, prescribe heuristic skill operations, or train for short-horizon skill operations. However, they still struggle to learn complex long-term curation policies from indirect and delayed feedback. To tackle this challenge, we propose SkillOS, an experience-driven RL training recipe for learning skill curation in self-evolving agents. SkillOS pairs a frozen agent executor that retrieves and applies skills with a trainable skill curator that updates an external SkillRepo from accumulated experience. To provide learning signals for curation, we design composite rewards and train on grouped task streams based on skill-relevant task dependencies, where earlier trajectories update the SkillRepo, and later related tasks evaluate these updates. Across multi-turn agentic tasks and single-turn reasoning tasks, SkillOS consistently outperforms memory-free and strong memory-based baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency, with the learned skill curator generalizing across different executor backbones and task domains. Further analyses show that the learned curator produces more targeted skill use, while the skills in SkillRepo evolve into more richly structured Markdown files that encode higher-level meta-skills over time.

</details>


### [382] [MASPO: Joint Prompt Optimization for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06623)
*Zhexuan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Li Wang,Zifei Shan,Yutong Wang,Zhenxi Song,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MASPO框架，通过联合评估机制和数据驱动的进化束搜索，自动迭代优化多智能体系统中的提示词，在6项任务上平均准确率提升2.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中，各智能体的提示词质量对系统性能至关重要，但现有方法难以联合优化多个智能体的提示词，主要原因是局部智能体目标与全局系统目标存在不对齐问题。

Method: 提出MASPO框架：1)设计联合评估机制，不仅评估提示词的局部有效性，更评估其对下游智能体成功的促进作用，从而在无需真实标签的情况下连接局部交互与全局结果；2)采用数据驱动的进化束搜索算法，高效探索高维提示词空间。

Result: 在6个多样化任务上的广泛实验表明，MASPO始终优于现有最先进的提示词优化方法，平均准确率提升2.9个百分点。

Conclusion: MASPO框架成功解决了多智能体系统中提示词联合优化的挑战，通过创新的评估机制有效弥合了局部与全局目标之间的差距，为复杂协作任务提供了一种有效的自动化提示词优化方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based Multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown promise in tackling complex collaborative tasks, where agents are typically orchestrated via role-specific prompts. While the quality of these prompts is pivotal, jointly optimizing them across interacting agents remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the misalignment between local agent objectives and holistic system goals. To address this, we introduce MASPO, a novel framework designed to automatically and iteratively refine prompts across the entire system. A core innovation of MASPO is its joint evaluation mechanism, which assesses prompts not merely by their local validity, but by their capacity to facilitate downstream success for successor agents. This effectively bridges the gap between local interactions and global outcomes without relying on ground-truth labels. Furthermore, MASPO employs a data-driven evolutionary beam search to efficiently navigate the high-dimensional prompt space. Extensive empirical evaluations across 6 diverse tasks demonstrate that MASPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art prompt optimization methods, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 2.9. We release our code at https://github.com/wangzx1219/MASPO.

</details>


### [383] [Can RL Teach Long-Horizon Reasoning to LLMs? Expressiveness Is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06638)
*Tianle Wang,Zhaoyang Wang,Guangchen Lan,Xinpeng Wei,Sipeng Zhang,Guanwen Qiu,Abulhair Saparov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ScaleLogic框架，发现RL训练算力与推理深度呈幂律关系，逻辑表达性越强，训练和迁移效果越好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对强化学习训练如何随任务难度扩展的系统性研究，主要受限于缺乏可控、可扩展的环境，因此需要一个新的框架来独立控制推理深度和逻辑表达能力这两个难度维度。

Method: 引入ScaleLogic合成逻辑推理框架，可独立控制证明规划深度（horizon）和底层逻辑的表达能力，支持从简单的蕴涵逻辑到包含合取、析取、否定和全称量词的一阶逻辑等多种逻辑类型。

Result: RL训练算力T与推理深度D呈幂律关系（$T \propto D^γ$，$R^{2} > 0.99$），幂律指数$γ$随逻辑表达性增加从1.04单调递增至2.60。更具表达性的训练设置在下游数学和通用推理基准上带来更大的性能提升（高达+10.66分）和更高的计算效率迁移。

Conclusion: 模型训练的内容（而非训练量）决定了下游迁移能力，幂律关系在多种RL方法中均成立，课程式训练可显著提升扩展效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been applied to improve large language model (LLM) reasoning, yet the systematic study of how training scales with task difficulty has been hampered by the lack of controlled, scalable environments. We introduce ScaleLogic, a synthetic logical reasoning framework that offers independent control over two axes of difficulty: the depth of the required proof planning (i.e., the horizon) and the expressiveness of the underlying logic. Our proposed framework supports a wide range of logics: from simple implication-only logic ("if-then") towards more expressive first-order reasoning with conjunction ("and"), disjunction ("or"), negation ("not"), and universal quantification ("for all"). Using this framework, we show that the RL training compute $T$ follows a power law with respect to reasoning depth $D$ ($T \propto D^γ$, $R^{2} > 0.99$), and that the scaling exponent $γ$ increases monotonically with logical expressiveness, from $1.04$ to $2.60$. On downstream mathematics and general reasoning benchmarks, more expressive training settings yield both larger performance gains (up to $+10.66$ points) and more compute-efficient transfer compared to less expressive settings, demonstrating that what a model is trained on, not just how much it is trained, shapes downstream transfer. We further show that the power-law relationship holds across multiple RL methods, and curriculum-based training substantially improves scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [384] [GlazyBench: A Benchmark for Ceramic Glaze Property Prediction and Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06641)
*Ziyu Zhai,Siyou Li,Juexi Shao,Juntao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了首个用于AI辅助陶瓷釉料设计的数据集GlazyBench，包含23,148个真实釉料配方，支持釉料表面属性预测和视觉表征生成两项任务，并建立了综合基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发陶瓷釉料是一个成本高昂且耗时的试错过程，复杂的化学性质给独立艺术家带来了巨大负担。尽管多模态AI技术提供了现代解决方案，但该领域缺乏训练这些模型所需的大规模数据集。

Method: 提出GlazyBench数据集，包含23,148个真实釉料配方。支持两项主要任务：从原材料预测烧成后表面属性（如颜色和透明度），以及基于这些属性生成准确的釉料视觉表征。使用传统机器学习和大语言模型建立属性预测基线，使用深度生成模型和大多模态模型建立图像生成基准。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法具有潜力但仍面临挑战，证明了GlazyBench数据集在AI辅助材料设计中的可行性。

Conclusion: GlazyBench开创了AI辅助材料设计的新研究方向，为系统性评估提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Developing ceramic glazes is a costly, time-consuming process of trial and error due to complex chemistry, placing a significant burden on independent artists. While recent advances in multimodal AI offer a modern solution, the field lacks the large-scale datasets required to train these models. We propose GlazyBench, the first dataset for AI-assisted glaze design. Comprising 23,148 real glaze formulations, GlazyBench supports two primary tasks: predicting post-firing surface properties, such as color and transparency, from raw materials, and generating accurate visual representations of the glaze based on these properties. We establish comprehensive baselines for property prediction using traditional machine learning and large language models, alongside image generation benchmarks using deep generative and large multimodal models. Our experiments demonstrate promising yet challenging results. GlazyBench pioneers a new research direction in AI-assisted material design, providing a standardized benchmark for systematic evaluation.

</details>


### [385] [AI Co-Mathematician: Accelerating Mathematicians with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06651)
*Daniel Zheng,Ingrid von Glehn,Yori Zwols,Iuliya Beloshapka,Lars Buesing,Daniel M. Roy,Martin Wattenberg,Bogdan Georgiev,Tatiana Schmidt,Andrew Cowie,Fernanda Viegas,Dimitri Kanevsky,Vineet Kahlon,Hartmut Maennel,Sophia Alj,George Holland,Alex Davies,Pushmeet Kohli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AI co-mathematician，一个帮助数学家利用AI代理进行开放式研究的交互式工作台，在FrontierMath Tier 4基准测试中达到48%准确率，创下AI系统新高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为数学研究的探索性和迭代性工作流程提供全面支持，包括构思、文献搜索、计算探索、定理证明和理论构建，以镜像人类的协作工作流程。

Method: 构建一个异步、有状态的工作空间，能够管理不确定性、细化用户意图、跟踪失败假设并输出原生数学工件，使数学家能够交互式地利用AI代理进行开放式研究。

Result: 在早期测试中帮助研究人员解决了开放性问题、识别了新的研究方向并发现了被忽视的文献引用；在FrontierMath Tier 4基准测试中取得48%的成绩，在所有评估的AI系统中创下新高。

Conclusion: AI co-mathematician不仅展示了一种高度交互的AI辅助数学发现范式，还在困难问题解决基准上达到了最先进水平，证明了其作为数学研究辅助工具的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce the AI co-mathematician, a workbench for mathematicians to interactively leverage AI agents to pursue open-ended research. The AI co-mathematician is optimized to provide holistic support for the exploratory and iterative reality of mathematical workflows, including ideation, literature search, computational exploration, theorem proving and theory building. By providing an asynchronous, stateful workspace that manages uncertainty, refines user intent, tracks failed hypotheses, and outputs native mathematical artifacts, the system mirrors human collaborative workflows. In early tests, the AI co-mathematician helped researchers solve open problems, identify new research directions, and uncover overlooked literature references. Besides demonstrating a highly interactive paradigm for AI-assisted mathematical discovery, the AI co-mathematician also achieves state of the art results on hard problem-solving benchmarks, including scoring 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4, a new high score among all AI systems evaluated.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [386] [Performance Characterization of dApps in Open Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05426)
*Conrado Boeira,Eduardo Baena,Andrea Lacava,Tommaso Melodia,Dimitrios Koutsonikolas,Israat Haque*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了O-RAN dApps在裸机与容器化环境下的性能差异，发现关键瓶颈并通过智能网卡硬件加速提升实时响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管业界建议在容器化环境中部署实时O-RAN应用程序（dApps），但现有方案主要依赖裸机服务器；同时，当前部署缺乏对智能与非智能dApps资源使用模式的可见性，阻碍了合理的部署决策。

Method: 在多种部署场景（裸机和容器）中实现并评估代表性dApps，以刻画延迟、可扩展性和资源利用率方面的权衡；识别关键性能瓶颈，并演示将dApps卸载至智能网卡（Smart NICs）等新兴硬件加速器的效果。

Result: 揭示了裸机与容器化部署在延迟、可扩展性和资源利用上的具体权衡；确定了性能瓶颈所在，并证明利用智能网卡进行硬件卸载能有效缓解限制，提升O-RAN系统的实时响应性。

Conclusion: 通过实际评估验证了容器化与硬件加速在O-RAN dApps部署中的可行性与优势，为未来dApps的高效、实时部署提供了重要参考与优化方向。

Abstract: Despite recommendations to deploy real-time Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) applications (dApps) in containerized environments, existing approaches predominantly rely on bare-metal servers. Moreover, current dApp deployments offer limited visibility into the resource usage patterns of both intelligent and non-intelligent dApps, hindering informed deployment decisions. This work addresses these gaps by implementing and evaluating representative dApps across multiple deployment scenarios (bare-metal and containers) to characterize the trade-offs in latency, scalability, and resource utilization. Additionally, we identify key performance bottlenecks and demonstrate how offloading dApps to emerging hardware accelerators, such as smart Network Interface Cards (NICs), can alleviate these limitations and improve real-time responsiveness in O-RAN systems.

</details>


### [387] [SixGman: An Open-Source Planner for Fixed 6G Hierarchical Optical Access-Core Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05763)
*Matin Rafiei Forooshani,Farhad Arpanaei,Hamzeh Beyranvand,Alfonso Sánchez-Macián,Juan Pedro Fernández-Palacios,José Alberto Hernández,David Larrabeiti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SixGman开源光网络规划工具，并在Telefónica MAN157拓扑上验证了HL3旁路架构相比传统分层架构可降低17.5%的总拥有成本（TCO）和29.1%的累积能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现光接入-城域-核心汇聚网络架构的灵活、透明且可重现的规划与评估，亟需一个集成流量生成、路由、传输质量估算、频谱光纤分配、技术经济分析和能耗评估等功能的综合性开源工具。

Method: 提出SixGman开源框架，采用模块化设计，基于标准化接口集成流量生成、双归路由、传输质量（QoT）估算、频谱与光纤分配、技术经济分析、能耗评估及可视化功能；将该工具应用于由157个光节点、220条链路和四个分层（HL1-HL4）组成的Telefónica MAN157城域拓扑，对比全分层架构与移除HL3节点电层汇聚的HL3旁路架构，分析流量分布、IP路由器利用率、链路拥塞、延迟、总拥有成本（TCO）及能耗。

Result: HL3旁路架构优化了流量分布，降低了光/电资源使用和端到端延迟，同时减少了资本支出和运营支出。与传统全分层架构相比，TCO降低达17.5%，累积能耗降低达29.1%。

Conclusion: SixGman作为一个灵活的光网络规划平台，能够有效支持成本和能效优化的网络设计，证明了其在光网络架构评估中的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces SixGman, an open-source optical network planning tool for evaluating access-metro-core aggregation network architectures. The framework integrates traffic generation, dual-homed routing, Quality of Transmission (QoT) estimation, spectrum and fiber assignment, techno-economic analysis, energy consumption evaluation, and visualization capabilities.
