Its modular design, based on standardized interfaces and clearly defined functions, enables flexible, transparent, and reproducible network simulations. SixGman is applied to the Telefónica MAN157 metro-urban topology, composed of 157 optical nodes, 220 links, and four hierarchical layers (HL1-HL4), to compare a conventional full hierarchical architecture with an HL3-bypassed architecture where electrical aggregation at HL3 nodes is removed. The analysis includes traffic distribution, IP router utilization, link congestion, latency, Total Cost of Ownership (TCO), and energy consumption. Results show that HL3 bypassing improves traffic distribution, reduces optical and electrical resource usage, lowers end-to-end latency, and decreases both capital and operational expenditures. Compared to the full hierarchical architecture, the HL3-bypassed scenario achieves reductions of up to 17.5% in TCO and 29.1% in cumulative energy consumption. These results demonstrate the potential of SixGman as a flexible planning platform for cost- and energy-efficient optical network design.

</details>


### [388] [Bridging the 6G Gap: Scaling Sustainable ROADM-Based IP-over-WDM via DSCM-Enabled Point-to-Multipoint Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05793)
*Matin Rafiei Forooshani,Farhad Arpanaei,Hamzeh Beyranvand,Mahdi Ranjbar Zefreh,Juan Pedro Fernández-Palacios,Alfonso Sánchez-Macián,José Alberto Hernández,David Larrabeiti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对比了三种接入-城域架构，发现PtMP IPoWDM架构在十年周期内可将CAPEX降低92.0%、功耗降低99.2%，显著优于传统方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估和对比不同光接入-城域融合架构的性能与经济性，研究旨在寻找更高效的解决方案以降低成本和能耗。

Method: 对比分析了基于转发器的架构、点对点（Point-to-Point）架构以及基于DSCM的点对多点（PtMP）接入-城域架构，并进行了为期十年的周期性评估。

Result: PtMP IPoWDM架构在不同地理类型场景中均展现出显著的效率优势；与传统基准相比，十年周期内CAPEX降低92.0%，功耗降低99.2%。

Conclusion: PtMP IPoWDM架构是一种极具优势的接入-城域融合方案，能够在大规模部署中实现显著的成本节约和能效提升。

Abstract: This study compares transponder-based, Point-to-Point, and DSCM-based Point-to-Multipoint (PtMP) access-metro architectures. Findings demonstrate that PtMP IPoWDM significantly optimizes efficiency across diverse geotypes, slashing CAPEX by 92.0% and power by 99.2% compared to the traditional benchmark over a ten-year horizon.

</details>


### [389] [Comparative Analysis of Direct-to-Cell (D2C) and 3GPP Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN) for Global Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05843)
*Donglin Wang,Anjie Qiu,Qiuheng Zhou,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了D2C（利用现有频谱和未改装终端）和3GPP NTN（标准化卫星原生框架）两种卫星通信架构。结论是：D2C利于快速市场准入，但NTN在性能、安全性和可扩展性上更优，是6G星地融合的基础；两者结合的混合模式是实现全球连接的最佳路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对无处不在的移动覆盖需求的增长，业界涌现出两种截然不同的架构范式：由SpaceX等推动的D2C和标准化的3GPP NTN。为了理清这两种方案的优劣并为未来网络部署提供指导，有必要对它们进行全面的技术比较与权衡分析。

Method: 本文从标准化演进路径、网络架构、物理层创新、安全态势和运营权衡等多个维度，对D2C和3GPP NTN进行了综合技术对比。此外，还结合自动驾驶等6G应用场景，探讨了结合地面5G、NTN宽带和D2C应急回退的混合三链路架构。

Result: 分析表明，D2C凭借对传统设备的兼容性能实现快速的市场切入；而3GPP NTN则提供了更优越的性能、安全性和可扩展性。虽然两者路径不同，但结合两者优势的混合模型被认为是实现真正全球连接最切实可行的路径。

Conclusion: NTN凭借其系统性和长期可扩展性，被定位为6G卫星-地面融合的基础框架。而D2C作为有效的补充方案，与NTN结合的混合模式将共同推动未来全球无缝连接的实现，特别是在自动驾驶等安全关键型场景中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: The quest for ubiquitous mobile coverage has catalyzed two fundamentally distinct architectural paradigms: Direct-to-Cell (D2C) and standardized 3GPP Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN). D2C, pioneered by SpaceX Starlink and AST SpaceMobile, leverages existing terrestrial spectrum and unmodified consumer handsets to provide emergency connectivity as a market-driven overlay. In contrast, 3GPP NTN, standardized across Releases 17-19, offers a systematic satellite-native framework designed for long-term scalability, high-throughput broadband, and deep integration with terrestrial 5G/6G networks. This paper presents a comprehensive technical comparison of these approaches, analyzing their standardization trajectories, network architectures, physical-layer innovations, security postures, and operational trade-offs. We further examine their implications for emerging 6G use cases, particularly autonomous driving, where safety-critical redundancy motivates a hybrid tri-link architecture combining terrestrial 5G, NTN broadband, and D2C emergency fallback. Our analysis shows that, although D2C enables rapid market entry through legacy-device compatibility, NTN provides superior performance, security, and scalability, positioning it as the foundational framework for 6G satellite-terrestrial convergence. A hybrid model that combines the strengths of both paradigms is identified as the most practical path toward truly global connectivity.

</details>


### [390] [A Disaster-Aware Integrated TN-NTN System-Level Simulator for Resilient 6G Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05852)
*Donglin Wang,Anjie Qiu,Qiuheng Zhou,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个轻量级系统级仿真器，用于评估灾害场景下地面网络（TN）与非地面网络（NTN）融合的故障回落行为与韧性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对极端事件和灾害场景，3GPP将NTN（包括LEO卫星、HAPS和UAV）标准化为未来6G系统的关键组件以增强覆盖和韧性，但目前缺乏在灾害模型下评估TN-NTN融合网络故障回落行为的仿真工具。

Method: 提出一个遵循3GPP Rel-17/18建模原则的轻量级系统级仿真器，支持概率性地面gNB故障建模、服务向NTN迁移，并在不同用户负载、灾害严重程度和NTN配置水平下对比分析吞吐量、分组接收率（PRR）和时延。

Result: 仿真结果揭示了地面运行的容量-时延权衡、非地面服务的可靠性与稳定性，以及混合TN-NTN运行的平衡韧性表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为研究未来6G移动系统中无线网络韧性和流量管理提供了一个易于处理的仿真工具。

Abstract: Non-terrestrial networks (NTN) have been standardized by the 3rd generation partnership project (3GPP) as a key component of future 6G systems to enhance coverage and resilience. In particular, NTN technologies such as low-earth orbit (LEO) satellites, high-altitude platform stations (HAPS), and unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are expected to support terrestrial networks (TN) during extreme events and disasters. In this paper, we present a lightweight system-level simulator for evaluating post-failure fallback behavior in integrated TN-NTN wireless networks under a partial-failure disaster model. The simulator follows 3GPP Rel-17/18 modeling principles, supports probabilistic terrestrial next-generation node B (gNB) failures, and service migration to NTN. The simulator supports comparative analysis of throughput, packet reception ratio (PRR), and latency under different user loads, disaster severities, and NTN provisioning levels. Results show the expected capacity-delay tradeoff of terrestrial operation, the reliability and stability of non-terrestrial service, and the balanced resilience behavior of hybrid TN-NTN operation. The proposed framework provides a tractable tool for studying wireless network resilience and traffic management in future integrated 6G mobile systems.

</details>


### [391] [FluxShard: Motion-Aware Feature Cache Reuse for Collaborative Video Analytics in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06027)
*Xiuxian Guan,Zongyuan Zhang,Zheng Lin,Zekai Sun,Tianyang Duan,Zihan Fang,Rui Wang,Heming Cui,Wei Ni,Jun Luo,Yuanwei Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: FluxShard提出了一种基于运动感知的边缘-云视频分析系统，通过利用编解码器块级运动向量以运动区域粒度管理特征缓存，解决了非均匀运动场景下的缓存失效问题，在保持精度的同时将延迟降低32.6-83.8%，能耗降低14.9-64.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘云视频分析方法以整个场景为单位管理特征缓存，而移动场景中存在非均匀运动模式。这种粒度不匹配导致即使大部分内容仅发生空间位移，缓存也会大面积失效，造成计算和带宽浪费。

Method: FluxShard利用编解码器块级运动向量以运动区域粒度管理特征缓存重用与重计算。核心技术包括：(1)基于运动向量重索引缓存特征，分离空间位移与内容变化；(2)提出感受野对齐原则识别需重计算的位置；(3)运动向量引导缓存重映射维持帧间缓存一致性；(4)性能分析驱动的调度器在边缘与云间分配稀疏工作负载。

Result: 在多个视觉任务、动态视频基准和网络条件下的评估表明，FluxShard在指定精度预算下相比所有基线将延迟降低32.6-83.8%，能耗降低14.9-64.0%。

Conclusion: 通过将缓存管理粒度从场景级别细化到运动区域级别，FluxShard有效解决了非均匀运动场景下的粒度不匹配问题，显著提升了边缘云视频分析系统的效率和资源利用率。

Abstract: Caching and reusing intermediate features across consecutive frames is a common technique to reduce redundant computation and transmission for edge-cloud video analytics in mobile edge computation. Existing methods manage the cache in a fixed or globally shifted coordinate system, treating it as an indivisible whole. Under the non-uniform motion patterns of mobile scenes, this whole-scene granularity invalidates large portions of the cache even when most content has merely shifted spatially, wasting computation and bandwidth. The root cause is a granularity mismatch: the cache is managed per scene, yet motion varies per region. In this paper, we present FluxShard, a motion-aware edge-cloud video analytics system that uses codec-level block motion vectors (MVs) to manage feature cache reuse and recomputation at the granularity of individual motion regions. By re-indexing cached features along per-block MVs, FluxShard separates spatial displacement from content changes, recovering reusable content that whole-scene methods would otherwise discard. To ensure correct reuse under heterogeneous motion, the Receptive Field Alignment Principle (RFAP) identifies, from the input-level MV field alone, the positions that must be recomputed due to inconsistent spatial composition within receptive fields. To maintain cache coherence across frames, MV-guided cache remapping warps the entire feature cache to the current coordinate system each frame, sustaining a high reuse ratio over time. A profiling-driven dispatcher routes the remaining sparse workload between edge and cloud for lower latency. Evaluation across multiple vision tasks, dynamic video benchmarks, and network conditions shows that FluxShard reduces latency by 32.6-83.8% and energy by 14.9-64.0% over all baselines under the prescribed accuracy budget.

</details>


### [392] [Temporal Spectral Noise-Floor Adaptation for Error-Intolerant Trigger Integrity in IoT Mesh Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06338)
*Sergii Makovetskyi,Lars Thomsen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于物联网传感器节点的轻量级嵌入式算法，通过在固件中维护时变频谱噪声基底，实现了动态环境下的自主边缘事件触发，有效抑制了干扰并提高了可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态的户外环境中，非平稳的环境激励（如降雨、风和机械振动）会导致传感器节点产生大量误触发。现有的自适应阈值方法多用于管理通信负载，缺乏针对事件触发完整性的动态噪声基底维护机制，限制了物联网节点在无校准、无云侧推理场景下的自主部署能力。

Method: 该算法是一种轻量级的嵌入式方法。设备采集本地传感器数据，在固件中执行确定性的FFT频谱特征提取，并维护一个时变频谱噪声基底，以吸收非平稳的环境激励。该方法无需共享噪声模型或云端推理，依靠本地决策权威在连接间歇时保持节点响应性。

Result: 在部署于动态户外环境的单节点网状传感器中（使用雷达级接近传感器作为示例），实验结果表明该方法显著抑制了干扰引起的触发，减少了网状网络中的误报流量放大，同时具备有界的嵌入式执行性能，并能可靠地检测真实频谱特征。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了动态环境下自主节点的免校准部署，通过本地决策保证了触发完整性，有效解决了非平稳环境下的误触发问题，并缓解了集中式远程分析系统的安全风险。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a lightweight, embedded algorithm for autonomous edge event triggering in IoT sensor nodes suitable for operating in mesh networks. The device acquires local sensor data, performs deterministic FFT spectral feature extraction in firmware, and maintains a temporal spectral noise-floor baseline that absorbs non-stationary environmental excitations such as rain, wind, and mechanical vibration. While adaptive thresholds in IoT sensor nodes are often applied to manage communication load or stabilize long-term metrics, this work focuses on maintaining a time-evolving spectral noise floor to preserve event trigger reliability in dynamic environments. Our method targets trigger integrity under environmental non-stationary conditions, enabling calibration-free deployment of autonomous nodes; without shared noise models or cloud-side inference. Local decision authority preserves node responsiveness when connectivity is intermittent and mitigates security risks inherent in centralized remote-analysis systems. We validate the algorithm in a single node mesh sensor deployed in a dynamic outdoor environment using a radar-class proximity sensor as one example sensor modality. Results demonstrate substantial suppression of nuisance-induced triggers, reduced false-event traffic amplification in the mesh, bounded embedded execution, and reliable detection sensitivity to true spectral signatures.

</details>


### [393] [Edge Triggering in IoT Mesh Networks: A Comparative Monte Carlo Study of Seven Detection Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06358)
*Sergii Makovetskyi,Lars Thomsen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时序谱噪底自适应（TSNFA）方法，在物联网网状传感器网络中实现了100%检测率和零误报，证明了“谱段选择+时序持续滤波+自适应噪底跟踪”三重防护机制是实现资源受限环境下自主边缘触发的必要且充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网网状传感器网络中的实时事件检测面临三大核心挑战：时变噪声条件、边缘节点计算资源有限、以及缺乏中心化协调的自主运行需求。现有检测算法难以在这些约束条件下同时保证高检测率和低误报率。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛仿真方法，在200节点网状网络上进行24小时测试，对比TSNFA方法与6种替代检测算法。仿真包含真实噪声模型：60Hz电磁干扰、正弦漂移噪声功率（±6dB）和间歇性数字开关突发。TSNFA方法整合了三重相互锁定的防护机制：谱段选择、时序持续滤波和自适应噪底跟踪。

Result: TSNFA实现了100%检测率和零误报的完美表现。所有竞争算法都缺失至少一种防护机制并相应失效，误报率从0（Send-on-Delta算法，但也无检测）到13,387,930（宽带能量比算法）不等。结果表明三重防护组合是实现自主边缘触发的必要且充分条件。

Conclusion: 研究识别出三重防护机制（谱段选择、时序持续滤波、自适应噪底跟踪）的组合是在资源受限物联网部署中实现自主边缘触发的必要且充分条件，为边缘节点的事件检测算法设计提供了明确的理论指导。

Abstract: Real-time event detection in Internet of Things (IoT) mesh sensor networks presents significant challenges due to time-varying noise conditions, limited computational resources at edge nodes, and the need for autonomous operation without centralised coordination. This paper presents a comprehensive Monte Carlo simulation study comparing the Temporal Spectral Noise-Floor Adaptation (TSNFA) method against six alternative detection algorithms, evaluated across a 200-node mesh network over 24 hours with realistic noise models including 60 Hz electromagnetic interference (EMI), sinusoidally drifting noise power (+/- 6 dB), and intermittent digital switching bursts. TSNFA achieves 100% detection rate with zero false positives, uniquely combining three interlocking defences: spectral band selection, temporal persistence filtering, and adaptive noise-floor tracking. Every competing algorithm omits at least one of these three defences and fails correspondingly, with false-positive rates ranging from 0 (Send-on-Delta, which also detects nothing) to 13,387,930 (broadband energy ratio). These results identify the three-defence combination as necessary and sufficient for autonomous edge triggering in resource-constrained IoT deployments.

</details>


### [394] [Delay-Robust Deep Reinforcement Learning for Ranging-Free Channel Access under Mobility in Underwater Acoustic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06536)
*Huaisheng Ye,Xiaowen Ye,Liqun Fu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MobiU-MAC协议，一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）的水声网络MAC协议。通过引入无需测距且延迟鲁棒的CHILL-STER算法，有效解决了移动节点场景下的长传播延迟和拓扑动态变化问题，显著提升了网络吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水声网络（UWANs）中固有的长传播延迟会导致时空不确定性，严重限制了介质访问控制（MAC）协议的信道利用率。在自主水下航行器（AUV）等移动节点场景中，节点移动性进一步引入了动态传播延迟和时变的空间拓扑，使得现有的MAC协议面临更大挑战。

Method: 提出了MobiU-MAC协议，核心包含一种名为CHILL-STER的新型深度强化学习算法。该算法具备两大创新点：1）信用视界受限λ回报（CHILL-Return）机制，用于在异步延迟奖励下实现稳定学习，无需测距；2）时空经验回放（STER）机制，专门用于处理节点移动引起的拓扑变化。同时，文中从理论上证明了DRL在长传播延迟下无需测距即可达到与标准马尔可夫决策过程等效的最优策略学习。

Result: 性能评估表明，MobiU-MAC协议在无需承担测距开销的情况下，利用最大系统延迟边界，在吞吐量性能上优于现有的基于DRL的水声网络MAC协议，验证了所提理论和算法在复杂水下动态环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了在不依赖测距的前提下，利用深度强化学习解决水声网络长传播延迟问题的可行性。所提出的MobiU-MAC协议及CHILL-STER算法有效克服了节点移动性和延迟带来的挑战，为动态水下环境的MAC协议设计提供了新的理论依据和高效解决方案。

Abstract: Long propagation delays in underwater acoustic networks (UWANs) cause spatio-temporal uncertainty, constraining channel utilization in medium access control (MAC) protocols. Node mobility within autonomous underwater vehicle scenarios exacerbates these challenges by introducing dynamic propagation delays and varying spatial topologies. We present MobiU-MAC, a deep reinforcement learning (DRL)-based MAC protocol for mobile node access in UWANs that maximizes throughput via autonomous learning. MobiU-MAC incorporates CHILL-STER, a novel DRL algorithm optimized for UWANs that is both ranging-free and delay-robust. CHILL-STER employs a credit horizon-limited $λ$-return (CHILL-Return) mechanism to achieve stable learning under asynchronous delayed rewards, while the companion spatio-temporal experience replay (STER) mechanism addresses topological changes arising from node mobility. This work also demonstrates theoretically that DRL attains optimal policy learning equivalent to a standard Markov decision process under long propagation delays without requiring ranging. Performance evaluations indicate that MobiU-MAC outperforms existing DRL-based MAC protocols for UWANs by leveraging the maximum system delay boundary without ranging overhead, supporting the effectiveness of the proposed theory and algorithm in complex underwater dynamic environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [395] [Nitsum: Serving Tiered LLM Requests with Adaptive Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05467)
*Vikranth Srivatsa,Zijian He,Pu Guo,Dongming Li,Yiying Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Nitsum分布式LLM服务系统，将张量并行作为运行时控制变量，通过联合优化TP等级、prefill/decode GPU划分和请求调度，在多租户分层SLO场景下将SLO合规吞吐量相比SOTA提升高达5.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务日益呈现多租户特征，同一部署需在固定GPU预算下同时处理延迟敏感的交互请求和宽松的后台任务，形成分层SLO场景。由于工作负载组合、请求长度和负载强度随时间变化，现有系统主要优化请求级控制（如排队和批处理），而执行配置基本静态，限制了多层竞争下的适应性，难以最大化整体有效吞吐量。

Method: 1. 将张量并行（TP）视为一等运行时控制面而非静态部署选择；2. 联合优化TP等级、prefill/decode GPU划分和请求调度；3. 引入TP感知的权重复用和快速KV迁移技术，使频繁TP适配切实可行。

Result: 在真实trace和针对性微基准测试上，Nitsum将SLO合规吞吐量相比现有最优方法提升高达5.3倍。

Conclusion: Nitsum证明了将TP作为运行时动态控制面的有效性，通过TP感知权重复用和快速KV迁移克服了频繁TP适配的实践障碍，为多租户分层SLO场景下的LLM服务优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly multi-tenant: the same deployment must handle latency-critical interactive requests and more relaxed background workloads under a fixed GPU budget. This creates a tiered-SLO setting where maximizing overall goodput (requests that satisfy both TTFT and TPOT targets) is challenging because workload mix, request lengths, and load intensity vary over time. Existing systems mainly optimize request-level controls (e.g., queuing and batching) while keeping execution configuration largely static, which limits adaptation under multi-tier contention.
  We present Nitsum, a distributed LLM serving system that treats tensor parallelism (TP) as a first-class runtime control surface rather than a static deployment choice. Nitsum jointly optimizes TP level, prefill/decode GPU split, and request scheduling. To make frequent TP adaptation practical, Nitsum introduces TP-aware weight reuse and fast KV migration. Experiments on real traces and targeted microbenchmarks show that Nitsum improves SLO-compliant goodput over SoTA by up to 5.3 times.

</details>


### [396] [EdgeServing: Deadline-Aware Multi-DNN Serving at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05527)
*Jiahe Cao,Xiaomeng Li,Qiang Liu,Tao Han,Ning Zhang,Weisong Shi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了EdgeServing，一种面向边缘设备的多DNN模型服务系统。通过时间分片GPU共享与早期退出推理机制，并引入稳定性评分来量化调度决策的全局影响，在运行时协同选择模型、退出点和批次大小，从而显著降低SLO违规率和P95延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘计算的发展，在单个共享GPU上服务多个深度神经网络（DNN）模型变得普遍但充满挑战。现有的调度器依赖局部启发式方法，无法捕捉调度决策对所有并发队列尾延迟的全局影响，而GPU空间共享方法则牺牲了延迟的可预测性。

Method: 提出EdgeServing系统，采用时间分片GPU共享结合早期退出推理技术以保证高推理可预测性；引入稳定性评分量化每个候选调度决策对未来队列状态的影响；在运行时协同选择模型、退出点和批次大小，以最小化预测的系统级SLO影响。

Result: 在多个硬件平台上的实验结果表明，EdgeServing在SLO违规率和P95延迟方面均显著优于代表性基线方法。早期退出机制在严格的延迟约束下扩展了调度动作空间，从而实现了性能提升。

Conclusion: EdgeServing通过全局感知的调度策略和早期退出机制，有效解决了边缘设备上多DNN模型服务中的尾延迟预测难题，实现了更优的服务水平目标遵守率和延迟性能。

Abstract: As edge computing expands, serving multiple deep neural network (DNN) models on a single shared GPU has become a common yet challenging scenario, where each scheduling decision affects the tail latency of all concurrent queues. Existing schedulers rely on local heuristics and fail to capture this global impact, while GPU spatial-sharing approaches sacrifice latency predictability. In this paper, we propose EdgeServing, a deadline-aware multi-DNN serving system for edge devices. EdgeServing adopts time-division GPU sharing with early-exit inference for high inference predictability, and introduces a stability score to quantify how each candidate scheduling decision impacts the future queue status. At runtime, it cohesively selects the model, exit point, and batch size to minimize predicted system-wide SLO impact. Experimental results on multiple hardware platforms show that EdgeServing consistently outperforms representative baselines in both SLO violation ratio and P95 latency, enabled by early-exit mechanism, which expands the scheduling action space under tight latency constraints.

</details>


### [397] [A Scalable Digital Twin Framework for Energy Optimization in Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05581)
*Raphael Hendrigo de Souza Gonçalves,Wendel Marcos dos Santos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种可扩展的数据中心数字孪生框架，集成IoT数据采集、云计算和机器学习（LSTM）技术，实现了实时监控、预测和智能能源管理，实验证明能有效降低能耗并提升PUE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决数据中心能耗优化问题，实现可持续运营，需要一个能够实时监控、预测并智能管理能源的可扩展、具有成本效益的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个可扩展的数字孪生框架，集成IoT数据采集、云计算和机器学习技术；构建了小规模数据中心受控环境，监控功耗、温度和计算负载；采用长短期记忆网络（LSTM）模型预测能源需求并支持运营决策。

Result: 实验结果显示，该框架在能效方面取得了显著改进，包括电力消耗的减少和电源使用效率（PUE）的提升。

Conclusion: 尽管在受限环境中进行评估，该框架仍展现出作为可扩展且具有成本效益的可持续数据中心管理解决方案的巨大潜力。

Abstract: This study proposes a scalable Digital Twin framework for energy optimization in data centers.The framework integrates IoT-based data acquisition, cloud computing, and machine learning techniques to enable real-time monitoring, forecasting, and intelligent energy management. A controlled small-scale data center environment was developed to monitor variables such as power consumption, temperature, and computational workload. Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) models were employed to predict energy demand and support operational decision-making. Experimental results demonstrated improvements in energy efficiency, including reductions in power consumption and enhancements in Power Usage Effectiveness (PUE). Despite being evaluated in a constrained environment, the proposed framework demonstrates strong potential as a scalable and cost-effective solution for sustainable data center management.

</details>


### [398] [Irminsul: MLA-Native Position-Independent Caching for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05696)
*Bole Ma,Jan Eitzinger,Harald Köstler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对智能体LLM场景中前缀缓存失效问题，提出Irminsul系统，利用MLA架构特性实现内容寻址缓存，在多变位置场景下恢复最高约83%的提示词Token，节省63%的预填充能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM工作负载在每轮对话中会将相同Token置于不同位置，导致前缀缓存在首个分歧字节处失效，造成缓存命中率下降及TTFT严重飙升（10-16秒）。现有位置无关缓存系统需在全维度Key上校正RoPE，成本较高；而Multi-Head Latent Attention（MLA）将KV行分解为位置无关的c_KV和可闭式校正的k_r，为内容寻址缓存提供了天然适配。

Method: 提出Irminsul系统，扩展SGLang的基数缓存，在CDC分块的片段上采用内容哈希键控，并对k_r应用δ旋转规则。该方法利用MLA架构中c_KV的位置无关特性和k_r的可校正性，实现高效的内容寻址缓存，而非将其作为GQA的补救方案。

Result: 在三个原生MLA-MoE部署（DeepSeek-V2-Lite、Kimi Moonlight-16B-A3B、JoyAI-Flash）上评估，全部实现输出一致性。在智能体流量中，相比精确前缀匹配，Irminsul恢复了最高约83%的额外提示词Token，每次缓存命中节省63%的预填充能耗。

Conclusion: 内容寻址缓存应作为服务栈的一等原语而非前缀匹配的补救措施，MLA架构使其成为自然适配而非权宜之计。

Abstract: Agentic LLM workloads put bit-identical tokens at shifted positions every turn, voiding prefix caches at the first byte of divergence. Operators report cache-hit regressions ranging from moderate slowdowns to severe TTFT spikes of 10-16s on unchanged content. Prior position-independent caching systems correct RoPE on the full $d_K$-dimensional key, an architectural cost imposed by GQA, not by caching itself. Multi-Head Latent Attention, deployed at scale in DeepSeek-V2/V3/R1, Kimi-K2/Moonlight, GLM-5, and Mistral Large 3, factors each KV row into a position-free $c_{KV}$ and a 64-dim $k_r$ correctable in closed form; this structure motivates content-addressed caching as a natural fit rather than a GQA workaround. We present Irminsul, which extends SGLang's radix cache with content-hash keying over CDC-chunked segments and a $δ$-rotation rule for $k_r$. We evaluate three native MLA-MoE deployments - DeepSeek-V2-Lite (16B/2.4B), Kimi Moonlight-16B-A3B, and JoyAI-Flash (48B/3B) - with output-consistency on all three and recovery measured on the two endpoints; Irminsul recovers up to ~83% of prompt tokens above exact-prefix on agentic traffic while delivering 63% prefill energy savings per cache hit. We argue that content-addressed caching belongs in the serving stack as a first-class primitive, not a retrofit over prefix matching.

</details>


### [399] [LLM-Enhanced Deep Reinforcement Learning for Task Offloading in Collaborative Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05727)
*Hao Guo,Kaixiang Xv,Ziwu Ge,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了LeDRL框架，通过结合轻量级大语言模型（LLM）与增强自注意力的深度强化学习（DRL），解决了协同边缘计算中动态任务卸载的实时决策问题，在任务成功率上提升超过17%，并在Jetson边缘设备上验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同边缘计算需要动态任务卸载以维持低延迟和高可靠性，尤其在节点故障不可预测的情况下。现有方法存在局限：深度强化学习（DRL）样本效率低且易陷入局部最优，而大语言模型（LLM）难以满足实时决策需求。

Method: 提出LeDRL混合决策框架，将轻量级LLM与自注意力增强的DRL相结合。框架构建包含节点状态、任务语义和链路动态的结构化上下文提示，推导高层策略先验；通过基于自注意力的对齐模块进行上下文感知的策略优化；引入反思评估器从历史轨迹中提取语义反馈，指导未来提示生成。

Result: 在多种网络规模下，LeDRL在任务成功率、收敛速度和实时响应性方面均优于基线方法，任务成功率提升超过17%。同时，在基于Jetson的边缘设备原型系统CoEdgeSys上部署，验证了其在资源约束下的鲁棒性和可行性。

Conclusion: LeDRL成功融合了LLM的语义理解能力与DRL的实时决策优势，有效解决了协同边缘计算中的动态任务卸载问题，在性能和实用性上均表现出色，为边缘智能决策提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative edge computing uses edge nodes in different locations to execute tasks, necessitating dynamic task offloading decisions to maintain low latency and high reliability, especially under unpredictable node failures. Although deep reinforcement learning (DRL) and large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for task offloading, DRL often suffers from high sample inefficiency and local optima, whereas LLMs struggle with real-time decision-making. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{LeDRL}, a hybrid decision framework that couples a \emph{lightweight LLM} with self-attention-enhanced DRL for real-time task offloading. LeDRL constructs structured, context-aware prompts capturing node status, task semantics, and link dynamics to derive high-level strategy priors. These are selectively processed by a self-attention-based alignment module for context-aware policy optimization. A reflective evaluator distills semantic feedback from past trajectories to guide future prompts, enabling more informative and temporally generalizable LLM queries. Extensive experiments show that LeDRL outperforms baselines in task success rate, convergence speed, and real-time responsiveness across diverse network scales, achieving over 17\% improvement in success rate. Furthermore, we deploy LeDRL on Jetson-based edge devices using our prototype system \textit{CoEdgeSys}, demonstrating its robustness and feasibility under resource constraints. Our code is available at:https://github.com/GalleyG5/LeDRL.git.

</details>


### [400] [A Privacy-Preserving Machine Learning Framework for Edge Intelligence: An Empirical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05751)
*Quoc Lap Trieu,Bahman Javadi,Jim Basilakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘智能场景，提出了一种隐私保护机器学习（PPML）框架，对比评估了差分隐私（DP）、安全多方计算（SMC）和全同态加密（FHE）三种主流方法的性能与安全性。结果显示DP对系统效率影响最小但会降低精度，SMC性能受网络制约，FHE计算开销最大；安全性方面，DP能防御模型窃取，而SMC和FHE需额外控制输出才能实现类似防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘智能（EI）在智慧医疗、制造和关键基础设施等领域的广泛应用，如何在保障数据隐私的同时维持系统效率成为日益严峻的挑战。现有的隐私保护方案在边缘计算资源受限场景下的性能与安全性权衡尚缺乏系统的评估与指导。

Method: 本文提出了一种面向边缘智能应用的隐私保护机器学习（PPML）框架，包含四层系统架构及训练与推理算法。研究聚焦于三种主流方法——差分隐私（DP）、安全多方计算（SMC）和全同态加密（FHE），通过真实实现与大规模基于轨迹的仿真实验，评估了它们对模型精度、响应时间及能耗等关键性能指标的影响，并分析了各方法在抵抗黑盒模型窃取方面的安全性。

Result: 实验结果表明：DP的吞吐量与延迟接近明文基线，但精度随模型复杂度下降（AlexNet下降达35%，FordA上的LeNet低于18%）；SMC的性能主要由通信决定，提升链路带宽（250Mbps至500Mbps）可使AlexNet延迟降低约30%；FHE对模型结构与数值精度高度敏感，计算开销巨大，响应时间约为DP的1000倍。在安全性方面，DP通过降低攻击者数据效率来抵御黑盒模型窃取，而SMC和FHE虽能保护推理过程中的输入与参数，但需配合输出控制机制才能实现类似的抗提取能力。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了边缘智能场景下隐私保护、系统性能与资源效率之间的复杂权衡关系。不同隐私保护技术各有优劣：DP在效率方面优势明显但牺牲精度，SMC受网络条件制约，FHE计算开销极高。研究为边缘环境中隐私保护方案的选择与优化提供了重要依据，同时指出SMC和FHE需补充输出控制以增强抗模型提取能力。

Abstract: As Edge Intelligence (EI) becomes increasingly prevalent in domains such as smart healthcare, manufacturing, and critical infrastructure, ensuring data privacy while maintaining system efficiency is a growing challenge. This paper presents a new privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML) framework tailored for EI applications, including a four-layer system architecture and training and inference algorithms. We focus on three leading approaches: Differential Privacy (DP), Secure Multi-party Computation (SMC), and Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE), and assess their impact on key performance metrics, including model accuracy, response time, and energy consumption. Results from real implementation and extensive trace-based simulations of inference tasks show that DP generally preserves throughput and latency close to plaintext baselines, while accuracy drops with model complexity (up to 35 percent on AlexNet and under 18 percent on LeNet for FordA). SMC performance is driven by communication; network bandwidth and round complexity determine end-to-end latency. For AlexNet, increasing link capacity from 250 Mbps to 500 Mbps reduces latency by about 30 percent. FHE is highly sensitive to model structure and numerical precision bit width, with tighter parameters imposing substantial compute overhead; we observe roughly a 1000 times increase in response time compared to DP. Beyond efficiency, DP shifts the privacy-utility-extractability frontier by reducing the attacker's data efficiency in black-box model stealing, whereas SMC and FHE, while protecting inputs and parameters during inference, require complementary output controls to achieve similar resistance to extraction. These findings provide critical insights into the trade-offs between privacy, performance, and resource efficiency in edge computing scenarios.

</details>


### [401] [Relay Buffer Independent Communication over Pooled HBM for Efficient MoE Inference on Ascend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06055)
*Tianlun Hu,Tiancheng Hu,Shengsheng Litang,Sheng Wang,Xiaoming Bao,Yuxing Li,Wei Wang,Zhongzhe Hu,Lijun Li,Hongwei Sun,Jingbin Zhou\\*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向昇腾系统的无中继缓冲区MoE推理通信设计，通过直接向目标专家窗口写入和读取，消除了大部分中间缓冲区，从而降低了调度与合并延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型（MoE）推理中，跨设备的调度与合并是主要瓶颈。现有的通信路径以缓冲区为中心，存在路由驱动的布局转换、临时中继和输出恢复等额外开销，限制了推理性能。

Method: 基于全局池化高带宽内存和对称内存分配，设计了无中继缓冲区的通信机制。将调度与合并重组为直接向目标专家窗口写入和从远程专家窗口读取，仅保留轻量级的控制状态（计数、偏移和同步元数据），并为MoE推理的两个主要阶段分别实例化调度方案。

Result: 在昇腾平台的MoE负载实验中，预填充和解码阶段的调度与合并延迟均得到降低。服务级别上，首字元时间（TTFT）有所改善，每输出字元时间（TPOT）保持竞争力，并在实际延迟约束下扩大了可行调度空间。

Conclusion: 在具有全局可寻址设备内存的平台上，减少专家执行周围的中间缓冲和输出恢复是加速MoE推理的有效途径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference requires large-scale token exchange across devices, making dispatch and combine major bottlenecks in both prefill and decode. Beyond network transfer, routing-driven layout transformation, temporary relay, and output restoration can add substantial overhead. Existing MoE communication paths are often buffer-centric, using explicit inter-process relay and reordering buffers around collective transfer. This report presents a relay-buffer-free communication design for MoE inference acceleration on Ascend systems. The design reorganizes dispatch and combine around direct placement into destination expert windows and direct reading from remote expert windows. Built on globally pooled high-bandwidth memory and symmetric-memory allocation, it removes most intermediate relay and reordering buffers while retaining only lightweight control state, including counts, offsets, and synchronization metadata. We instantiate the design as two schedules for the main phases of MoE inference: a prefill schedule with richer planning state for throughput-oriented execution, and a compact decode schedule for latency-sensitive execution. Experiments on Ascend-based MoE workloads show reduced dispatch and combine latency in both settings. At the serving level, the implementation improves time to first token (TTFT), preserves competitive time per output token (TPOT), and enlarges the feasible scheduling space under practical latency constraints. These results indicate that, on platforms with globally addressable device memory, reducing intermediate buffering and output restoration around expert execution is an effective direction for accelerating MoE inference.

</details>


### [402] [FalconGEMM: Surpassing Hardware Peaks with Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06057)
*Honglin Zhu,Jiaping Cao,Jiang Shao,Siyuan Feng,Qian Qiu,Peng Chen,Xu Zhang,Yixian Zhou,Man Lung Yiu,Guang Ji,Minwen Deng,Wenxi Zhu,Jintao Meng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了FalconGEMM框架，通过自动化部署、优化和选择低复杂度矩阵乘法算法（LCMAs），在多种硬件平台上实现了超越峰值性能，相比主流GEMM库性能提升7.59%-17.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低复杂度矩阵乘法算法（LCMAs）理论上能提升深度学习特别是大语言模型训练和推理的性能，但在异构硬件环境下的实际部署面临可移植性差、优化困难和策略选择等挑战，现有GEMM库难以充分发挥LCMAs的潜力。

Method: 提出FalconGEMM跨平台框架，包含三个核心模块：（1）部署模块：通过代码生成实现跨硬件和输入配置的可移植执行；（2）执行模块：采用组并行优化策略，最大化片上数据复用，充分利用并行资源并降低带宽开销；（3）决策模块：基于轻量级分析性能模型，根据矩阵形状和硬件特性选择最优算法策略。

Result: 在GPU（H20、A100）和CPU（ARM、x86）架构上针对LLM工作负载进行广泛评估，覆盖多种数据类型。FalconGEMM实现了超越峰值性能，相比cuBLAS、CUTLASS、Intel MKL等GEMM库性能提升7.59%-17.85%，相比AlphaTensor等LCMA竞争方案性能提升12.41%-55.61%。

Conclusion: FalconGEMM成功将低复杂度矩阵乘法算法的理论优势转化为实际生产部署能力，实现了跨现代异构硬件环境的自动化优化，证明了LCMAs在实际深度学习应用中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Peak breaking Matrix Multiplication is a promising technique to improve the performance of DL, especially in LLM training and inference. We present FalconGEMM, a cross-platform framework that automates the deployment, optimization, and selection of Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication Algorithms (LCMAs) across diverse hardware. There are three key innovations: (1) a Deployment Module that enables portable execution across various hardware and input configurations through code generation; (2) an Execution Module with Group-Parallel Optimizations that maximizes on-chip data reuse, utilizes parallel resources, and reduces bandwidth overhead; and (3) a Decision Module featuring a lightweight analytical performance model to select the optimal strategy based on matrix shapes and hardware profiles. Extensive evaluation is conducted on LLM workloads across GPU (H20, A100) and CPU (ARM, x86) architectures with multiple data types. FalconGEMM succeeds in delivering peak breaking performance and outperforms GEMM libraries (e.g., cuBLAS, CUTLASS, Intel MKL, etc) by 7.59%-17.85% and LCMA competitors like AlphaTensor by 12.41%-55.61%. Our framework makes the theoretical promise of LCMAs practical for production deployment across the heterogeneous landscape of modern hardware.

</details>


### [403] [Tackling the Data-Parallel Load Balancing Bottleneck in LLM Serving: Practical Online Routing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06113)
*Tianci Bu,Yuan Lyu,Zixi Chen,Chendong Song,Hong Liang,Tsepten Gurung,Yuwei Fan,Yinyu Ye,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了BalanceRoute系列在线路由算法，用于解决大规模LLM服务中数据并行（DP）负载不平衡这一关键瓶颈问题，在144-NPU集群上部署实验表明，该算法显著降低了平均DP不平衡度并提升了端到端服务吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模LLM服务中，数据并行负载平衡已成为首要瓶颈。当模型通过张量并行（TP）或专家并行（EP）分片到设备上并在多个DP worker间复制时，每个解码步骤都以同步屏障结束，其延迟由负载最重的worker决定。即使是轻微的持续不平衡，经过多个步骤的累积也会造成大量计算浪费。该问题因LLM解码的特殊性而具有挑战性：分配具有粘性（迁移KV缓存成本高）、每个请求的负载随时间增长、请求到达具有非平稳性，且路由器必须在亚100毫秒的解码预算内对数百个等待请求和数十个worker做出决策。

Method: 提出了BalanceRoute系列实用在线路由算法：1）BR-0：无需预测基础设施，采用分段线性F-score来捕捉填充安全余量的接纳决策与溢出到边界的不对称性，通过两阶段分解将每步成本控制在毫秒级调度兼容范围内；2）BR-H：将BR-0泛化，引入短的常量前瞻H和轻量级终止分类器接口，将F-score扩展为视界折扣形式。两个算法都针对毫秒级调度约束设计，能在极短时间内处理大规模请求分配决策。

Result: 在144-NPU集群上部署BalanceRoute，并与vLLM基线进行对比评估，使用私有生产轨迹和公共Azure-2024轨迹两种工作负载进行测试。实验结果表明，在两种工作负载下，BalanceRoute均显著降低了平均DP不平衡度并提升了端到端服务吞吐量。

Conclusion: BalanceRoute系列算法成功解决了大规模LLM服务中数据并行负载不平衡的核心问题，通过创新的F-score评分机制和高效的在线决策策略，在满足毫秒级调度约束的前提下实现了更好的负载均衡效果，为大规模LLM服务的性能优化提供了实用有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Data-parallel (DP) load balancing has emerged as a first-order bottleneck in large-scale LLM serving. When a model is sharded across devices via tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP) and replicated across many DP workers, every decode step ends in a synchronization barrier whose latency is set by the most heavily loaded worker; even modest persistent imbalance across DP workers compounds, step after step, into a substantial fraction of wasted compute. The problem is hard for reasons specific to LLM decoding: assignments are sticky (migrating KV caches has a high cost), per-request loads grow over time, arrivals are non-stationary, and the router must decide within a sub-100\,ms decode budget over hundreds of waiting requests and tens of workers. We present \textbf{BalanceRoute}, a family of practical online routing algorithms that target this bottleneck. The first, \textbf{BR-0}, requires no prediction infrastructure and uses a piecewise-linear F-score that captures the sharp asymmetry between admissions that fill safe margin and those that overflow into the envelope; a two-stage decomposition keeps per-step cost compatible with millisecond-scale scheduling. The second, \textbf{BR-H}, generalizes BR-0 with a short, constant lookahead $H$ and a lightweight termination-classifier interface, extending the F-score to a horizon-discounted form. We deploy BalanceRoute on a 144-NPU cluster and evaluate against vLLM baselines on both a proprietary production trace and the public Azure-2024 trace. Across both workloads, BalanceRoute substantially reduces average DP imbalance and improves end-to-end serving throughput.

</details>


### [404] [TACO: A Toolsuite for the Verification of Threshold Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06118)
*Paul Eichler,Tom Baumeister,Mouhammad Sakr,Mahboubeh Kalateh Dowlati,Marcus Völp,Swen Jacobs*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TACO工具套件，用于开发和自动验证容错及基于阈值的分布式算法，实现了三种可判定片段的模型检测方法和两种半判定过程，并提供了模块化、可扩展的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发和自动验证容错及基于阈值的分布式算法面临挑战，缺乏统一的工具支持，因此需要构建一个模块化、可扩展且文档完善的框架来支持相关算法和工具的开发与验证。

Method: 实现了三种针对阈值自动机不同可判定片段的模型检测方法，以及两种超越这些可判定片段的半判定过程；设计了模块化、可扩展且文档完善的框架架构。

Result: 完成了TACO工具套件的设计与实现，通过实验评估了所实现算法的性能，验证了其在开发和验证容错分布式算法方面的有效性。

Conclusion: TACO为基于阈值的分布式算法提供了一个功能完善、性能良好的开发和验证平台，其模块化设计便于后续扩展和新算法的集成。

Abstract: We present TACO, a toolsuite for the development and automatic verification of fault-tolerant and threshold-based distributed algorithms. Our toolsuite implements three approaches for model checking threshold automata in different decidable fragments known from the literature and two semi-decision procedures going beyond these decidable fragments. Moreover, TACO is a modular, extensible, and well-documented framework for developing algorithms and tools for threshold automata. We present important features, give an overview of the implemented algorithms, and evaluate their performance experimentally.

</details>


### [405] [ResiHP: Taming LLM Training Failures with Dynamic Hybrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06374)
*Tenghui Ma,Jihu Guo,Wei Gao,Sitian Lu,Zhisheng Ye,Hanjing Wang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ResiHP系统，通过工作负载感知的故障检测器和动态调度器，解决了混合并行LLM训练中的故障检测与适应问题，将训练吞吐量提升了1.04-4.39倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有容错系统忽视了数据集序列长度可变性和混合并行下的设备性能偏差，导致迭代时间波动引发虚假故障检测，以及通过单独适配缓解故障造成不必要的开销和低效的容错训练。

Method: 设计了ResiHP系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) Detector——采用工作负载感知的执行时间预测器，将故障与迭代时间波动解耦，实现在线轻量级检测；(2) Scheduler——动态调整并行组大小、模型分区和工作负载调度策略以提高故障下的训练效率。

Result: 在256-GPU集群上的实验表明，与最先进的容错训练系统相比，ResiHP在各种故障场景下将训练吞吐量提升了1.04-4.39倍。

Conclusion: ResiHP通过准确识别故障和细粒度的并行适应机制，有效提升了混合并行LLM训练的容错能力和整体效率。

Abstract: Hybrid parallelism underpins large-scale LLM training across tens of thousands of GPUs. At such scale, hardware failures on individual devices lead to performance skew across devices, diminishing overall training efficiency. Existing resilient systems overlook sequence length variability in datasets and device performance skew under hybrid parallelism. As a result, (1) iteration time fluctuations induced by sequence length variability can trigger spurious fail-slow detections, and (2) failures are mitigated through individual adaptations in hybrid parallelism, leading to unnecessary detection overhead and inefficient resilient training. To respond, this paper presents ResiHP, a resilient system that enables robust failure detection and fine-grained adaptation for hybrid parallel training. First, we develop a Detector to accurately identify failures. In particular, it employs a workload-aware execution time predictor that disentangles failures from iteration time fluctuations while remaining lightweight for online detection. Second, we design a Scheduler that dynamically adapts parallelism group sizes, model partitioning, and workload scheduling policies to improve training efficiency under failures. Experiments show that ResiHP improves training throughput by 1.04-4.39$\times$ compared with state-of-the-art resilient training systems under diverse failure scenarios in a 256-GPU cluster.

</details>


### [406] [ADELIA: Automatic Differentiation for Efficient Laplace Inference Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06392)
*Afif Boudaoud,Lisa Gaedke-Merzhäuser,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Vincent Maillou,Alexandru Calotoiu,Marcin Copik,Håvard Rue,Mathieu Luisier,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ADELIA是首个支持自动微分(AD)的INLA实现，利用结构感知的多GPU反向传播，在超参数优化中比传统有限差分方法实现4.2-7.9倍加速，并节省5-8倍能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的INLA实现使用中心有限差分(FD)近似导数，需要2d+1次评估（d为超参数数量），计算量和能耗随d线性增长，在固定资源预算下限制了解决方案的时间；而反向模式自动微分(AD)可以独立于d计算精确梯度，但如何将其高效应用于INLA的结构化稀疏核是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出ADELIA，首个AD使能的INLA实现，采用结构感知的多GPU反向传播策略，充分利用模型稀疏性，实现了对INLA结构化稀疏核的高效自动微分计算。

Result: 在十个基准模型（包括真实空气污染监测）上评估，实现了每个梯度4.2-7.9倍的加速；在具有高达190万潜在变量的生产级模型上实现可靠收敛（FD在此规模下表现不佳）；即使将FD扩展到16-32 GPU以匹配ADELIA的挂钟时间，FD仍消耗5-8倍更多能量。

Conclusion: ADELIA成功解决了INLA中自动微分的高效实现问题，在计算速度和能效方面显著优于传统有限差分方法，为大规模时空贝叶斯推断提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal Bayesian inference drives environmental and health sciences using latent Gaussian models. Integrated Nested Laplace Approximations (INLA) enable inference for these models at HPC scale but rely on derivative-based optimization over $d$ hyperparameters. State-of-the-art INLA implementations approximate derivatives via central finite differences (FD), requiring $2d{+}1$ evaluations. These evaluations are embarrassingly parallel, but total work and energy grow with $d$, limiting time-to-solution under fixed budgets. Reverse-mode automatic differentiation (AD) computes exact gradients independently of $d$, but its efficient application to INLA's structured-sparse kernels is an open challenge. We present ADELIA, the first AD-enabled INLA implementation with a structure-exploiting multi-GPU backward pass leveraging model sparsity. We evaluate ADELIA on ten benchmark models, including real-world air-pollution monitoring. We achieve $4.2$--$7.9\times$ per-gradient speedups and reliable convergence on production-scale models with up to 1.9M latent variables, where FD struggles. Even when scaled to 16--32 GPUs to match ADELIA's wall-clock time, FD consumes $5$--$8\times$ more energy.

</details>


### [407] [CCL-Bench 1.0: A Trace-Based Benchmark for LLM Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06544)
*Eric Ding,Byungsoo Oh,Bhaskar Kataria,Kaiwen Guo,Jelena Gvero,Abhishek Vijaya Kumar,Arjun Devraj,Lindsey Bowen,Atharv Sonwane,Emaad Manzoor,Rachee Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了CCL-Bench，一种基于轨迹的大语言模型基础设施基准测试框架，通过记录可复用的执行轨迹、工作负载卡片和启动脚本，揭示了传统基准测试无法发现的细粒度性能洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型基础设施评估基准仅发布少量端到端性能数据，无法解释为何某种配置优于另一种配置。由于评估涉及硬件加速器、互连带宽、软件框架、并行策略和通信库等复杂配置空间，缺乏细粒度分析数据使得性能优化决策缺乏依据。

Method: 提出CCL-Bench，一种基于轨迹的基准测试框架。每个数据点包含：执行轨迹（execution trace）、YAML格式的工作负载卡片（workload card）以及启动脚本。开发了社区可扩展的工具包，基于这些证据计算细粒度的计算、内存和通信效率指标。该框架支持社区贡献和扩展。

Result: 利用CCL-Bench发现了三个传统基准测试无法支持的发现：（i）更高的计算-通信重叠可能伴随更长的训练步时间，揭示了低效的并行化选择；（ii）对于中小规模工作负载，TPU互连带宽翻倍带来的端到端步时间提升显著高于GPU互连带宽翻倍；（iii）在相同硬件上，不同训练框架的最优配置之间性能差异可达3倍。

Conclusion: CCL-Bench通过提供可复用的细粒度性能证据，弥补了现有基准测试的不足，使研究人员能够深入理解配置选择对性能的影响机制，从而做出更明智的硬件和软件配置决策。

Abstract: Evaluative claims about LLM infrastructure -- ``workload X is fastest on hardware Y with software Z'' -- depend on a complex configuration space spanning hardware accelerators, interconnect bandwidth, software frameworks, parallelism plans, and communication libraries. Current infrastructure evaluation benchmarks publish a small set of end-to-end numbers that do not explain why one configuration outperforms another. We present CCL-Bench, a trace-based benchmark that addresses the limitations of existing benchmarks by recording reusable evidence for every ML workload. Each contributed data point in CCL-Bench packages an execution trace, a YAML workload card, and the launch scripts. We have developed a community-extensible toolkit to compute fine-grained compute, memory, and communication efficiency metrics from this evidence. Using CCL-Bench, we surface three claims that summary-statistic benchmarks cannot support: (i) higher compute-communication overlap can coincide with longer training step time and reveal inefficient parallelization choices, (ii) doubling TPU interconnect bandwidth yields a much higher end-to-end improvement in step time than doubling GPU interconnect bandwidth on small and medium workloads, and (iii) the best-tuned configuration on one training framework can run up to 3$\times$ slower than the best-tuned configuration on a peer framework on identical hardware.

</details>


### [408] [ROSE: Rollout On Serving GPUs via Cooperative Elasticity for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06534)
*Wei Gao,Yuheng Zhao,Dilxat Muhtar,Dakai An,Xuchun Shang,Tianyuan Wu,Lunxi Cao,Shaopan Xiong,Weixun Wang,Ju Huang,Teng Ma,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Bo Zheng,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ROSE系统，通过协同弹性机制安全地复用服务集群中的闲置GPU资源来加速智能体强化学习训练，在保证服务SLO的前提下实现了1.20-3.31倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）对提升LLM的多步推理和工具使用能力至关重要，但其训练效率受限于长尾rollout和多轮环境交互。静态GPU资源配置面临两难困境：过度配置导致资源浪费，配置不足则引发竞争并降低训练速度。作者观察到生产服务集群通常存在大量GPU计算和内存空闲，这为资源复用提供了机会。

Method: 提出ROSE系统，包含三个核心组件：
（1）SLO安全的协同服务执行器：通过高效的GPU内存和计算共享提升rollout吞吐量，同时保证服务SLO；
（2）跨集群权重传输引擎：利用权重分片和稀疏性实现跨集群快速权重同步；
（3）弹性rollout调度器：动态配置协同资源容量，并在专用rollout GPU和机会性服务GPU之间调度轨迹rollout。

Result: 在多种模型规模和集群规模上的实验表明，与最先进的固定资源和弹性基线相比，ROSE将平均端到端吞吐量提升了1.20-3.31倍。

Conclusion: 协同弹性是解决智能体RL训练资源效率瓶颈的有效途径，ROSE证明了在保护服务SLO的前提下安全复用服务集群闲置资源加速训练的可行性，为LLM后训练系统的资源管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key driver for improving the multi-step reasoning and tool-use capabilities of LLMs. However, its efficiency is bottlenecked by long-tail rollouts with multi-turn environment interactions, making static GPU provisioning a poor fit: overprovisioning wastes GPUs on stragglers, while underprovisioning increases contention and slows training.
  We observe that production serving clusters routinely leave substantial GPU compute and memory headroom. Based on this observation, we argue for cooperative elasticity: opportunistically repurposing underutilized serving GPUs to execute rollouts. Realizing cooperative elasticity is non-trivial because it must preserve serving Service Level Objectives (SLOs) under bursty traffic and minimize communication overhead. To address these challenges, we present ROSE, a cooperative, resource-elastic post-training system that safely harvests idle compute and memory on serving GPUs to accelerate agentic RL rollouts. ROSE consists of three components: (1) an SLO-safe co-serving executor that improves rollout throughput while preserving serving SLOs through efficient GPU memory and compute sharing; (2) a cross-cluster weight transfer engine that leverages weight shards and sparsity for fast weight synchronization across clusters; and (3) an elastic rollout scheduler that dynamically provisions cooperative capacity and routes trajectory rollouts across dedicated rollout GPUs and opportunistic serving GPUs. Experiments across multiple model sizes and cluster scales show that ROSE improves average end-to-end throughput by 1.20-3.31 x compared with state-of-the-art resource-fixed and elastic baselines.

</details>
